# CLAUDE.md

This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.

## What this is

A dental-clinic management system for "DGT One Dental Center - Bulacan" (a few legacy seed/comment strings still say the old name "Dr. Elaine Silva Dental Clinic"; `dentist1`'s seeded display name is also "Dr. Elaine Silva" — that is a person, not the clinic): PHP 8.2 (PDO) · MySQL/MariaDB (`dental_clinic_system`) · vanilla JavaScript (Fetch API). Runs under XAMPP on Windows at `http://localhost/dgt_dental_clinic/`. There is no package manager or automated test suite — edit a `.php`/`.js` file and reload the browser.

### Styling: the DGT design system (in progress)

There is a project skill at **`.claude/skills/taste-skill/`** (adapted from
`github.com/Leonxlnx/taste-skill`, MIT). It is an anti-slop *frontend* pass —
**public pages only** (`index.php`; later the auth pages), **never** the app
shell or any `modules/**` screen. `SKILL.md` is the DGT adapter (scope, stack
overrides — vanilla JS not React, Tailwind v3 CDN not v4, inline SVG icons,
no gradients, DGT palette, `DESIGN.md`/`CLAUDE.md`/pinned memories win on every
conflict); `reference.md` is the vendored upstream rule set. Invoke it when
building or reworking a public page's look.

`DESIGN.md` carries three sections distilled from the skills in
`github.com/nextlevelbuilder/ui-ux-pro-max-skill` (MIT):
**`## Design tokens & component state`** (the three token layers as this project
already has them — `tailwind.php` scale → `roles:` → `components:` — the "no raw
hex" rule + its two exceptions, and the `disabled > loading > active > focus >
hover` state precedence), **`## Accessibility`** (keyboard/focus, `openModal()`
focus-trap, SR labels, `aria-invalid`/`aria-describedby` on `.field-err`,
reduced-motion), and **`## Interaction & performance`** (pointer/disabled state,
CLS / layout stability, motion timing, `nav.js` focus handling, `aria-sort` on
`.sortable`, chart `aria-label`). `DESIGN.md`'s **`## Icons`** section (inline-SVG conventions — `viewBox 24`,
`currentColor`, round caps, one stroke width, `aria-hidden` vs `aria-label`) is
the one scrap kept from that repo's `design` skill, which is otherwise an
AI-image-generation umbrella (logo / CIP mockup / banner / social-photo / pitch-
deck / AI-icon generation, needs a paid Gemini key) with nothing else applicable.
The source skills — `ui-styling` (shadcn/React build-step toolchain),
`ui-ux-pro-max` (Python design-search DB), `design-system` (CSS-var token
generator + slide decks), `design` (AI asset generation) — were **not**
vendored: they don't fit this PHP + vanilla-JS + Tailwind-v3-CDN, no-build
stack, and their native-mobile / dark-mode / CSS-variable / AI-generation
assumptions don't apply. Only the stack-agnostic principles were kept, in
`DESIGN.md`.

`DESIGN.md`'s **`## Visual hierarchy & craft`** section is the same treatment
applied to Dammyjay93's `interface-design` skill
(`github.com/Dammyjay93/interface-design`, MIT) — a craft / hierarchy / tokens /
motion guide scoped to **app screens** (dashboards, tables, forms, settings),
the inverse of `taste-skill`'s public-page scope. It was **not** vendored; its
portable parts (one-focal-point, weight+colour over size, type-scale-as-ratio,
density-as-a-decision, 60/30/10, the swap / squint / signature / token checks)
went into that section plus additions to `## Elevation` (whisper-quiet ladder,
concentric radius) and `## Interaction & performance` → Motion (durations,
custom ease-out, never `scale(0)`). Read those when building or reworking a
`modules/**` screen.

The user is re-skinning the app to its **clinic-logo colours** — a project `DESIGN.md`
at the repo root is the spec (its format follows `voltagent/awesome-design-md`).
Palette, derived from `assets/img/dgt_logo.jpg`: **DGT Blue `#006CC0`** (primary
accent, links, focus, single-series charts) whose darkest step **`dgt-blue-900`
`#102F51`** is the navy for **every heading and the sidebar**; **DGT Teal `#0086A0`**
(`dgt-teal-600` on buttons) for the primary CTA + secondary accents + the patient
hero banner; **DGT Ink** — a cool metallic-grey scale (`dgt-ink-50` canvas → white
cards → `dgt-ink-200` hairline borders → `dgt-ink-700` body → `dgt-ink-500` labels).
Tokens are nested under `dgt` in `includes/tailwind.php` (`bg-dgt-blue-500`,
`text-dgt-ink-700`, …), **additive** — the legacy `navy` / `steel` / `accent` /
`teal` tokens stay until each page is converted, then retire. Feel: calm, light,
white canvas, 12px cards, hairline borders, `shadow-sm` only; saturated colour is
for small marks (tile accent edges, chart lines, badges) — never large gradient
blocks. **DGT Blue and DGT Teal must never be two adjacent chart categories** (they
fail the colour-blind separation floor; validated with the `dataviz` skill).

Rollout is group-by-group like the Tailwind migration below:

- **Done — `modules/admin/dashboard.php` (first page):** `dgt-*` tokens; navy
  headings; entity-stable chart colours (a
  service keeps its hue as data changes); every donut keeps
  its ring (a lone 100% category still renders as a full ring — the user wants
  the circle kept — only a genuinely empty dataset drops to a text line, via the
  `distro()` helper).
  **Stripped down at the user's request (2026-09) — the advisor wanted it
  focused on daily clinic transactions, not user/account or static counts.**
  Removed: the whole **User Analytics** card (Account-Distribution-by-Role
  donut, per-role count tiles, *and* the Account-Growth line — `accounts` /
  `account_growth` gone from the response); the Total-Expenses & Net-Profit
  tiles (finance is **gross revenue only** — no `expenses` / `net_profit`); the
  Service-Usage bar chart (`top_services` gone); and every **Services Analytics**
  tile — Total Services (`service_total`), Most Popular (`most_popular`) and Avg
  Service Cost (`service_avg_cost`) are all gone. **Refactored again on panel
  feedback (2026-09) — "focus purely on gross revenue accumulation + revenue by
  service":** the **Monthly Revenue Trend line** and the **Payment Methods
  donut** were removed (`revenue_by_month` / `payment_methods` gone), and the
  generic period `<select>` was replaced by three fixed operational-period
  tiles. **What's left:** **Revenue Summary** (renamed from "Revenue Analytics"
  2026-09) = three gross-revenue-to-date
  tiles — **Today's Revenue** (`today`), **Weekly Revenue** (`week`, Monday of
  the current week onward) and **This Month's Revenue** (`month`, 1st of the
  month onward), each recognised on `COALESCE(DATE(paid_at), payment_date)` with
  windows computed on the PHP clock; **Services Analytics** = the all-time
  Revenue-by-Service donut only (no period control — all-time by design, so its
  centre total legitimately exceeds the month tile). The analytics response is
  now just `today`, `week`, `month`, `revenue_by_service`.
  `assets/js/charts.js` `PALETTE` is now the DGT categorical set (DGT blue slot 1
  + a CVD-validated hue order) and its chrome hexes are DGT ink — this is
  app-wide, so any other consumer of `charts.js` picks it up too.
  **A `Clinic Operations` section was added above Revenue Summary (2026-09)** —
  seven metric cards (Today's Schedule / Ongoing Patients / Pending
  Schedule / Rescheduled / Doctors on Duty / Assistant Dentists on Duty / Total Active
  Accounts) polled from `api/dashboard.php?action=operational` on a 30 s
  auto-refresh toggle; **every card is clickable** → a drill-down modal (the four
  appointment cards → an appointment table whose rows `data-view` → the shared
  `appointmentModal()`; the three staff cards → a people table).
  **Every panel on the dashboard is interactive** — the three Revenue
  Summary cards and the Revenue-by-Service donut card are clickable too
  (transaction list / per-service breakdown; `revenue_list` / `service_revenue_list`).
  Full spec in the *Core workflow* section (step 5) and the reports paragraph.
  **The whole page was relaid out to a compact elevated "bento" layout (2026-09):**
  no more three big container cards. `<h1>` is **"Dashboard Overview"** with the
  Auto-refresh toggle beside it. The seven operational cards sit in one
  `#opsCards` grid (`grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4`); **Today's Schedule** is the
  navy hero and spans two columns (`featured` + `wide`). Below them the lower row
  is **two equal-height cards** (`lg:grid-cols-2 lg:items-stretch`) — **Revenue
  Summary** (the three gross-revenue tiles, `#revenueCards` is a
  `grid-cols-2 auto-rows-fr` filling its card, This Month's Revenue the double-
  wide navy hero) on the left and the **Revenue by Service** donut card
  (`distro()` → `donutChart` `size 220`, centred in a `flex-1` body) on the
  right. **The whole page content is a `min-h-[calc(100vh-150px)]` flex column
  and that lower two-column row is `flex-1`**, so the two cards stretch down to
  fill the viewport rather than leaving dead canvas at the bottom. The advisor's
  rule is that the dashboard shows **only** these panels — do **not** add new
  sections to fill space; expand what's there.
  (An earlier pass had a read-only Upcoming Appointments feed in the right
  column — `api/dashboard.php?action=upcoming`, still live but no longer
  consumed — the user replaced it with the donut, then had the section renamed
  from "Revenue Analytics" to "Revenue Summary" and its "Gross · fully-paid"
  caption dropped.)
  **A 2026-09 visual restyle / regroup pass (header band, group dividers, accent
  caps, fraction bars, ranked service list, an a11y hardening layer) was reverted
  at the user's request — they did not like the look.** The shared, cross-cutting
  part of that pass was kept: `app.js` `openModal()` moves focus into the dialog,
  adds a Tab trap + `aria-labelledby` from the `<h3>`, and `closeModal()` returns
  focus to the trigger; `toast()` sets `role="status"`/`alert` and `#toastRoot`
  (in `layout.php`) is an `aria-live` region.
  **Card style is
  dashboard-only** and deviates from `DESIGN.md`'s elevation spec: `rounded-2xl`,
  `shadow-md` → `hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-xl`, icon chip top-left,
  bold value, no accent bar. **The white metric cards also have a Uiverse-style
  "hover flood" (added 2026-09 at the user's request):** a `<style>` block at the
  top of `$PAGE_CONTENT` defines `.dash-card` — a circle parked outside the
  top-right corner (`::before`, hidden at rest) that `scale(72)`s on
  `:hover`/`:focus-visible` to flood the card in **that card's own icon accent**
  (passed inline as `--flood`, deepened via `color-mix(… 84%, #0a1a2e)` so white
  text stays readable); the value/label/chevron (`.dash-ink`), sub-line
  (`.dash-ink-soft`) and icon chip (`.dash-chip`) / pill (`.dash-pill`)
  cross-fade to white. Adapted from `uiverse.io/SouravBandyopadhyay/giant-squid-24`
  — **solid colour, not the original gradient** (no-gradient rule kept). Featured
  navy hero cards are excluded; `prefers-reduced-motion` drops the transitions.
  (A hover-scale + press-tilt from `uiverse.io/SteveBloX/dangerous-warthog-85`
  was briefly added on top of this and then removed at the user's request.)
  **No decorative pills** — the user had every
  micro-badge removed (the `+/-%` prior-period deltas on the revenue/schedule
  tiles, the `Live` / `Needs approval` status pills); the one exception, a
  single `Online now` teal pill on **Total Active Accounts**, went away with
  that whole card — see below. `today_prev` / `week_prev` / `month_prev`
  still come back from the APIs but nothing renders them now. **Two "hero"
  cards** — Today's Schedule + This
  Month's Revenue — are **solid `dgt-blue-900` fill** (white text, `col-span-2`,
  faint watermark icon); **no gradients** (the user kept that rule). The
  operational **Pending Requests** card is now labelled **Pending Schedule**.
  One shared JS `tile()` renders every card; a unified `data-drill` + `drill()`
  dispatch routes appointment / staff / revenue / service drill-downs. See `DESIGN.md`.
- **In progress — app shell:** `includes/sidebar.php` re-skinned — `dgt-blue-800`
  body, `dgt-teal-600` brand cap, `dgt-blue-500` active nav item, white-alpha
  hover/borders. Each nav row's icon sits in a fixed `h-7 w-7` box so labels
  align. The nav glyphs are **inline Phosphor SVGs** (MIT), defined as
  `[outline, fill]` path pairs in the `$NAV_ICON` map in `sidebar.php` — keys
  `dashboard` (`squares-four`, the 2x2 dashboard grid; was `house` until 2026-10-12) / `schedule` (`calendar-check`) / `appointments`
  (`list-checks`) / `schedules` (`calendar-plus`) / `services` (`tooth`) /
  `users` (`users`) / `reports` (`chart-line`) / `logs`
  (`clock-counter-clockwise`) / `settings` (`gear`) / `transactions`
  (`cash-register`) / `calendar` (`calendar-dots`) / `records` (`file-text`) /
  `billing` (`receipt`); every nav key across all roles has one (the dot
  fallback is unused). A nav row picks its glyph by its `$key`, unless the
  `$navByRole` tuple carries an explicit 4th element (an icon key) — used so the
  dentist's **My Schedule** (`dashboard` key) shows `calendar-check` and the
  receptionist's **Appointments** (`dashboard` key) shows `list-checks` instead
  of the admin's `house`. **Twitter/X-style active state** (a scoped
  `<style>` block in `sidebar.php`): at rest the row shows the dim **outline**
  glyph; the active row swaps to the **solid fill** glyph — same white, no
  background chip (`.ico-line` / `.ico-fill` toggle). The old whitened 20px PNG
  nav icons (`assets/icons/*.png` — `home` / `checklist` / … / `history`) were
  deleted 2026-09; only `log-out.png` remains there. The
  **Logout** button in the footer is a Uiverse-style
  outline→fill: a `danger` (red) 2px border on transparent, filling
  `danger` on hover over `.5s`, with the `assets/icons/log-out.png` glyph
  (`brightness-0 invert` → white) nudging `translate-x-[5px]` on hover. Adapted
  from `uiverse.io/reshades` — recoloured to the `danger` token (the user asked
  for red), not its raw `#3654ff` blue. `header.php` + `layout.php` canvas still
  legacy, but the patient **notification bell** (`#bellBtn`, patient-only) was
  restyled to a 40px navy (`#102f51`) circle with an inline white bell SVG that
  "rings" (`@keyframes dgt-bell-ring` rotateZ swing) on hover and dips
  `scale(.85)` on press — a scoped `<style>` in `header.php`, adapted from
  `uiverse.io/vinodjangid07`, recoloured to DGT navy, `prefers-reduced-motion`
  off-switch. The red unread-count badge is unchanged.
  **Desktop sidebar collapse, every role (2026-09, ChatGPT-style):**
  `.sb-toggle-header` — a stroked "panel" glyph (a rounded rectangle split by a
  vertical line, not a Phosphor fill icon like the real nav rows) — overlays
  the brand block's **bottom-right corner**, deliberately aligned with the
  short second line ("Bulacan Dental Clinic") rather than the long first line
  ("DGT One Dental Center"), which needs the row's full width and would
  otherwise get truncated by a reserved icon gutter. The brand tag and
  `.sb-toggle-header` are wrapped in one plain `relative` `<div>` (so the
  button's `absolute` positioning anchors to the brand row's own box, not the
  fixed, full-viewport-height `<aside>` — anchoring to the aside directly was a
  real bug caught by screenshot, pinning the button near the sidebar's bottom
  instead of the header's); it can't be a DOM child of the brand tag itself
  since that tag is a real link for the `patient` role and a `<button>` can't
  legally nest inside an `<a>`. Hidden below the `nav:` 820px breakpoint (the
  mobile off-canvas drawer has its own show/hide mechanism via `.open`);
  clicking it flips a `body.sb-collapsed` class (CSS in `includes/tailwind.php`,
  guarded to `@media (min-width: 820px)`), which shrinks `#sidebar` to a 72px
  icon-only rail, hides `.sb-toggle-header` (nothing replaces it — see below),
  and shifts the content wrapper (`#appShellContent`, the `nav:ml-[244px]` div
  in `layout.php`) to `margin-left: 72px` to match.
  **Re-expanding has no second visible button anywhere** — the logo badge
  itself doubles as the control, matching ChatGPT's own "the brand mark becomes
  the reopen affordance" behaviour (an earlier pass tried a separate rail
  button, then a floating "Open sidebar" pill in the header outside the
  sidebar à la Gemini; both were rejected in favour of this). The badge
  (`#sbLogoToggle` / `.sb-logo-badge`, wrapping both `.sb-logo-img` — the real
  logo — and a `.sb-logo-ico` panel-glyph `<svg>` absolutely stacked on top,
  `opacity: 0` at rest) is never wrapped in `.sb-label`, so it stays put in
  both states and the header's height never changes — the nav list below never
  shifts when collapsing. While collapsed, hovering it swaps the two
  (`body.sb-collapsed #sidebar .sb-logo-badge:hover .sb-logo-img { opacity: 0 }`
  / `.sb-logo-ico { opacity: 1 }`, both `.15s ease`) and `cursor: pointer`;
  clicking it (`#sbLogoToggle` in `app.js`) always **expands** and is a no-op
  while already expanded, so it just behaves like a plain logo (and link, for
  patients) then — the click handler also `preventDefault()`s so it never
  navigates the patient's home link while collapsed.
  Every `.sb-label` span (the brand text, each nav item's label, the
  sidebar-footer profile name/role, "Logout") fades and collapses via a
  `max-width`/`opacity` transition rather than a hard `display:none`, so the
  rail slides shut instead of snapping — no squish, no line-wrap, no flash;
  `#sidebar` / `#appShellContent` transition their own width/margin on a shared
  `--sb-ease` custom property (`cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)`), same `.3s` duration
  for both so the rail and the content column never drift apart. **Smoothed
  2026-09** — the label's `opacity` fade is deliberately shorter than its
  `max-width` collapse (`.16s` vs `.3s`, both starting together) so the text is
  already invisible before the shrinking box would otherwise visibly clip it;
  the earlier version faded both on the same clock and read as the text
  getting chopped mid-transition. `.sb-toggle-header` used to `display:none`
  the instant `body.sb-collapsed` was added — an instant pop at the *start* of
  the collapse — and now fades the same way as a label instead. The old
  collapsed-state rule forcing `justify-content:center` + `padding:0` onto the
  nav rows / brand / footer (with a `position:static` override for the logout
  icon) was **removed outright** — it also applied at the very start of the
  transition, so every icon would snap to "centered in the still-mostly-wide
  rail" for a frame before the width even began visibly shrinking, reading as
  a sideways jump. Nothing needed it: every row's existing, unchanged padding
  already sits close enough to centered once the rail settles at 72px, so
  dropping that toggle just removes the discontinuity — nothing but
  width/margin/opacity animates any more, and `overflow-x: hidden` on
  `#sidebar` guards against a sub-pixel scrollbar flash mid-transition. What's
  left after the labels fade just sits where its normal padding puts it
  (`.sidebar-brand` / `.nav-item` / `.sidebar-user` / `.sidebar-logout` — the
  logout icon in particular, `position:absolute left-4` inside a box that
  happens to end up 48px wide when collapsed, lands dead-center with no
  override needed). Every nav `<a>` carries a `title` attribute so the
  icon-only rail still has a tooltip. `.sb-toggle-header`'s click is wired via
  a shared
  `.sb-toggle` class in `app.js` (`document.querySelectorAll('.sb-toggle')` →
  flips the class + `localStorage['dgt_sidebar_collapsed']` — currently the
  only element with that class, kept generic in case a future control needs
  it); an inline `<script>` right after `<body>` opens in `layout.php` applies
  the stored choice before the sidebar paints, so there's no flash of the
  expanded sidebar on load. Because `nav.js` only ever swaps `#app-content`
  (the sidebar is never removed or re-rendered on an in-app navigation), the
  collapsed state persists automatically across every page without any extra
  re-apply logic.
  **Smoothing the collapse on `auto-fill`/`minmax()` card grids (2026-09)** —
  the user flagged that toggling the collapse bar looked "awkward" specifically
  on the patient **Services** (`#svcGrid`) and **Appointments** (`.appt-grid`)
  pages. Both size their column *count* off their own container width via CSS
  Grid `auto-fill`/`minmax()` (not a fixed Tailwind breakpoint like every other
  responsive grid in the app, e.g. the admin dashboard's `grid-cols-2
  lg:grid-cols-4`), so while `#appShellContent`'s `margin-left` is mid-transition
  the browser re-solves that column count on every animation frame and the
  cards visibly snap between counts instead of smoothly resizing. `app.js`'s
  `setSbCollapsed()` (next to the collapse-toggle wiring) now freezes any
  element matching `REFLOW_GRID_SEL = '#svcGrid, .appt-grid'` to its
  currently-resolved column count (`repeat(N, minmax(0, 1fr))`, read via
  `getComputedStyle(...).gridTemplateColumns`) for the ~320ms the sidebar
  transition takes, so every column stays an ordinary fluid `1fr` track (only
  resizing, never re-counting) until the sidebar settles; the inline override
  is then cleared so the grid re-resolves once, cleanly, for the final width.
  Skipped under `prefers-reduced-motion`, same convention as the rest of the
  app's motion. If a future page adds another `auto-fill`/`minmax()` grid that
  sits under `#app-content`, add its selector to `REFLOW_GRID_SEL` too, or —
  better, if the design allows it — just use a fixed `grid-cols-N` breakpoint
  set instead, which never depends on the animating container width at all.
- **Done — `index.php` (landing page):** **Rebuilt from scratch 2026-09 (third
  pass) in the `floria-landing-page.vercel.app` idiom** — the user asked
  repeatedly, then explicitly: "really make it like Floria, everything, start
  from scratch". Confirmed via a 3-question `AskUserQuestion`:
  **(1) palette — keep the DGT cool palette, borrow only Floria's layout**
  (so: navy `dgt-blue-900` headings + DGT teal/blue accent, NOT Floria's warm
  stone + emerald); **(2) Services — rebuild as a Floria staggered card grid**
  (**superseded 2026-09** — the user later reverted the Services body to the
  horizontal card slider while keeping the new Floria section header; see the
  `homepage-services-carousel` memory); **(3) typography — add the Geist
  typeface + serif-italic accent words.**
  - **Fonts:** Geist + Geist Mono via a Google Fonts `<link>` (Geist IS on
    Google Fonts). Scoped to this page by `body.floria { font-family: 'Geist', … }`
    in the page `<style>` — the rest of the app keeps its system stack, no
    `tailwind.php` change. `.mono` = Geist Mono (prices, `01/02/03` step
    numbers). `.ser` = `Georgia, serif; font-style: italic` — the last word of
    a heading is wrapped in `.ser` (Floria's `var(--font-serif)` trick): "Your
    complete dental care *partner.*", "Our *services.*", "How it *works.*",
    "About *us.*", "Ready when you *are.*".
  - **Type scale:** `$secH` (light sections) / `$secHW` (dark) =
    `clamp(2.25rem,5vw,3.6rem) font-bold tracking-[-0.03em]`, navy / white.
    Hero H1 `clamp(2.7rem,7.4vw,5.15rem) font-extrabold`. `$eyebrow` =
    `text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.24em] text-dgt-teal-600`
    (max one per section). `$lede` = the body-lede class.
  - **Buttons:** `.btn` pill (`h-14 px-8 rounded-full`) in three skins —
    `.btn-light` (white on dark), `.btn-ghost` (outline on dark),
    `.btn-dark` (navy pill on light). Arrow `$arrow` nudges right on hover.
    The old `.book-btn` / `.link-underline` / `bb-*` markup is gone.
  - **Section order:** fixed pill **nav** (`#siteHeader`, `bg-dgt-blue-900/80
    backdrop-blur`, slides down on load via `.in`) → **Hero** (`#home`,
    `min-h-[100svh] bg-dgt-blue-900`, photo at `opacity-[0.22]` under a
    left→right navy scrim (no bottom fade — removed 2026-09, the user found the
    white haze at the hero's foot odd; the hero now ends on a clean navy edge
    against the white Services section), one slow `hero-kb` Ken Burns;
    eyebrow pill, H1, one subcopy line, `.btn-light` + `.btn-ghost`) →
    **Our Services** (`#services`, white, a centered header — serif-accent "Our
    services." + a lede, no eyebrow, no slider arrows) — **rebuilt 2026-09 into a
    static 2-column card grid with a click-to-open detail modal**, replacing the
    horizontal `#svcTrack` slider (which had itself replaced, then been restored
    over, an earlier staggered-grid attempt — see the `homepage-services-carousel`
    memory, now superseded by this pass) at the user's request, from a reference
    screenshot of a "Healthcare Services" card-grid + detail-popup pattern.
    `grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-2 md:gap-7` of `<button class="svc-card">`
    elements (a plain button, not a link — the whole card is clickable, not just
    the icon): each card is a white `rounded-2xl border` box with a header block
    (service name + `services.short_description` — a short, **independently
    written** card blurb, migration `045`; see below) and a circular diagonal-arrow icon chip
    (outline at rest, fills `dgt-teal-500` on `group-hover`) on one row, a
    hairline `border-b` beneath it, then the service photo filling a fixed
    `aspect-[16/10]` box below (a navy gradient + tooth-outline placeholder when
    a service has no `image`); the image zooms `scale-105` on hover via
    `motion-safe:group-hover:` (no custom CSS needed, so no separate
    `prefers-reduced-motion` override is required for it either). **The blurb
    paragraph reserves a fixed 2-line height (`line-clamp-2 min-h-[3rem]`,
    2026-09 fix)** — `.svc-card` is a `flex flex-col`, and CSS Grid's default
    `align-items: stretch` makes both cards in a row match whichever one's
    header/blurb text is taller; before this fix a card whose own blurb fit on
    one line (e.g. Cosmetic Dentistry) sat next to one that wrapped to two
    (Dental Implants), got stretched taller than its own header + fixed-aspect
    photo combined, and showed a blank gap below the photo but still inside
    the card's own border — reported directly ("theres a empty on bottom")
    with a screenshot of exactly that pairing. **First attempt (superseded):**
    made the photo box `flex-auto` so it would grow and absorb the leftover
    stretch height instead of leaving it blank — this did close the gap, but
    it made the photo itself a different height on every card (whichever had
    the shorter header grew its photo more to compensate), which broke the
    grid's visual consistency and drew an immediate follow-up ("now there not
    eqully even make it same height"). **Final fix:** leave the photo box a
    plain fixed-height `shrink-0` `aspect-[16/10]` (uniform on every card,
    matching the original design), and instead make every header reserve the
    *same* height regardless of its actual blurb length — `line-clamp-2`
    caps a longer blurb at two lines with a trailing ellipsis, and
    `min-h-[3rem]` (≈ two lines at this text's `leading-[1.6]`) keeps a
    one-line blurb from collapsing shorter than that reserved space. With
    headers now uniform, the grid's default row-stretch never has anything to
    compensate for, so card / header / photo heights all end up identical
    without any grow/absorb trick. `<img>` stays `absolute inset-0 h-full
    w-full object-cover` inside a `relative` wrapper (so it can never itself
    contribute to the wrapper's flex sizing) even after dropping `flex-auto`,
    since that part was harmless and kept the fix isolated to just the two
    classes that mattered. The `#svcModal` popup image kept the same
    `absolute inset-0` treatment too, though its `aspect-[4/3]` wrapper was
    never at risk of either bug (it's a CSS Grid child with `md:items-center`,
    not a stretched flex column). Verified by measuring every card's card /
    header / photo height across the grid at 1440px — all three numbers are
    now identical for all ten services. Each card
    carries the service's name/description/image as `data-*` attributes —
    **no price anywhere on this section, card or modal** (the user explicitly
    said not to add one after an initial pass showed it, matching neither
    reference screenshot); price stays where it already lived — the patient
    "Our Services" page and the booking wizard's payment summary.
    Clicking a card (or a footer "Our Services" link, or a service-picker Book
    Appointment CTA — anything with `data-svc-link`) opens **`#svcModal`**, a
    fixed-overlay dialog (`role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`, Escape/backdrop-click/×
    all close it, focus moves to the close button on open and back to
    the trigger on close, `document.body.style.overflow` locked while open) —
    plain vanilla JS since `index.php` loads no `app.js`/`openModal()` (it's a
    public page with its own self-contained script, same as every other index.php
    interaction). The close button (`#svcModalClose`) sits at `-right-3 -top-3`
    (`sm:-right-4 sm:-top-4`) — **floating half outside the modal card's own
    corner, not inset inside it** — because an earlier `right-5 top-5` inset
    placement sat on top of the padded grid content and visibly overlapped the
    service photo's corner (flagged directly: "the x is too close to the pic");
    `#svcModalCard` has no `overflow-hidden`, so the button isn't clipped by
    floating outside the card's bounds. The modal is a `max-w-[920px]` white
    card, `md:grid-cols-2`: left column = service name (large, navy, bold), the
    full `services.description` (unrelated wording to the card's blurb — see
    migration `045` below), and a `.btn-dark` **Book Appointment** button
    (→ `$bookUrl`, same target as every other booking CTA on the page); right
    column = the service photo, `order-1` on mobile so the image leads the
    stacked layout there. Fade + scale transition via a
    `.is-in` class (same pattern as `openModal()` in `app.js`, reimplemented
    locally), skipped under `prefers-reduced-motion`. **Below the grid:** a hairline-topped
    CTA row — a "Pick a service, choose a date and time, and reserve your visit online…" line
    on the left and a `.btn-dark` **"Book a visit"** pill on the right (the user
    moved the CTA here from the header for prominence; the line must reflect that
    booking *requires* choosing a service — an earlier "our dentists will talk it
    through" line was cut as misleading) → **How it works** (`#process`, **dark
    `bg-dgt-blue-900`**, Floria "Architecture of Nature" layout: a
    `bg-dgt-teal-500/[0.07] blur-[130px]` glow, and `process-bg.jpg` (a blue-lit
    clinic scene, Pexels #15225509) as the **Floria "Architecture of Nature"
    backdrop**. **The "falls when scrolling" effect is pure CSS, no JS** — this
    is exactly Floria's technique (its process image has zero scroll-parallax

    so scrolling the section slides the image up past the pinned heading. An

    + `mix-blend-lighten` keep the photo edgeless where it overlaps the white
    Services/About sections (no visible rectangle — the user flagged "we can see
    the lines of it"). `#process` is `z-20` but **both `#services` and `#about`
    are `z-30`** — their white backgrounds cap the image's up- and downward
    bleed so it never ghosts into either neighbour (the user flagged the pale

    wrapper still slides in from the left with the section
    (`.rv-l`, ≈ Floria's `x:-100→0`). The left column carries a

    `lg:sticky lg:top-36` left heading column and a right column of three
