Each file's own `reschedCalHtml()`/inline legend was reworded to match (`Slots open` → `Available`, `Full Schedule` → `Fully Booked`, `Clinic closed` → `Closed`), and the "every slot taken" day-detail note changed from "This day has a Full Schedule…" to "This day is fully booked…". At the individual-slot level, every taken `<span class="pcal-slot taken">` gained a `<small>Occupied</small>` (was `Full`) and — new — every free slot (`<button>`/`<a class="pcal-slot free">`) gained a `<small>Available</small>` sub-label, reversing the 2026-09 "a free slot now shows only the time itself" simplification noted above; no new CSS was needed since `.pcal-slot.free`'s existing green text colour and `.pcal-slot.taken small`'s existing red already apply to the new `<small>` children for free, respectively taken up, slots. Verified end-to-end with headless Chrome across all three surfaces (patient Calendar View, the patient booking wizard's date/time step, and the receptionist's Walk-in/Reschedule modal) against real seeded data — a day with every one of its 7 slots filled to the shared 5-chair capacity correctly showed the red "FULLY BOOKED" day pill and its day-detail note, a day with only one slot filled showed a plain green "AVAILABLE" day pill with that one hour reading "OCCUPIED" (red) and the other six reading "AVAILABLE" (green) in the day-detail panel, identically on all three surfaces. **The day-level "Available" tag was removed again the same day, from a screenshot** — the user pointed at the month grid and asked to "remove the available here"; `cellInfo()`'s `open` branch reverts to `tag: ''` (a plain green left-edge, no text) in all three files, while the `full` branch keeps `tag: 'Fully Booked'` (still the useful exception to flag) — individual time-slot "Available"/"Occupied" labels in the day-detail panel are unaffected, this only touched the month-grid day cells. **The individual time-slot buttons show a start–end range (2026-09, same-day follow-up), from a screenshot asking "on time make it like this — 10:00AM-11:00AM"** — previously they showed just the start time ("10:00 AM"); a `slotRange(s)` helper (added next to each file's existing `pad`/`keyOf` helpers in `modules/patient/calendar.php`, `modules/patient/appointments.php`, and `modules/receptionist/dashboard.php`) parses the `HH:MM` slot string, adds one hour for the end (every clinic slot is a flat 1-hour chair booking, so this needed no lookup against the actual next slot — the gap around the lunch break, e.g. 11:00→13:00, is irrelevant to a single slot's own displayed range), and formats both ends through the existing shared `fmtTime()` as `"10:00 AM – 11:00 AM"` (en dash, spaced — matching this app's other range-label convention, e.g. Revenue Reports' `rangeLabel()`). Both the free (`Available`) and taken (`Occupied`) slot renders in all three files now call `slotRange(s)` instead of `fmtTime(s)`. **The longer text needed more room** — `includes/patient_calendar_style.php`'s `.pcal-slots` grid column minimum grew `132px → 168px` (`.rs-pcal .pcal-slots`' own tighter modal override `118px → 150px`) and `.pcal-slot`'s font-size dropped `14px → 13px` (`.rs-pcal .pcal-slot` → `12px`, since the modal is narrower still), plus an explicit `white-space: nowrap` on `.pcal-slot` so a range is never split mid-phrase (e.g. "…11:00" / "AM" landing on separate lines, the exact wrapping bug seen in first-pass testing at the old 132px width) if a future viewport ever comes up short. Verified end-to-end with headless Chrome on a throwaway test patient account: the full-page Calendar View, the booking wizard's embedded calendar, and the CSS shared by the receptionist's identical `.rs-pcal` modal all render every range on one clean line — confirmed both visually (screenshots) and via the exact seven range strings for a real day ("10:00 AM – 11:00 AM" through "5:00 PM – 6:00 PM"). Test account deleted afterward. **A real bug in that last range, caught immediately ("dgtone only open from 10 to 5 not 10 to 6")**: the clinic's `time_slots` setting genuinely ended at `17:00` (5:00 PM, confirmed against the live `settings` table alongside `clinic_close = 17:00`) — the last bookable slot started right at closing time, so blindly adding one hour made the final slot claim "5:00 PM – 6:00 PM," implying the clinic stays open an hour past when it actually closes. A first fix suppressed the range display for whichever slot happened to be last (`slotRange(s, isLast)`); the user then clarified the real intent one message later — **"no remove the 5pm becasue 4 to 5is last one"** — the 5:00 PM slot itself shouldn't be offered at all, since a 1-hour visit starting then would run past the clinic's own closing time; 4:00 PM (running exactly to 5:00 PM close) should be the last bookable slot. **Migration `053_remove_5pm_time_slot.sql`** drops `17:00` from the `time_slots` setting (`'10:00,11:00,13:00,14:00,15:00,16:00,17:00'` → `'…,16:00'`, `clinic_close` itself unchanged at `17:00`) — applied after confirming zero live `pending`/`approved`/`rescheduled` appointments already sat at 5:00 PM, so nothing existing needed to move; `schema.sql`'s seed was updated to match for a fresh install. Because every slot list in the app (`generateSlots()`, `action=slot_list`, `action=availability`, the admin Settings "Clinic Time Slots" chip list) reads this one setting, the 5 PM option disappeared everywhere with no other code change. **The `isLast` special-casing from the first fix was then reverted** (back to a plain, unconditional `slotRange(s)` in all three files) — with 5:00 PM gone, the new last slot (4:00 PM) correctly ends exactly at closing, so the flat "+1 hour" range is accurate again for every slot including the last one; keeping `isLast` would have wrongly suppressed the now-valid "4:00 PM – 5:00 PM" range. Re-verified with headless Chrome on a fresh throwaway account: `action=slot_list` returns exactly 6 slots ending at `16:00`, and a real day's detail panel shows six ranges ending at "4:00 PM – 5:00 PM" with no 5 PM entry at all — confirmed via the exact returned strings and a screenshot. Test account deleted afterward.

All 40 seeded test appointments and the throwaway test patient account were deleted afterward; the receptionist test login used a temp password reissued through the legitimate admin `action=reset_password` endpoint (not a raw DB write), leaving that real account in the same "temp password, must-change-password" state the app's own reset flow always leaves an account in. Choosing a free slot stashes `{date,slot}` in `sessionStorage` (`dgt_book_prefill`) and navigates; `appointments.php`'s IIFE reads and clears it on load and opens the booking wizard on step 1 with **Preferred Date + Preferred Time pre-filled** (`sessionStorage`, not a query string, because nav.js re-runs the target page's script *before* it pushes the new URL). `fillSlotOptions()` still re-validates the slot when the wizard opens, so a slot that filled in between is swapped for the next free one. The patient sidebar is **Appointments / Calendar View / Treatment & Billing / Services** — `modules/patient/services.php` (sidebar key `services`, sidebar label + `<h1>` "Services" — the user shortened it from "Our Services" 2026-09) leads with a teal `.clinic-hero` banner (hard-coded `DGT One Dental Center - Bulacan` + real address / `09764484252` / `customerservice@dgtonedentalph.com` + "Open Monday–Saturday by appointment · Closed Sundays"), then a searchable `.svc-grid` of `.svc-card` (service image with a 🦷 fallback, name, description, green price — no specialty badge and no duration line, the user had both removed) off `services.php?active=1` — **no category filter** (dental-only clinic), just the service list. Notifications are on their own page: the bell dropdown (`header.php`, patient-only) has a **See all notifications** link and each row is a `data-nav` link to **`modules/patient/notifications.php`** — a full table (Notification / Received / Status / actions) with per-row **Mark read** / **Delete**, plus **Mark all read** / **Clear all**. `api/notifications.php` backs it: `list` (`?all=1` → 200 rows, else 25), `count`, `read_all`, `read_one`, `delete`, `clear`. **Update 2026-10-02 (later): both rules are now switches in `includes/functions.php`** — `ONLINE_LEAD_DAYS` (0 = off, set 2 to restore; mirrored as a JS const in `modules/patient/appointments.php` + `calendar.php`) and `BLOCK_PASSED_SLOTS` (false = a slot that already started today is still bookable). Both are OFF for now. **Earlier the same day: the 2-day lead time is OFF again "for now"** (user request): `action=create` / `action=availability` / the wizard `minDate` / Calendar View `earliestKey` all floor at plain today (DB clock), and a slot whose start time has already passed today is reported as taken by `action=availability` and refused by `action=create`. The paragraph below describes the 2-day rule as it was. **Online bookings need 2 days' notice** (removed 2026-09 "for now", **re-added 2026-09** at the user's request) — `action=create` blocks a new request dated less than 2 days out (`$minOnlineDate`, computed on the DB clock — `db()->query('SELECT CURDATE()')` + 2 days, not PHP's `date()`, for the same reason `dentalAssistantsOnDuty()` had to be fixed the same way); `action=availability` mirrors it in `$earliest` (same DB-clock expression) so today and tomorrow render greyed/inert on the calendar exactly like a genuinely past day, and day-after-tomorrow onward is bookable; the booking wizard's date `min` is `todayISO(2)`, and patient Calendar View's `earliestKey` is `today + 2 days` (a client-side mirror of the server's `info.past` flag, kept in sync defensively). **This is a new-request rule only — it does not touch anything already booked.** A front-desk walk-in is still exempt entirely (`action=walk_in` never runs this check; same-day stays normal), and rescheduling an existing appointment is still exempt too — see *Rescheduling* just below, which only ever needed a future date and was never subject to the 2-day floor either before or after this change. **Rescheduling** — a patient just needs a future date: `action=reschedule` blocks a patient from picking earlier than `+1 day` (tomorrow), and the reschedule modal's date `min` is `todayISO(1)`; staff may reschedule to the same day. Front-desk **walk-ins are exempt** entirely (same-day is normal — `validateNew()` itself only blocks past dates). **Reservation-fee / 24-hour notice policy (2026-09):** the reservation fee is **non-refundable** but **transfers automatically** to a rescheduled visit (same `payments` row, same `appointment_id`) — *provided the patient reschedules ≥ 24 h before the appointment's scheduled start*. `action=reschedule` enforces this: a **patient** rescheduling an `approved`/`rescheduled` row whose `scheduled_date + time_slot` is less than 86400 s away gets a 422 ("must be made at least 24 hours before … contact the clinic"). Staff (receptionist/admin/dentist/cashier) are **not** subject to the 24 h gate — they reschedule for a patient who has phoned in. The patient Appointments card hides its **Reschedule** button and shows a muted "Less than 24 hours away … you can still cancel, but the fee is forfeited" note when inside the window (`tooLate` in `card()`). **Cancel** (patient or staff, reason required) exists and always forfeits the fee — it simply stays on the cancelled appointment and is never credited. Full policy text is T&C §3–§4 (`includes/terms_content.php`).

**Booking "for someone else," migration 043 (replaces the earlier migration-042 "family members" table).** Before 2026-09, booking "For Someone Else" only ever wrote free-text `patient_name`/`patient_age`/`relationship` onto the appointment — the appointment itself, and any clinical chart the dentist later built, still hung off `appointments.patient_id` (the **account holder**), not the person actually treated. The user first asked for this fixed ("everyone needs a record like for dentist side") and a `patient_dependents` table (migration 042, no login, family-relationship rows) was built for it — then the user asked to redo it: **"can u just make it like for someone else they can just have a record not adding some family table thing like from walk-in."** Migration 043 drops `patient_dependents` entirely and instead has "for someone else" create a **real patient account**, the same way the front-desk walk-in's "New patient" flow already does — no separate family-relationship table at all. Three explicit decisions shaped the redo: (1) replace the family-member system outright, not keep both; (2) the new account gets **no e-mail and no usable login** ("just a record" — the same shape a walk-in creates, minus the credential handover, since there's no front desk here to hand it to); (3) a repeat visit for the same person is offered from that account's **own past "for someone else" bookings**, not a saved list, so no new table is needed for reuse either. `appointments.treated_patient_id` (nullable, FK → `users(user_id)`, `ON DELETE SET NULL`) is the pointer to "who was actually seen" when it differs from the booking account — `appointments.patient_id` never changes meaning and still means "which login owns/can view/cancel/reschedule this booking."
- **Patient side:** the booking wizard's "For Someone Else" step shows a **Person** `<select>` — "+ New Person" (default) plus anyone this account has booked for before — and, when "+ New Person" is picked, plain **First Name / Last Name / Age** inputs right in the same step (no add-on modal, no separate save button). **Relationship to Patient** is always a free-text input, typed fresh for every booking regardless of whether the person is new or being booked for again (it's per-visit context, not a saved attribute). Age is read-only once an existing person is picked (computed from their real `patient_records.date_of_birth`); Phone Number stays editable (the reachable number for this visit). There is no equivalent management screen — once created, that person's record is edited only by staff via the normal Patient Records screens, exactly like any walk-in-created patient.
- **Server side:** `api/appointments.php action=create`'s `validateNew()` skips its raw name/age check when `booking_for='other'` and either a `treated_patient_id` or a `new_patient` payload is present, and now also requires a non-empty `relationship` for any "for someone else" booking. For a **new person**, `createUnlinkedPatientRecord()` (`api/_bootstrap.php`) inserts a bare `users` row (role `patient`, a real `makeUsername()`-generated username, a random unshown `tempPassword()` hash, `is_verified = 0`, no e-mail) plus a `patient_records` row with an approximate `date_of_birth` from the given age (DB-clock convention, same as `api/records.php action=create`'s own walk-in fallback) — this is deliberately **not** login-capable in practice (no e-mail on file means no password-reset path either). For a **reused person**, the server re-derives `patient_name`/`patient_age` from that person's real account, scoped by `WHERE a.patient_id = ? AND a.treated_patient_id = ? AND a.booking_for = 'other'` on the caller's own past appointments — a patient can never look up or reuse anyone outside their own booking history. Either way `patient_name`/`patient_age` are never trusted as the client sent them. `GET action=for_others` (`apiAuth`'d inline to the `patient` role, same file) returns that reuse list: `treated_patient_id`, `full_name`, computed `age` — built from a plain query over the account's own appointments, not a saved table. A walk-in (`action=walk_in`) never sends any of this — walk-ins are always `booking_for='self'`, unaffected.
- **Records side:** because "for someone else" now creates a genuine `users` + `patient_records` row, that person just appears in the ordinary dentist/receptionist Patient Records list like any other patient — `api/records.php`'s `list`/`get`/`save` actions are back to their pre-042 single-query shape, no `type` param, no merge, no "Family Member Of" column. The one thing that does carry over: a visit's last-visit/visit-count and appointment-history queries match on **either** `(a.patient_id = ? AND a.treated_patient_id IS NULL)` — a self-booking — **or** `a.treated_patient_id = ?` — a visit where this person was booked for by someone else — so a "for someone else" visit still counts on the treated person's own record and never on the booking account's.
- **Deliberately out of scope (kept small on purpose):** there is no self-service way for a patient to edit or rename a person they created via "for someone else" — that's staff's job now, same as any walk-in-created record. `appointmentModal()`'s "For: `<name>` (`<relationship>`)" display line and the receipt/View-modal rendering were **not changed** — they already read `patient_name`/`relationship`/`patient_age` straight off the appointment row. No data migration was run from migration 042 to 043: every appointment's `dependent_id` was set to `NULL` before the column was renamed to `treated_patient_id`, so an old "for someone else" visit keeps its own `patient_name`/`patient_age`/`relationship` exactly as before, it just no longer links to a separate per-person record (the `patient_dependents` rows it pointed at are gone).

**The clinic is closed on Sundays** — `isClinicClosed()` in `api/appointments.php` (Sunday = weekday 0) rejects any Sunday date in `validateNew()` (online + walk-in), the `reschedule` action, and `action=slots` (returns `blocked`); the dentist calendar hatches Sunday cells and labels them "Closed". (A 2026-09 "for now" toggle that allowed Sunday booking was reverted at the user's request.)
2. **Receptionist** approves (assigns a dentist → status `approved`) or declines (with a reason the patient sees). **Walk-ins now enter the queue as `pending` too** (2026-09 — the user wanted both online and walk-in patients visible in one "waiting for their turn" queue): the front desk logs the walk-in, it sits in **Appointments for Approval** and counts on the dashboard's **Pending Schedule** card, and the receptionist hits **Approve** when it's the patient's turn (`api/appointments.php action=confirm` — assigns the dentist and flips the status to `approved`; **it does not check the patient in**, that is always a separate step from the Approved Appointments table when the patient actually arrives). **A walk-in must resolve to a real patient account** — the Front Desk **Walk-in Booking** modal has an *Existing patient* / *New patient* toggle: *Existing* searches `records.php?action=list&q=` (debounced) and the pick fills age + phone; *New* collects First / Middle / Last **+ a required e-mail** (needed for password reset) and calls `records.php?action=create` (making the `users` + `patient_records` rows) before booking; that endpoint issues a temp password + `is_verified = 1`. The modal also has a **required Service `<select>`** (from `services.php?active=1`) so the walk-in carries a `service_id` like an online booking (`action=walk_in` 422s without a valid active service). It then shows the **username + temporary password once** so the walk-in can also sign in and book online under the same account. **The Time `<select>` now blocks an already-full slot, the same as an online booking (2026-09, revised same day)** — `fillWalkinSlotOptions()` (a near-literal port of the patient booking wizard's own `fillSlotOptions()`) calls `action=slots&date=` on the Date field's `change` (and once on open, for the default today value), `disabled`s any option whose slot has hit capacity (labelled "— Full", e.g. "11:00 AM — Full") and auto-selects the next open slot if the current one just filled; if every slot that day is taken it shows a single disabled "— Fully booked, choose another day —" option. `api/appointments.php action=walk_in` re-checks the identical `slotIsFull()` server-side as a backstop, returning the same 422 `action=create` does. **This reverses the walk-in flow's original design** — walk-ins used to be deliberately exempt from the capacity gate entirely (a physically-present patient could still be squeezed into a "full" hour; the front desk would call in the roaming dentist and reassign the overflow), and a first pass here only *labelled* a full slot while leaving it selectable. **The clinic's own dentist clarified the real-world rule (2026-09): online and walk-in draw from the exact same 5-chair capacity per hour, and it must never be exceeded by either source** — so a walk-in can no longer be booked past capacity at all; the front desk must pick a genuinely open slot, or a different day, exactly like a patient booking online would have to. `api/appointments.php action=walk_in` **requires** `patient_id` to be an active `role='patient'` row and 422s otherwise — there is no receptionist-record fallback any more, so every walk-in (and any repeat visit) accumulates under one Patient Record with a clinical chart and billing history. Walk-ins now book with `booking_for = 'self'`. A future-dated walk-in (or any online booking) is **checked in** later from the Front Desk *Approved Appointments* table — the status badge stays "Approved", but check-in is what lets the dentist log the procedure. The Front Desk "Appointments for Approval" table is a wide 13-column view (#, Ref. No., Email, Patient Name, Relation, Age, Phone, Service, Doctor, Date, Payment, Receipt, Actions — the headers were shortened 2026-09) inside an `overflow-x-auto` wrap, styled by the per-file `$compactCls` (12px, `px-[7px]` cells, `align-top`). **Cells wrap instead of scrolling (2026-09):** `$compactCls` has no blanket `whitespace-nowrap`; the Email and Service cells wrap at `max-w-[150px]` via the JS `CLIP` const (`[overflow-wrap:anywhere]`, no ellipsis), Patient Name / Relation / Doctor wrap freely, and only the cells that must stay one line — #, Age, Phone, Date, Payment, Receipt — carry the `NW` (`whitespace-nowrap`) class; the Actions buttons are `flex-wrap`. So on a wide desktop every row is one line with no scrollbar, and on a laptop-width window the long cells break to two+ lines rather than forcing a horizontal scroll. The page is `$PAGE_FULL` (full-bleed, like every module page). the row list comes from `appointments.php?filter=online_queue` (now **every pending row, online + walk-in**; returns `account_email`, `account_phone`, `patient_phone`, `relationship`); the Patient Name cell carries an Online/Walk-in `srcBadge()`; the Phone cell shows `patient_phone` and falls back to `account_phone`. Payment Method renders a badge from `payments.reservation_method` — `CASH` / `GCASH` / `BANK TRANSFER` (green) when a reservation payment is on file, else `NOT PAID` (red).

**Walk-in reservation fee / downpayment (2026-09, at the clinic's request — matching the instructor's exact downpayment rule).** Unlike an online booking, a walk-in never *required* a reservation fee (the patient is already standing at the counter) — but the front desk can now optionally collect one on the spot, the same way the online flow already does: `walkInModal()` gained a **"Collect a reservation fee / downpayment now"** checkbox (unchecked by default, so a plain walk-in with no fee behaves exactly as before), which reveals **Amount** (defaults to the clinic's `reservation_fee` setting, editable) and **Payment Method**. `api/appointments.php action=walk_in` validates all of this server-side too and, when a fee was collected, inserts a `payments` row alongside the appointment inside one transaction — `reservation_fee_paid` / `reservation_method` / `reservation_reference`, `payment_status` left `'unpaid'` (a reservation-fee credit alone never advances status past unpaid, matching `recomputePayment()`'s existing online-booking rule — see the Discounts section's cashier engine paragraph). Because this reuses the exact same `payments.reservation_fee_paid` column and `recomputePayment()` logic the online flow already relies on, it **automatically deducts from the total at checkout** with zero cashier-side changes: `billing.php`'s existing "Less: Reservation Deposit" totals row and `receipt.php`'s equivalent both already key off `a.reservation_fee_paid > 0` regardless of `booking_type`. **Migration `054_walkin_reservation_fee.sql`** added `'cash'` to `payments.reservation_method`'s ENUM (previously `bank`/`gcash`/`paymaya` only, since online booking never offers Cash). A plain walk-in with the checkbox left off still creates no `payments` row at all until the dentist completes the visit, unchanged. **The companion instructor rule — "restrict the Refund status/action strictly to online bookings" — needed no code change**: confirmed by grepping the whole codebase that there is no Refund payment status, button, or API action anywhere in the app for *any* booking source; the only "refund" concept that exists is the policy copy stating the reservation fee is never refunded (T&C §3-§4), which already applies identically to online and walk-in. Confirmed with the user via `AskUserQuestion` before treating this as done rather than guessing and building an unrequested feature.

**Payment section simplified for front-desk use, same day, 2026-09 follow-up.** The first pass mirrored the online booking wizard too closely — a GCash-only method with its own phone-number field, plus a strict 13-digit reference format. The user asked for something simpler, matching how a receptionist actually takes a walk-in payment: **Payment Method** offers **Cash / GCash / Bank Transfer** (`payments.reservation_method`'s ENUM already had `bank` from the online flow, so no further migration was needed). Choosing GCash or Bank Transfer reveals one generic **Reference Number** text field (`#w_resRefWrap`/`#w_resRef`) for the transaction code the patient already has — no PH-mobile GCash-number field any more (the front desk never needs the patient's GCash number, only proof the transaction happened), and no format is enforced on the reference itself (unlike the 13-digit-only rule on the *online* GCash field — this one field covers both GCash and bank-slip references, which have different shapes), just a non-empty check plus the same duplicate-transaction check (`wireRefCheck()` against `check_reference_number.php`) every other reference field in the app already gets. Cash hides the field entirely. **Deliberately excludes a QR code image and a receipt-upload input** — those are the online booking wizard's own affordances (the patient scans/uploads on their own device); a walk-in patient pays the receptionist directly at the counter or hands over a physical slip, so neither ever existed in this modal and nothing needed to be removed. `api/appointments.php action=walk_in` was loosened to match — it accepts `cash`/`gcash`/`bank`, requires only a non-empty reference (plus the duplicate check) for the latter two, and no longer expects or stores a `gcash_number`.

**Payment Method restyled to card radios (2026-09, same-day follow-up), from a screenshot of the patient booking wizard's own "Select Payment Method" step.** The user pointed at that step's `.pm-card` layout (a full-width bordered box per option, radio + bold title + a muted one-line subtitle, the selected one tinted) and asked for the walk-in modal's Payment Method picker to look the same, replacing the plain inline radio row the simplification pass above had left it with. `modules/receptionist/dashboard.php` now `require`s `includes/patient_booking_style.php` (already dark-mode-aware, namespaced `.pm-*` classes — safe to pull into a second page since nothing there collides with the receptionist dashboard's own classes) and reuses `.pm-cards`/`.pm-card`/`.pm-card-txt`/`.pm-card-t`/`.pm-card-s` verbatim rather than reinventing the look with Tailwind utilities. A page-local `RES_METHOD_SUB` const supplies the walk-in's own three subtitles ("Pay at the counter" / "Pay using your GCash wallet" / "Pay via online banking" — different wording from the online wizard's `METHOD_SUB`, since a walk-in has no QR/bank-account panel to describe). Selecting a card toggles the `.on` class on it and syncs the Reference Number field's visibility, mirroring the exact interaction pattern `modules/patient/appointments.php`'s own payment step already uses (`document.querySelectorAll('.pm-card').forEach(c => c.classList.toggle('on', …))`). At this point the **"Collect a reservation fee / downpayment now"** checkbox was still unchanged (a distinct opt-in toggle, separate from the Payment Method picker inside it). Verified end-to-end with headless Chrome against a real receptionist login: at rest Cash renders as the highlighted (`.on`) card with the Reference Number field hidden; selecting GCash correctly re-highlights only the GCash card and reveals the field; a screenshot confirms the rendered layout visually matches the reference screenshot (full-width bordered cards, tinted selection, subtitle text) with no QR image or file-upload input anywhere in the modal.

**The checkbox was removed outright the same day, in a third follow-up — the reservation fee is now a mandatory, standard part of every walk-in, matching online booking's own always-required fee.** The user asked directly to drop the opt-in checkbox and move the payment fields up to sit right under Age/Phone Number, ahead of Service. Field order in the modal is now: Patient picker → Age → Phone Number → **Reservation Fee / Downpayment Amount** → **Select Payment Method** (the `.pm-card` radios) → Reference Number (GCash/Bank only) → Service → Date & Time → Concern. Both the client submit handler and `api/appointments.php action=walk_in` were tightened to match — `reservation_method` and a positive `reservation_amount` are now required on every walk-in (previously optional, gated behind the now-removed checkbox), and a GCash/Bank reference is still required and duplicate-checked exactly as before; the server always inserts the `payments` row now (no more conditional branch). This reverses the walk-in flow's original "never required a reservation fee" design from earlier in this same feature's history (see the paragraph above it) — the front desk now collects one for every walk-in, same as online. Verified end-to-end with headless Chrome: `#w_resToggle` no longer exists in the DOM at all; the Amount field and all three payment cards render unconditionally with no toggle needed; the rendered field order matches Age → Phone → Amount → Cash/GCash/Bank cards → Reference → Service exactly; a full Cash walk-in submission (no explicit method selection needed, Cash is the default-checked card) succeeded and persisted `reservation_method='cash'` server-side. Test appointment/payment rows were deleted afterward.

**Amount locked to the fee setting, and the reference label reads "GCash"/"Bank" instead of generic (2026-09, same-day follow-up).** `#w_resAmount` gained a plain `readonly` attribute (plus a muted `bg-steel-50`/`bg-white/5` fill so it visually reads as locked, not just editable-but-ignored) — the receptionist can no longer type over it; it always shows whatever `reservation_fee` (₱500 by default) the admin has configured in Settings, the same setting the online booking wizard's own fixed fee already reads. `readonly` still leaves `.value` readable by the submit handler's `v('w_resAmount')` exactly as before, so no other JS changed. Separately, the Reference Number field's `<label>` now has an inner `#w_resRefLbl` span that `syncResMethod()` rewrites per selected card — **"GCash Reference No."** for GCash, **"Bank Reference No."** for Bank Transfer (falls back to the generic "Reference Number" for any other value, though only those two ever reveal the field at all) — matching the online booking wizard's own per-method labelling instead of one flat generic label for both. Verified end-to-end with headless Chrome: the Amount field reports `readOnly: true` with value `500`; selecting each payment card in turn updates the label to the correct text ("GCash Reference No." / "Bank Reference No." / hidden entirely for Cash); a screenshot confirms the locked, muted-styled Amount field.

**Reference Number now enforces the exact same per-method format the online booking wizard already does (2026-09, same-day follow-up) — GCash exactly 13 digits, Bank 10-20 alphanumeric characters — reversing the earlier "no format enforced, just non-empty" decision from the initial simplification pass.** Since the label is now method-specific, applying the matching format made sense too. The trickier part: `#w_resRef` is one persistent DOM element shown/hidden across method switches, but `wireRefCheck()`'s format rule is captured once in its closure at call time — calling it again on the same, already-wired element would just stack a second set of debounced listeners. Fixed the same way the patient booking wizard's own `payPanel()`/`wirePanel(m)` avoids this: `syncResMethod()` now rebuilds `#w_resRefWrap`'s entire inner markup (a fresh `<label>`+`<input id="w_resRef">`+error `<div>`) on every method change and calls `wireRefCheck()` fresh against that new element with `REF_FORMATS[method]` (`{gcash: /^\d{13}$/, bank: /^[A-Za-z0-9]{10,20}$/}`, literal duplicates of the constants `modules/patient/appointments.php`'s own `validatePayment()` uses) — so a stale value or stale listener never survives a GCash↔Bank switch, confirmed live (switching from a filled GCash field to Bank leaves the fresh input genuinely empty, not carrying the old value over). The walk-in submit handler (`w_go` click) also gained a hard format check before allowing submission — `if (!REF_FORMATS[method].re.test(ref)) return toast(...)` — mirroring the online wizard's own `validatePayment()` gate rather than relying solely on the live inline check + disabled-button state. `api/appointments.php action=walk_in` was tightened to match server-side too: `preg_match('/^\d{13}$/', $resRef)` for GCash and `preg_match('/^[A-Za-z0-9]{10,20}$/', $resRef)` for Bank, literally the same patterns and error text `action=create`'s own GCash/bank validation already uses a little earlier in the same file. Verified end-to-end with headless Chrome: a 3-digit GCash reference shows the red inline error "GCash Reference Number must be exactly 13 digits" and disables Book & Schedule; a valid 13-digit one clears it and shows the green "✓ Reference number available" duplicate-check state; switching to Bank Transfer rebuilds the field with an empty value and the "Bank Reference No." label; a 5-character bank reference is correctly rejected at submit time with a toast (`"Bank Reference Number must be 10-20 characters."`); a 14-character alphanumeric one submits successfully and persists `reservation_method='bank'` server-side. Test appointment/payment rows were deleted afterward. **The reference-number duplicate check is shared across booking sources, not scoped to walk-ins** — `referenceInUse()` (`api/_bootstrap.php`) queries the whole `payments` table (plus `counter_transactions`) with no `booking_type` filter at all, so a reference already used on an *online* booking is correctly rejected if a receptionist tries to reuse it on a walk-in, and vice versa; confirmed live by reusing a real online-booking GCash reference in the walk-in modal — rejected client-side with the duplicate message and, via a direct API call bypassing the disabled button, rejected server-side too (HTTP 409).

**The reference field also carries the same input attrs/placeholder as the online wizard's own `#p_ref` field (2026-09, same-day follow-up)** — GCash gets `type="tel" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="13" pattern="[0-9]{13}" placeholder="13-digit reference number"` (numeric keypad on mobile, hard 13-char cap); Bank gets `maxlength="20" pattern="[A-Za-z0-9]{10,20}" placeholder="10–20 letters / digits"`. A `REF_ATTRS` map (mirroring `REF_LABELS`/`REF_FORMATS`) supplies the right attribute string per method, spliced into the same freshly-rebuilt `<input id="w_resRef">` `syncResMethod()` already constructs on every method switch. Verified live: the GCash field's DOM properties read exactly `{type:'tel', inputMode:'numeric', maxLength:13, placeholder:'13-digit reference number', pattern:'[0-9]{13}'}`; the Bank field reads `{type:'text', maxLength:20, placeholder:'10–20 letters / digits', pattern:'[A-Za-z0-9]{10,20}'}` — both byte-for-byte matching `modules/patient/appointments.php`'s own `#p_ref` markup.

Verified end-to-end with headless Chrome against a real receptionist login: the three cards render with the right labels, no QR image or `<input type="file">` exists anywhere in the modal, the Reference Number field correctly stays hidden for Cash and appears for Bank Transfer, a fresh bank reference passes the live duplicate check, and a submitted Bank Transfer walk-in persisted `reservation_method='bank', reservation_reference='BANKTEST12345'` exactly as entered. Test appointment/payment rows were deleted afterward.

Receipt is a **View** button or "No receipt" (a walk-in's own reservation fee has no receipt image — it was paid in person, not uploaded); Actions are **Approve** (the modal just assigns the dentist — check-in stays a separate step) and **Decline** (the per-row **Details** button was removed 2026-09 — the `data-view` → `appointmentModal()` handler on `#reqTable` stays wired but nothing triggers it here now).

**Payment Decline & Resubmission (migration `056`, 2026-09) — online bookings only.** A receptionist reviewing a pending online request may reject just the reservation receipt without cancelling the appointment. There is **one Decline button** on every row in Appointments for Approval — not two — and which flow it opens depends on that row's own `booking_type` (`data-booking-type` on the button, checked client-side): on an **online** row it opens the pause-and-resubmit flow described below; on a **walk-in** row it opens the plain full-cancel flow (its optional reservation fee was collected in person, so there's nothing to "resubmit"). **A pending online request can no longer be declined outright from this button** — declining it always pauses it for resubmission rather than cancelling it; a walk-in's Decline still fully cancels, unchanged. (This merged a short-lived design where online rows briefly had two adjacent buttons — "Decline" and "Decline Payment" — side by side; the user asked for just one.) It opens the same two-step confirm pattern as every other status-changing action here — step 1 a **reason `<select>`** (Blurry Image / Reference Code Mismatch / Invalid Screenshot / Other, the last requiring a typed detail) + an optional free-text detail textarea, step 2 a plain-language "this pauses the request, doesn't cancel it" confirmation — then calls **`api/appointments.php action=decline_payment`**, which requires `status='pending'` and flips it to the new **`payment_declined`** status (`appointments.status` ENUM's 6th value), writing the combined "Label: detail" string into the *existing* `decline_reason` column plus the slug into a new `payment_decline_code` column — deliberately **not** touching `cancellation_reason`/`staff_cancellation_reason` (those mean "why was this cancelled," and this appointment isn't being cancelled). The patient is `notify()`'d with the reason.

On the patient's own Appointments page, `payment_declined` is a fully first-class status — its own tab (**Payment Declined**, between Pending and Approved) and its own badge colour (a one-off burnt-orange hex, `BADGE_C.payment_declined` in `app.js`, distinct from Pending's yellow and Cancelled's red, same "entity colour outside the token set" exception as `src-online`/`src-walkin`). The card shows the reason (reusing the existing cancelled-row reason box, `st.key === 'cancelled' || declined`) and an **Update Payment / Resubmit Receipt** button (`canResubmitPayment = declined && !past`) that reopens the exact **Payment Information** step from the booking wizard — `payPanel()`, the OCR scan functions, `wireRefCheck()` — now factored so the receipt-upload wiring (`wireReceiptUpload()`) is shared between a brand-new booking and a resubmission, rather than duplicated. The appointment's own date/time/service are shown read-only in an Appointment Summary block and are never re-collected — only the reservation method/reference/receipt are. Cancel is still offered on a `payment_declined` row (`canCancel` now also true for `declined`, independently of `active`) in case the patient would rather not resubmit at all; **Reschedule is not** (nothing about the date/time is the issue). Submitting calls the same `action=upload_receipt` the wizard uses, then **`action=resubmit_payment`** (patient-only, own-booking-only, same past-date guard as `action=cancel`) — this re-runs the identical reservation-payment validation `action=create` uses (factored into a shared `validateReservationPayment($in, $excludePaymentId)` helper so the two paths can't drift), **excluding the appointment's own existing `payments` row** from the duplicate-reference check via `referenceInUse()`'s `$excludePaymentId` param, so resubmitting doesn't trip over the very reference/receipt already on file for it. On success it **updates the existing `payments` row in place** (not a new one) and flips the appointment back to plain **`pending`** — the exact status a fresh request starts at — clearing `decline_reason`/`payment_decline_code`, so it reappears in the receptionist's existing Appointments for Approval queue (`filter=online_queue` is already just `WHERE status='pending'`) with no separate queue or filter needed. `expireLapsedPendingAppointments()` (the sweep that auto-cancels a `pending` request whose date lapsed unapproved) now also sweeps a lapsed `payment_declined` row the same way, so an ignored resubmission prompt doesn't sit forever. The admin **Appointment** page's status filter/metric-tile row and `modules/admin/logs.php`'s underlying `historyLogRows()`/`action=logs` were extended with the new status (a 6th tile, dropdown option, `$valid` whitelist, and a `payment_declined` count) for full audit visibility. Verified end-to-end with headless Chrome against a real seeded pending online booking: Decline Payment correctly moved it out of the approval queue and into `payment_declined` with the exact combined reason text; the patient's own page showed the right tab/badge/reason and the resubmit modal; resubmitting with a fresh reference + a re-uploaded receipt correctly updated the same `payments` row and flipped the appointment back to `pending`, reappearing in the receptionist's queue. All test rows were deleted afterward.
3. **Dentist** logs procedures in the "Services Availed" modal and marks the visit `completed` → this auto-creates a `payments` row in the cashier queue. The modal parses `appointments.notes` into the patient's **initial concern** (everything before the `Treatment notes:` marker), shows it in an info banner ("None specified" when blank), and pre-ticks the service on `appointments.service_id`, tagged "Booked" (set for both online bookings and walk-ins now that the walk-in modal has a required Service picker). The dentist can tick/untick any box and edit each charge; the modal previews `Total Charges − reservation fee paid = Balance to Cashier`. The modal is the shared `window.logProcedureModal({appt, services, onDone})` in `app.js`, opened **only from the Queue** (Today's Schedule, which is today-only). The **calendar day view is read-only** — every row there is View-only, no "Log Procedure" button. `api/treatments.php` also rejects a future `scheduled_date` as a backstop. The dentist's queue and calendar day-detail tables split booking source from lifecycle: a **Scheduled** column shows an Online/Walk-In badge (`srcBadge()` in `app.js`, indigo/teal), and the **Status** column (`opStatus()`) shows an amber **Pending** badge for a booked-but-untreated `scheduled` row (a patient waiting in the queue) and the real badge for `rescheduled`/`completed`/`cancelled`. "Log Procedure" flips the row to `completed` and it moves to the Recently Completed table, whose Services cell and the calendar's Service cell both use `serviceSummary()` ("A, B +N more" past two items). The shared View modal lists every performed service line-by-line with a **Total** foot row, shows **Booked service** vs **Services availed** once procedures exist, and splits `appointments.notes` into a **Patient's concern** row and a **Treatment notes (dentist)** row. The dentist's "Consultation Notes" post as `dentist_notes` and the backend appends them to `appointments.notes` as a new `Treatment notes: …` line (`api/treatments.php action=log` accepts `services_availed`/`dentist_notes`, with the older `services`/`notes` keys still honoured).
4. **Cashier** works from `modules/cashier/transactions.php` (sidebar key `transactions`, the role's home via `roleHome()`). The cashier sidebar is exactly **Transaction / Revenue Reports / Calendar** — no Dashboard, no Transaction Logs nav entry (`modules/cashier/logs.php` still resolves by URL). "Revenue Reports" points at `modules/admin/reports.php` (its guard is `checkAuth(['admin','cashier'])`; `api/reports.php` lets a cashier hit `action=revenue` only, other actions stay admin). "Calendar" is `modules/cashier/calendar.php`, an identical read-only copy of the dentist calendar guarded to `cashier`. The Transaction page: bare header **"Transaction"** (no breadcrumb — the user had the `Dashboard › Transaction` / `Dashboard › Transaction › Billing` breadcrumb rows removed from both this page and `billing.php`, same "keep the area under the heading clean" preference as the no-subtitle rule), card **"Patient Transactions"** with a single **Refresh** button — **no "+ Add Transaction"**. The over-the-counter transaction modal (unbooked walk-in dental service / manual billing adjustment written to `counter_transactions`) was **removed at the user's request (2026-09)**; `payments.php?action=counter_add` / `action=counter_list` and the whole `counter_transactions` table still exist but have **no UI**, and the Transaction page no longer lists counter rows. Table columns: #, Name, Specialist, Service, Date of Service, Status, Action — the `#txTable` carries an inline `style="table-layout:fixed !important;width:100%"` (belt-and-braces over the `table-fixed` utility) plus a `<colgroup>` of explicit per-column widths (**5 / 23 / 14 / 16 / 16 / 9 / 17 %**, all left-aligned; Date of Service th + td are `whitespace-nowrap`, its cell is `fmtDate(q.scheduled_date)` only — **date, no time** (2026-09: briefly showed `fmtDate · fmtTime`, then the time was removed again at the user's request, since the stacked-date-time treatment is scoped to the patient Treatment & Billing History table only, see the stacked-date-cell paragraph below)) so the columns are evenly gapped across the full-bleed card and the **Proceed to Billing** button (itself `whitespace-nowrap`) stays on one line at laptop width. Rows are billing-queue appointments only (`payments.php?action=queue`, **`completed`-only** — a dentist marking a visit `completed` auto-creates the `payments` row that lands here as **Pending**), **ordered newest-completed-first (2026-09)** — `ORDER BY pay.payment_id DESC, a.appointment_id DESC` (was `a.scheduled_date ASC, a.time_slot ASC`, oldest-scheduled-first); the user asked for new ones to appear "on top rather than at the bottom." `payment_id` is a strictly-increasing autoincrement stamped the instant the dentist's completion auto-creates the row, so it's a reliable "most recently entered the queue" signal independent of the visit's own (possibly much earlier-booked) `scheduled_date`; `appointment_id DESC` is the tiebreak for the rare legacy row with no payment row at all. The **only** row action is a single blue **Proceed to Billing** — a `data-nav` link to `modules/cashier/billing.php?appointment=<id>` (the cashier has no Reschedule / Cancel / No-Show — those are the receptionist's).
   **`modules/cashier/billing.php`** is the full-page Service Invoice (Figure 4.1), `$PAGE_FULL` with a full-width `.invoice-sheet` inside a `.card.card-inv` (roomy padding): centered header (hard-coded `CLINIC_NAME` / `CLINIC_LINES` in the page script: `DGT One Dental Center - Bulacan`, real address `B-6L-47 Delrey Bldg., Bgy. Tungkong Mangga, San Jose Del Monte, Bulacan`, a `Raffy D.G. Tamayo - Prop.` line, and a `NON-VAT Reg. TIN: 482-915-307-00000` line — no phone/email line; this TIN line was briefly removed 2026-09 as (mis-scoped) instructor feedback, then restored the same day once the user clarified the feedback actually meant the payer-entered "with TIN" field below, not the clinic's own registration line — see the dedicated paragraph below) + `SERVICE INVOICE`; Date (readonly text, no `type="date"` picker — 2026-09, same "never cashier-editable" treatment as the Payment Date field below; purely cosmetic/display, never submitted to the backend, always today's date from `todayISO()`) / Received from / Phone Number / "The sum of" (auto words) / "pesos (₱)" fields (the "with TIN (optional)" field that used to sit between Received-from and Phone Number was removed 2026-09, see below); an **editable line-items table** (Qty · Unit · Description · Unit Cost · Amount, red-× delete) seeded from the dentist's logged procedures, with **+ Add Service** (green, service picker) and **+ Add-ons** (blue, free row); a right **totals panel** — Total Sales → less SC/PWD 20% (checkbox) → **less an admin Promotion** (the `Less: <promo name>` row; see *Discounts & promotions* below) → Total Due → less Doctor's Fee 20% **(record only)** → less Withholding Tax **(record only)** → less Reservation Fee → **Total Amount Due** → Received Amount → Change (relabelled **Balance after downpayment** while Installment is selected). On a **fully-paid** re-render the "Total Amount Due" / "The sum of" / pesos / Total Amount fields show the **billable** figure (Total Due − reservation credit), **not** the now-zero `remaining_balance` — a ₱0.00 there reads as if the visit were free; Received Amount + Change below show the settlement. `receipt.php` does the same (`$dueShown` = `$billable` when `payment_status = 'fully_paid'`, else the remaining balance; the interim `Less: Payments Received` row only shows while still `partially_paid`); left payment block — a **Promotion / Discount** `<select>` of the promos eligible for the invoice (`no-print` — it's a screen control; the applied promo prints as the `Less: <name>` totals row), **Payment Type** (Full Payment / Installment radios — **prints** on the invoice, same as Form of Payment), **Cash / GCash / Bank / Split** radios. **Split** (migration `021_payment_mode_split.sql` adds `'split'` to the `payments.payment_mode` enum) reveals a `#pm_split` panel with a Cash / GCash / Bank amount each — a method's reference block (`#pm_gcash` / `#pm_bank`) appears once its amount is > 0, the three sum into a read-only **Split Total** which drives (read-only) **Received Amount**, and `markPaid()` sends `split_cash` / `split_gcash` / `split_bank`. The `#pm_split` / `#pm_gcash` / `#pm_bank` panels (and `#promoField` / `#promoRow`) are toggled with the **`hidden` attribute**, not `style.display`, because the print stylesheet's `.inv-grid2 { display:grid !important }` would otherwise force every non-selected method's panel back onto the printed invoice; `includes/invoice_style.php` carries `.invoice-sheet [hidden] { display:none !important }` so a hidden panel stays hidden on the printout — the printed invoice shows **only the chosen Form of Payment's fields**. `payments.php action=settle` distributes `$applied` across `amount_cash` / `amount_gcash` / `amount_bank` (change is taken out of the cash portion — rejected if it exceeds it), validating each method's reference only when its amount is non-zero. The GCash / Bank fields use the same `validatePayMode()` rules as the patient booking form (GCash Reference exactly 13 digits · Bank Reference 10-20 alphanumeric) — blocking **Paid** with red borders + inline `.field-err`, re-checked in `payments.php action=settle`; the GCash / bank reference fields also run the live `wireRefCheck()` duplicate check (see step 1). GCash → **GCash Reference No.** only (13 digits); Bank → **Bank Reference No.** only (10-20 alphanumeric) — **the GCash Number and Bank / Branch + Account No. input fields were removed 2026-09, instructor feedback**; see the dedicated paragraph below), **Payment Date** (readonly text, no longer a `type="date"` picker — 2026-09, the user asked that it never be cashier-editable; it always shows, and `action=settle` always persists, the DB clock's current date via `SELECT CURDATE()`, ignoring any client-sent value, so a bill can't be backdated/forward-dated from the browser), Total Amount, Sr. Citizen TIN, OSCA/PWD ID; a `.inv-sign` flex row (two blank signature lines: "Signature" left, "Cashier / Authorized Representative" right — no name printed), and a full-width centered `.inv-disclaimer` ("THIS DOCUMENT IS NOT VALID FOR CLAIMING INPUT TAXES") as the very last element of the card, below the action buttons. **Doctor's Fee & Withholding are shown but never reduce what the patient pays** (Total Amount Due = Total Sales − SC/PWD − promo − reservation − prior payments). The **Print Preview** and **Pay** buttons sit in a `#invActions` bar **below** the invoice card, outside `.invoice-sheet` (all `.no-print`) — relabelled from "Print / Save PDF" and "Paid" 2026-09. **A separate "Save as PDF" button (`savePdfBtn`) was removed again 2026-09 at the user's request** — the guaranteed-filename `html2pdf` export it drove, plus its now-unused `<script src>` for `assets/js/vendor/html2pdf.bundle.min.js`, are both gone from this page; **Print Preview** (`printInvoice()`, below) is the only export path left here. **`modules/shared/receipt.php`'s own, separate Save as PDF button was removed too, in a later pass the same month** — see the dedicated paragraph below (a real, patient-reported two-page overflow bug, not a stylistic follow-up).

   **The live editable screen above and the printed/saved-PDF output are two separate renders (2026-09), on purpose.** The user asked to "copy the format" of a reference invoice screenshot (a different clinic's document) — first tried as a redesign of the live editable form itself, then explicitly corrected: *"no revert it back, what i want is when i click the print preview and save pdf that format will be the one save as pdf not the billing"*. So `#invRoot` (everything described in the two paragraphs above — the centered "SERVICE INVOICE" letterhead, Qty/Unit/Description/Unit Cost/Amount table, Total Sales/Total Due/Total Amount Due labels) is what the cashier always works in, unchanged from before this reformat. A second, normally-hidden sibling node, **`#invPrintRoot`** (`hidden`, same `.invoice-sheet` class so it picks up the same CSS), sits right next to it in the markup. **`buildPrintDoc()`** in the page script builds the reference-format document; every value in its output is plain text (`<div>`, not `<input>`) — there is nothing to flatten for print, unlike the old form. `printInvoice()` calls `g('invPrintRoot').innerHTML = buildPrintDoc()` immediately before the existing filename-title trick and `window.print()`. `includes/invoice_style.php`'s `@media print` block opens with `#invRoot { display: none !important; } #invPrintRoot { display: flex !important; flex-direction: column; }` — the live form is never what's printed or saved as PDF, regardless of its own on-screen state (editing, paid, whatever); the print-only doc built fresh from it is. **Crucially, `buildPrintDoc()` reads its values live off the `#invRoot` form** (`collectItems()` for the line items; `g('t_sales').textContent` etc. for the totals after calling `recalc()`; `g('f_from').value` etc. for the patient fields) rather than re-fetching from the server — so **Print Preview always reflects whatever is currently on screen, including unsaved edits**, exactly like the old single-render `printInvoice()` used to (a hard requirement carried over, not dropped).

   **The reference-format document itself was matched to a second, more detailed reference screenshot in a follow-up pass (2026-09), tightening several things a first attempt got only approximately right:** `@page` is now **`portrait`** (`includes/invoice_style.php`, was `landscape` — the reference is a portrait document and, once the payment-detail column below was dropped, there was no more wide content that needed landscape width). `.inv-topgrid` header: round `dgt_logo.jpg` mark + `CLINIC_NAME`/`CLINIC_LINES` + a phone/e-mail line (hard-coded `09764484252` / `customerservice@dgtonedentalph.com`, matching `modules/patient/services.php`'s hero banner) on the left; on the right, an "INVOICE" title over a **borderless** `.inv-infobox` (no box/background at all — a plain right-aligned stack of label/value rows, matching the reference exactly; the first attempt had wrapped it in a bordered card) listing **Status** (a colour-coded pill — PAID green / PARTIALLY PAID amber / UNPAID red, `statusPill()`), **Invoice ID** (`INV-` + zero-padded `payment_id`), **Invoice Date**, **Due Date** (always the same value as Invoice Date — DGT has no credit terms, payment is due at time of service, but the reference's field is kept for a faithful layout match), and **Reference** (`Dr. <dentist_name>`, from `appointmentDetail()`'s existing join — the reference screenshot's "Reference: Dr. …" row maps naturally onto the attending dentist). Invoice Date/Due Date print as **"21 September 2026"** (full month name, `fmtLongDate()` in the JS / `date('j F Y')` in `receipt.php`) rather than the rest of the app's usual "Sep 21, 2026" style, matching the reference precisely; the basis date is `a.payment_date` (falling back to today for a still-unpaid bill), same as the old form's own Payment Date field used. Below the header: the **"Invoice To"** block (patient name + address only — no TIN field here; the clinic's own registration TIN prints in the letterhead above instead, see the header note above) + a separate "The sum of … pesos (₱…)" line, then the line-items table reordered to **Item · Qty · Unit Price (₱) · Amount (₱)** (`printRowHtml()` — the visible **Unit** column is dropped here only; the live form's own table still shows it, and `invoice_items.unit` still round-trips to the DB either way). **The whole "Payment Type / Form of Payment / GCash·Bank·Split reference numbers / Payment Date / Total Amount / Sr. Citizen TIN / OSCA-PWD ID" left column that a first attempt still showed beside the totals is now gone entirely** — the reference screenshot has no such column, and that information is already fully conveyed by the Transactions table below (its Method column shows Cash/GCash/Bank per transaction; Sr. Citizen TIN / OSCA-PWD ID / GCash-Bank reference numbers stay visible on the cashier's own live `#invRoot` screen, they just aren't printed on the customer-facing document any more). The totals panel now sits **alone, right-aligned** — `.inv-totals` is wrapped in a new **`.inv-totals-wrap`** (`display:flex; justify-content:flex-end`) instead of living beside a `.inv-split` sibling column — labels unchanged (**Subtotal Before Discount / Total / Amount Due**, vs. the live form's Total Sales/Total Due/Total Amount Due — same numbers, just different words). The **Transactions** history table gained a **Time** column and dropped the **Description**-labelled column in favour of **Type**, matching the reference's exact column set: **Date · Time · Type · Amount (₱) · Method** (was Date · Description · Method · Amount) — `txnRows(a)` now returns a `time` field too (`fmtClockTime()` in JS / `date('g:i A', ...)` in PHP, e.g. "4:21 PM"; a row sourced from `payment_date`, which is a bare `DATE` with no time component, shows "—" for time), and the Date column itself uses the reference's abbreviated style "21 Sep 2026" (`fmtShortDate()` / `date('j M Y', ...)`) rather than the header's spelled-out "21 September 2026". Signature lines, "Thanks for visiting us!", and the disclaimer are unchanged. **Pay** → `payments.php?action=settle` (now also takes `line_items[]`, which become the invoice of record — persisted to `invoice_items`, and drive `payments.total_amount`), then the invoice re-fetches and re-renders `#invRoot` read-only **before** the auto-print fires (not on a blind timer) — this calls `printInvoice()` itself, which rebuilds `#invPrintRoot` from the now-settled form, so the auto-triggered print after Pay shows the finished reference-format receipt exactly like a manual Print Preview would. The pre-existing `@media print` rules that flatten `<input>`/`<select>`/`<textarea>` into borderless plain text, keep `.inv-grid2`/`.inv-split` as on-screen grids, etc. still exist and still matter for **`#invRoot`'s own descendants in the DOM** even though it's hidden — because `modules/shared/receipt.php` (below) reuses the exact same `.inv-*` classes and `<input readonly>` fields for its own single, always-printed render, so those rules can't be deleted; they simply have no visible effect on `#invPrintRoot`, which never contains a form control to flatten. `.invoice-sheet` no longer forces `page-break-inside: avoid` on itself in print (dropped when moving off a forced-one-landscape-page design) — a long enough bill now paginates naturally onto a second portrait page rather than being crushed to fit one. **Print Preview's suggested Save-As filename (2026-09)** — `printInvoice()` sets `document.title` to `LastName,FirstName_MM-DD-YY` (today's date, from `invoiceFilename()`) right before calling `window.print()` (two `requestAnimationFrame`s deep, so the title change has actually committed before the dialog reads it), then restores the real title once `afterprint` fires (a 10s `setTimeout` backstop covers a print path where `afterprint` never fires). This only works when the person picks Chrome's own **Save as PDF** destination in the print dialog — a real printer driver (**Microsoft Print to PDF** included) is a completely different Chrome code path that hands the job to the Windows print spooler, and Chrome does not forward `document.title` into that driver's own Save dialog. That's a Chromium/Windows platform limitation, not a race condition, so no client-side timing fix can reach it — this screen no longer has a guaranteed-filename fallback for that case (the `savePdfBtn`/`savePdf()` `html2pdf` export that used to cover it was removed 2026-09, see above), so on a real printer driver the download simply keeps whatever default filename that driver's own Save dialog suggests. **`render()` (the live `#invRoot` form) restores the payment state from the persisted row** — it checks the Form-of-Payment radio for `payment_mode` (not a hardcoded Cash), fills the GCash Reference, Bank Reference, and Split per-method amounts from the `pay.*` columns, sets Payment Date from `payment_date`, and calls `syncPayPanels()` so only the used method's reference block shows on `#invRoot` itself — works for cash / gcash / bank / split, and on that settled render the "The sum of" / pesos / Total Amount / Total Amount Due fields show the **billable** figure (Total Due − reservation credit) while Received Amount / Change show `received_amount` / `change_amount`, feeding the same figures into `buildPrintDoc()`. Installment → `partially_paid`. `recomputePayment()` still derives status (reservation credit alone stays `unpaid`).

   **`modules/shared/receipt.php`** (`$orNo` still computed as `OR-000000` for the `<title>`/filename only; `checkAuth()` + patient-ownership guard) is a **read-only render of the settled bill** driven by the persisted `payments` row — reachable directly (the **Invoice** button on the patient dashboard's Completed Treatments table, or by URL), so it has no live-editable form to keep separate from its print output the way `billing.php` does: **its one and only render already uses the reference-format layout, on screen and when printed/saved as PDF alike**, kept in lock-step with `billing.php`'s `buildPrintDoc()`. All fields are static `readonly`; the controls are **Close** and **Print Preview**.

   **The page's own separate `savePdfBtn`/`savePdf()` (an `html2pdf` export, independent of the cashier's — see two paragraphs up) was removed outright 2026-09, after a patient reported the downloaded PDF splitting onto two pages** — page 1 held everything through the Transactions table, page 2 held nothing but the signature line, with a large blank gap between the two. Root cause: `includes/invoice_style.php`'s print CSS pins the "Thanks for visiting us!" footer to the bottom of the page via a `display:flex; flex-direction:column` `.invoice-sheet` forced to `min-height: 100vh` plus `.inv-footer-thanks { margin-top: auto }` — a standard "glue this to the page bottom" trick that only means what it says under a **real** paginated print engine, where `100vh` genuinely equals one physical A5 page (exactly how the cashier Billing screen's own **Print Preview**, `window.print()`, already used it correctly). `savePdf()` didn't print at all, though — it rendered via `html2canvas` (screenshotting the live DOM) and only *promoted* the `@media print` rules into an unconditional stylesheet on the cloned document afterward, so `100vh` resolved against the ambient **browser viewport** instead of any physical page — producing a canvas taller than one A5 page's worth of content, which `html2pdf`'s pagination then sliced into two, landing the auto-margin-pushed footer on its own near-empty second page. **Fix: stop trying to maintain a second, fragile html2pdf export path and make this page work exactly like the cashier's already-correct one** — `savePdfBtn`, `savePdf()`, and the now-unused `<script src>` for `assets/js/vendor/html2pdf.bundle.min.js` were deleted outright; **Print Preview** (still `printInvoice()`, the same `document.title` filename trick, unchanged) is now the only export path, and a patient saves the PDF the same way the cashier does — by picking Chrome's own "Save as PDF" destination in the native print dialog. Verified with a real headless-Chrome CDP session (`Network.setCookie` to carry a real authenticated session, `Page.printToPDF` against a real settled invoice) that the output is genuinely a single PDF page (`/Type/Page` object count = 1) — not just visually inspected.

   **Payment input fields simplified across all three payment-collection surfaces, and the static TIN line dropped from both printed templates (2026-09, an instructor feedback directive pasted verbatim: "Bank Transfer Input Cleanup," "GCash & Cashier Billing Input Cleanup," "Printable Invoice & Receipt Cleanup").** Three changes, each scoped exactly to what the directive named:
   - **Bank Transfer: "Bank / Branch" and "Account No." removed everywhere, Bank Reference No. is the sole identifier.** `modules/patient/appointments.php`'s `payPanel('bank')` dropped the `#p_bbranch`/`#p_bacct` inputs (and their `validatePayment()` checks, submit-payload keys, and red-highlight-clear wiring), leaving only `#p_ref` under the existing bank-instructions/account-display block (that display block — the *clinic's own* receiving bank/account/name, parsed from the `pay_bank_details` setting — is informational, not a patient input field, and was correctly left alone; the directive is about fields the payer fills in, not what they're told to send to). `modules/cashier/billing.php`'s `#pm_bank` panel dropped `#f_bbranch`/`#f_bacct` the same way (markup, `validatePayMode()`, the `wire()` red-highlight map, and the `markPaid()` payload), leaving a single `.inv-field` wrapping just `#f_bref`. Server-side, `api/appointments.php action=create` no longer collects/validates `bank_branch`/`bank_account` (still validates the reference: exactly the same `/^[A-Za-z0-9]{10,20}$/` rule as before) and no longer writes those two columns into `INSERT INTO payments`; `api/payments.php action=settle` likewise dropped its `mb_strlen($bankBranch) < 3` / `preg_match('/^\d{8,16}$/', $bankAccount)` checks and now always passes through the row's *existing* `bank_branch`/`bank_account` value (effectively `null` going forward) in its `UPDATE payments` call rather than trying to write a client-supplied one — the two DB columns themselves were **not** dropped (a schema change wasn't asked for; they just stop being populated with new data). The now-unreachable `action=counter_add` path (the old over-the-counter modal with no UI, see the Discounts/Transaction-page paragraphs) still has its own separate, untouched `bank_branch`/`bank_account` handling — out of scope since the directive named "Online Booking & Cashier Billing," not that dead flow.
   - **GCash: the "GCash Number" input removed from Cashier Billing, GCash Reference No. is the sole identifier there too.** `modules/cashier/billing.php`'s `#pm_gcash` panel dropped `#f_gnum` (markup, its `wirePhoneField()` wiring, the pre-Pay PH-mobile gate, and the `markPaid()` payload key) — the Walk-In modal already had no such field (removed in an earlier same-day pass, see above), and the online wizard never had one to begin with (a patient's own GCash number was never asked there; only the clinic's receiving number is shown, as static display text, same non-field exception as the bank account block). `api/payments.php action=settle` dropped its `$gcashNum`/`isPhMobile()` capture+validation and — like the bank columns — now always passes through the existing `gcash_number` value rather than writing a new one.
   - **Printed templates: the static clinic TIN line removed.** `modules/shared/receipt.php`'s `$clinicTopLines` and `modules/cashier/billing.php`'s `CLINIC_TOP_LINES` both dropped the `'NON-VAT Reg. TIN: 482-915-307-00000'` entry, leaving just `'Raffy D.G. Tamayo - Prop.'` before the address lines — both arrays are rendered by a plain `foreach`/`.map()`, so removing one entry needed no other markup change. The other two items in this cleanup section turned out to need **no code change at all**, confirmed by direct grep/read rather than assumed from memory: the "Notes / Remarks" box was already fully removed in an earlier 2026-09 pass (the A5-monochrome-redesign paragraph below), and neither printed document (`receipt.php`, `billing.php`'s `buildPrintDoc()`) has ever rendered sender account numbers or bank-branch details — that information only ever lived on the live, screen-only `#invRoot` form (which a prior redesign pass had already excluded from the printed/PDF output entirely). The live `#invRoot` screen's own **unrelated** "with TIN (optional)" field (the *customer's* own TIN, for their records) and the **Sr. Citizen TIN** SC/PWD-discount field are both untouched — the directive's "static TIN" wording is specifically the clinic's own fixed letterhead line, not a payer-entered value.
   - Verified end-to-end with headless Chrome across all three surfaces: the online booking wizard's Bank panel now renders exactly one input (`hasBranch:false, hasAcct:false, hasRef:true`); Cashier Billing's GCash panel renders exactly one input (`hasGnum:false, hasGref:true`) and its Bank panel renders exactly one input (`hasBbranch:false, hasBacct:false, hasBref:true`); a full `action=settle` payment on a disposable test appointment, paid via Bank Transfer with only a reference number, completed successfully and persisted `bank_reference` set with `bank_branch`/`bank_account`/`gcash_number` all `NULL`; the resulting printed invoice (both the cashier's auto-triggered Print Preview and the patient-facing `receipt.php`) contains the string `"TIN"` nowhere on the page. Test appointment/payment/line-item rows were deleted afterward; two demo accounts (`reception.demo`, `cashier.demo`) that turned out to be deactivated mid-verification were reactivated only for `reception.demo` (an earlier, explicitly user-approved standing change) — `cashier.demo` was restored back to its original deactivated state once this verification finished, since reactivating it permanently was never asked for.
   - **Corrected the same day: the clinic's own registration TIN line is back — the instructor's "static TIN" feedback was actually about the payer-entered "with TIN" field on the cashier Billing screen, not the clinic's letterhead line.** The user clarified this directly, pointing at a screenshot of `#invRoot`'s own "with TIN (optional)" field (`#f_tin`, sitting between "Received from" and "Phone Number", right above "The sum of") as "the one its talking about." `'NON-VAT Reg. TIN: 482-915-307-00000'` is back in both `modules/cashier/billing.php`'s `CLINIC_TOP_LINES` and `modules/shared/receipt.php`'s `$clinicTopLines` (as a second entry, right after `'Raffy D.G. Tamayo - Prop.'`), so it prints on both templates again exactly as before this cleanup pass first touched it. The `#f_tin` "with TIN (optional)" input — a free-text field for the *customer's* own TIN, never persisted to the DB and never rendered in `buildPrintDoc()`/`receipt.php` even before this correction — was removed outright instead (it had exactly one reference in `billing.php`, so nothing else needed updating); `#invRoot`'s payment-details grid now runs Date / Received from / Phone Number / The sum of / pesos — 5 fields instead of 6, so the trailing cell in the 2-column `.inv-grid2` grid simply sits empty on the last row. Nothing about the printed output changes from removing `#f_tin`, since that field was screen-only decoration to begin with.

   **A5-portrait redesign, twice in the same day (2026-09), replacing the reference-format layout described in the two paragraphs above — first a colored version, then a strict monochrome one that superseded it a few hours later.** Both passes were the user pasting a detailed AI dev-prompt spec (DOMPDF/table-based-layout terms, per the standing `requests-framed-as-react` convention — this app has no DOMPDF, so each spec's *intent* was translated to the existing vanilla-CSS `buildPrintDoc()`/`receipt.php` pair, not adopted literally) plus a reference receipt screenshot. The first pass landed a colored design (light-gray boxed panels, a green/amber/red "PAID IN FULL" pill, a separate bottom "Payment Breakdown & Status" box) with A5 sizing confirmed via `AskUserQuestion`. The **second pass explicitly replaced that first one** — confirmed via `AskUserQuestion` two ways: (1) full monochrome replacement vs. keeping the colored version and only touching one element — the user picked full replacement; (2) whether to collapse the real SC/PWD-discount / promo / Doctor's-Fee / Withholding-Tax rows down to the new spec's generic single "Less Custom Discount/Deposit" line — the user picked keeping every real row itemized by name, just restyled in black and white. What follows is the **final, current state** — the colored intermediate design is gone, not layered underneath.
   - **Strictly monochrome, no exceptions.** `includes/invoice_style.php` gained a new `.inv-mono` class (added to `billing.php`'s `#invPrintRoot` and `receipt.php`'s top `.invoice-sheet` wrapper — never to `#invRoot`) that overrides every colored fill the shared `.inv-lineitems`/`.inv-totals`/`.or-row.rec` classes normally carry on `#invRoot`'s own on-screen editable screen (light-gray header/box backgrounds, a light-blue "record only" row highlight) down to `background: none` + black borders, scoped so `#invRoot`'s own look is completely untouched. Every print-doc-exclusive class (`.inv-topgrid`, `.inv-infobox`, `.inv-to`, `.inv-txns`, `.inv-sign2`, `.inv-footer-thanks`, …) had its navy/steel-blue text colors (`#16263c`/`#48607a`) replaced with pure black (`#000`) or neutral gray (`#555`, no hue) directly, since those classes were already print-doc-exclusive and safe to edit in place. The status tag (`.inv-status-pill`) is now a plain bordered box (`border:1px solid #000`, no fill) with just the wording — **PAID / PARTIALLY PAID / UNPAID** — carrying the meaning, not a color; `statusPill()`'s inline `style="background:…;color:…"` was removed at the source (in both the JS and the fact that `receipt.php` never generated one to begin with), not merely overridden with CSS. Verified with a live DOM walk of every element inside the rendered doc confirming zero non-white/non-transparent `background-color` anywhere.
   - **`@page` margin dropped to `0`; the 10mm whitespace border is real `padding` on `.invoice-sheet` instead** (`box-sizing:border-box`), per the spec's own "margin 0 on the page, 10mm padding on the content" technique — more consistent across print engines than depending on how each one applies an `@page margin`. Re-verified the actual page size is still correct this way: a `Page.printToPDF` capture's `/MediaBox` still came back `148.2mm × 209.9mm`.
   - **Status and Reference Doctor moved back into the header's `.inv-infobox`**, which is now a boxed metadata block (`border-top`/`border-bottom: 1px solid #000`, no fill) listing **Status → Invoice ID → Invoice Date → Due Date → Reference Doctor → PRC Lic. No.** (the PRC license, sourced from the same `appointmentDetail()` join the first pass added, rides along with the doctor's name here rather than living in a separate patient/doctor panel) — undoing the first pass's choice to split Status into a separate bottom box and Reference into a "Patient & Doctor Information" panel. Title reverted from "Sales Invoice" back to plain **"Invoice"**; the first pass's header **Time** field (`nowClock()` et al.) was dropped outright since this spec's own header fields don't include one.
   - **"Invoice To:" is a plain unboxed block again** (`.inv-to`'s light-gray fill/border/radius from the first pass removed — this class is print-doc-exclusive, safe to edit directly) showing **Patient Name / Mobile Number / Patient Address** in a 2-column grid (Address spanning the full width). **Patient Address is new** — `patient_records.address`, fetched by a small added query alongside the existing phone lookup (`api/payments.php`'s `action=invoice`, and directly in `receipt.php`); still **no "Patient ID"** field (the pinned `hide-internal-ids-from-patients` memory rules out a raw/padded internal id on a patient-visible screen — this doc is reachable by the patient via `receipt.php`), and Attending Dentist/PRC License moved to the header box above instead of living here (avoiding showing the doctor's name in two places at once).
   - **Item table headers renamed** to the spec's exact wording — **Item Description / Qty / Unit Price / Amount (₱)** (was "Description / Procedure" / "Qty." / "Unit Price (₱)" / "Total (₱)") — columns still inline-percentage-widthed (`46/12/20/22`, `table-layout:fixed`) on just this table instance, `#invRoot`'s own 6-column live-editable table untouched.
   - **Totals panel folded back into one block, no separate payment-breakdown box.** The first pass's dedicated "Payment Breakdown & Status" box (itemizing Cash Tendered / GCash (Ref) / Bank Transfer (Ref) individually) is gone — per-method detail lives only in the Transactions table's own Method column now, matching this spec's simpler structure. The totals panel is one flow: **Subtotal Before Discount** → the real, itemized SC/PWD / promo "Less:" rows when active (kept exactly, per the user's explicit choice not to collapse them) → the existing record-only Doctor's-Fee/Withholding-Tax rows → **"Less: Reservation Deposit (Paid via GCASH Ref: 900123456)"** (kept from the first pass — `reservationLabel(a)`/PHP's `$reservationLabel`) → **"Total"** (the post-discount, post-reservation billable figure; the first pass's separate "Total After Discounts" checkpoint row was dropped as unneeded now that there's no second box to reconcile against) → **"Less: Amount Paid"** → **"Amount Due"** → **"Change"**.
