(3) **The admin dashboard's "Assistant Dentists on Duty" drill-down gains a "Total On Duty: N" summary line** above the table (`modules/admin/dashboard.php`'s `staffTable()`) — free to compute, since `api/dashboard.php`'s `action=staff_list&key=assistants_on_duty` query is already scoped to on-duty rows only (documented above), so `rows.length` *is* the total; the Mark-off-duty-button removal and the Active Patients / Availability columns the same spec asked for were already exactly in place from the prior pass (re-confirmed by reading the live code, not assumed) and needed no further change. The spec's items on the Assistant Dentist role's own scope — the My Duty Today toggle, self-check-in/undo on their schedule view, and the `receptionist`-equivalent non-clinical records access (`api/records.php`'s `$canClinical = in_array($me['role'], ['dentist','admin'])` already excludes `dental_assistant`) — were all likewise re-confirmed already correct and untouched. Verified end-to-end against the real DB and two more real HTTP calls: a manual pick (`assistant_id` explicitly passed) on a fresh appointment correctly wrote the assistant and inserted exactly one `notifications` row for its linked `user_id`; a simulated identical repeat call correctly computed no new notification. All seeded rows deleted afterward.

**Standardized status badge colours** (`app.js` `BADGE_C`, `modules/admin/logs.php`, `includes/calendar_style.php`, patient `statusOf()`): Pending = yellow (`warn`), Approved = blue (`info`), Completed = green (`ok`), **Rescheduled = fuchsia** (`#a21caf` on `#fae8ff`, 2026-10-11; was gray, too close to Approved blue), Cancelled = red (`danger`). There is **no "Ongoing" badge** (removed 2026-09). `window.badge()` renders a raw status; `window.apptStatusBadge(row)` / `window.opStatus(row)` are kept only as thin aliases that pull `.status` off a row and call `window.badge()`.

**Reschedule** (any role, including a patient acting on their own booking) **requires a reason**, enforced in `api/appointments.php` — it writes `reschedule_reason` and copies the old date into `previous_scheduled_date` before moving it. **`cancel`** (re-added 2026-09 to match the reference doc's "Cancelation / Rescheduling module") also **requires a reason**: a patient may cancel their own `pending`/`approved`/`rescheduled` booking, and staff (receptionist/admin/dentist/cashier) may cancel one on a patient's behalf; it writes `patient_cancellation_reason` **or** `staff_cancellation_reason` (by role) + `cancellation_reason`, flips the row to `cancelled`, and `notify()`s the patient. Cancelling **forfeits** the reservation fee — no refund code, the fee just stays on the never-completing appointment. There is no *lower* time gate on cancel (unlike the patient's day-based reschedule gate), but a **patient cannot reschedule or cancel an appointment whose own date+time has already passed** (both actions 422 for the `patient` role; staff are exempt). **Day-before appointment reminder (migration 048, 2026-09).** `sendUpcomingAppointmentReminders()` runs right next to `expireLapsedPendingAppointments()` (same best-effort, runs on every `action=list` GET, swallows all errors) and notifies every `approved`/`rescheduled` appointment whose `scheduled_date` is exactly tomorrow (`CURDATE() + 1`, DB clock): title **"Appointment reminder"**, message naming the patient, the clinic, the date, and an estimated one-hour time range (`fmtTime()` on the slot and the slot + 1 hour), asking them to arrive on time or use the Appointments page if they can't make it. `appointments.reminder_sent_at DATETIME` is the one-shot guard — the sweep only selects rows where it's still `NULL` and sets it to `NOW()` right after notifying, so the same appointment is never reminded twice no matter how many times `action=list` is hit before the appointment's day arrives. This is in-app only (the existing `notify()` bell) — there is no SMS integration in this app; the reference the user was matching (a real clinic's SMS reminder text) was adapted to the notification bell instead of adding a paid SMS gateway.

**Missed-appointment follow-up (migration 049, 2026-09, the companion notification to the reminder above).** `sendMissedAppointmentNotices()` runs alongside the same two sweeps and notifies once a confirmed appointment's own date has passed with the patient never checked in (`status IN ('approved','rescheduled') AND scheduled_date < CURDATE() AND checked_in_at IS NULL`): title **"Missed appointment"**, message naming the patient and the missed date, pointing them to contact the clinic or book a new appointment (a past appointment's own Reschedule/Cancel are already disabled on the patient's card, so "reschedule this one online" isn't an option — a new booking or a phone call is). **Deliberately excludes any row with `checked_in_at` set** — that means the visit actually happened and is just waiting on the dentist's procedure log, which is a different, already-documented case the front desk resolves by hand (see "Only `pending` is swept" just below); this sweep only fires for a genuine no-show. `appointments.missed_notified_at DATETIME` is the one-shot guard, same pattern as `reminder_sent_at`. The migration also **backfills `missed_notified_at = NOW()` for every already-qualifying row on the existing install** at the moment it's applied, so turning this feature on doesn't suddenly blast out notifications for old, already-resolved history — only appointments that become overdue-and-never-checked-in *after* the migration runs will ever notify. Like the reminder above, this is in-app only, adapted from a real clinic's SMS follow-up text the user referenced — no SMS gateway.

**Lapsed `pending` requests auto-expire:** `expireLapsedPendingAppointments()` (best-effort, runs on every `action=list` GET, swallows all errors — same spirit as `touchPresence()`) flips every `status='pending'` row with `scheduled_date < CURDATE()` to `cancelled` with reason "This request expired because it was not approved before the requested date." and notifies the patient. **Only `pending` is swept** — a past `approved`/`rescheduled` row may be a visit that happened and is only waiting on the dentist's procedure log, so the front desk resolves those by hand. `decline` (receptionist rejecting a still-*pending* request, reason required) is separate — it writes `decline_reason` + `staff_cancellation_reason` + `cancellation_reason` and also flips the row to `cancelled`. The receptionist's Approved Appointments table has **Reschedule** and **Cancel** buttons; the patient Appointments card has **Reschedule** (24 h-gated) and **Cancel**. **Both Reschedule modals embed a full month-grid calendar picker (2026-09, revised from an earlier plain-`<select>`-with-chips version the same day)** — a literal reuse of the patient Calendar View's `.pcal-*` widget (`includes/patient_calendar_style.php`, required into both `modules/patient/appointments.php` and `modules/receptionist/dashboard.php`) inside the modal instead of a date input: a prev/next month grid where each day is plain green (`open`), plain red (`full`), or hatched (`closed`, e.g. Sunday/blocked, still labelled "Closed") — the intermediate amber "few left" tier and the "Fully booked"/"N slots open"/"Full Schedule" text tags were all dropped 2026-09 (simplified at the user's request down to colour-only for open/full, applied identically to the full Calendar View page, not just these modals); clicking a day reveals a day-detail panel of time-slot buttons showing just the time (free, clickable) or "Full" (muted, inert) — **the per-slot "N of 5 open" chair-count label under each time was removed the same day** (found confusing sitting directly under a specific time-slot button; see the Calendar View paragraph above for the full explanation) — `.pcal-slot` carries a `min-height` so a one-line free slot and two-line taken slot stay the same height. The dialog itself is widened to `min(1040px, 100%-32px)` (via a `#modalRoot > [role="dialog"]:has(.rs-pcal)` override, the same "widen a specific modal" technique `records_chart_style.php` already used) and the cells/legend given a lighter `.rs-pcal` trim rather than a hard shrink (an initial pass compacted both aggressively and read as too cramped). **Picking a slot fills it solid green** (the same colour its own hover state uses) via `.selected-slot`, not just an outline, so a chosen time clearly reads as "picked" rather than merely "hovered once". Both modals call the day-range **`api/appointments.php?action=availability&from=&to=&dentist_id=<the appointment's own dentist>`** — the endpoint was extended 2026-09 with an optional `&dentist_id=` that swaps in that dentist's own working grid/absences instead of always defaulting to `ownerDentistId()`, since a reschedule can belong to a roaming dentist with a different working week than the online-booking Owner. Passing `dentist_id` also drops the response's `past` floor from "today + 2" (the online-booking lead time, still the default when `dentist_id` is omitted, e.g. the patient Calendar View's own call) down to plain "today" (DB clock) — a reschedule has no such lead time; the client additionally applies its own floor on top (`todayISO(1)` for a patient's own reschedule — "tomorrow onward" — vs plain `todayISO()` for staff, who may reschedule to the same day), mirroring the dual-layer defensive-floor pattern the Calendar View page itself already uses. The chosen date/slot are tracked in hidden `rs_date`/`rs_slot` (patient) or `r_date`/`r_slot` (receptionist) inputs, filled by the calendar's day-panel click handler rather than a visible `<input type="date">` or `<select>`.

**The same calendar-grid picker now drives every date/time field in the app (2026-09)** — previously only the two Reschedule modals and the read-only Calendar View page had it; the patient's own **"+ Request Appointment" wizard (step 1, "Preferred Date & Time")** and the receptionist's **Walk-in Booking modal ("Date & Time")** used a plain `<input type="date">` + `<select>` pair until the user asked for the same green/red visual concept everywhere ("get the concept of the reschedule... make it green... red if its full... add this on appointment calendar and same for walk in n online"). Both now embed `reschedCalHtml()` behind hidden `b_date`/`b_slot` (patient wizard) or `w_date`/`w_slot` (walk-in) inputs, reusing each file's own already-defined `wireReschedCal(dentistId, minDateKey, onPick[, preselect])` — no new widget was built. The three call sites differ only in what they pass:
- **Patient wizard:** `wireReschedCal(null, todayISO(2), onPick, preselect)` — no `dentist_id` (online bookings all go to the Owner), so `action=availability` applies its default "today + 2 days" lead-time floor, matching `action=create`'s own rule. A 4th `preselect` argument (`{date, slot}`, new — only `patient/appointments.php`'s copy of `wireReschedCal` has it) lets a prefill coming from Calendar View's "book this slot" flow (`sessionStorage dgt_book_prefill`) open the grid already on that month with the day panel expanded and the requested slot clicked programmatically — or, if that slot filled in the meantime, the day's first still-open slot instead, mirroring what the old `fillSlotOptions()` re-validation used to do.
- **Walk-in modal:** `wireReschedCal(ownerId, todayISO(), onPick)` — `dentists.find(d => d.doctor_type === 'owner').user_id` is passed as `dentist_id` specifically so the floor drops to plain "today" (same-day walk-ins are normal) while still pulling the Owner's real working grid, matching what `action=slots` already did for the old `<select>`.
- **Reschedule modals:** unchanged — `wireReschedCal(appt.dentist_id, todayISO() or todayISO(1), onPick)`.

The old per-page helpers this replaced — `fillSlotOptions()` / `wireClinicDate()` on the patient wizard's date input, `fillWalkinSlotOptions()` / `slotField()` on the walk-in modal — were deleted outright rather than left dead, along with the now-unused `SLOTS` array/fetch in both files (the calendar widget gets its slot list from `action=availability`'s own response, not a client-side default array).

The shared `appointmentModal()` in `app.js` still shows a single `.alert-warn` callout for a cancelled/rescheduled appointment — it names who ("by the patient" / "by the clinic") and carries the reason inline (labelled **Patient's reason** / **Receptionist's reason** / **Reschedule reason**); there is no separate detail row. Original→new dates still show as rows for a reschedule. The operational reason never leaks into the dentist queue's CONCERN column — that stays the patient's dental concern.

**Cancel and Decline are both two-step confirmations (2026-09)** — the user asked for a second "are you really sure" popup that spells out the consequence before either action actually fires, rather than the reason-entry modal's own confirm button being the point of no return. All three call sites (`patient/appointments.php`'s `cancelModal`; `receptionist/dashboard.php`'s `cancelModal` and `declineModal`) follow the same shape: **step 1** is the existing reason-entry modal, unchanged in appearance — its confirm button (`cx_go` / `c_go` / `d_go`) no longer calls the API directly, it validates the reason is non-empty and opens **step 2**, a second modal titled "Are You Absolutely Sure?" / "Are You Sure?" with a red `window.alertCls('error')` bullet list of exactly what confirming will do (cancel/decline immediately, forfeit the reservation fee — worded as *the patient's* fee on the two staff-facing modals, *your* fee on the patient's own — and, for Cancel, free up the slot; for Decline, remove the request from the queue and require a fresh submission) plus the reason typed in step 1 echoed back for a final look. Step 2's two buttons are **No, Go Back** (re-opens step 1 via `cancelModal(id, reason)` / `declineModal(id, reason)` — both functions now take an optional second `prevReason` argument so the typed reason survives the round-trip instead of being lost) and the actual danger button (**Yes, I'm Sure — Cancel It** / **Yes, Cancel It** / **Yes, Decline It**), which is the only thing that calls `action=cancel` / `action=decline`. Nothing about the reschedule flow changed — only Cancel and Decline gained the extra step.
`payments.payment_status`: `unpaid` / `partially_paid` / `fully_paid` (derived by `recomputePayment()` — `partially_paid` requires cash/GCash/bank collected, not just the reservation credit). **Exception (2026-10-07):** when the reservation fee (plus discounts) already covers the whole bill, the cashier presses Pay with ₱0 received; `settle` accepts it (no reference check, a second Pay is refused) and stamps `payment_date`, and `recomputePayment()` treats a non-null `payment_date` as "the cashier processed it", so the bill closes as `fully_paid`. The shared View modal's Billing block and the dentist Recently Completed table render this via `billingBadge()` / `BILLING_BADGE` as **Sent to Cashier** (amber) / **Partial Payment** (amber) / **Paid** (green).

### On-call decline workflow & structured notifications (migration 062, 2026-10)

Key notes only:
- **Schema:** `notifications.appointment_id` + `type` (`oncall_assigned` / `oncall_declined` / `appointment_rescheduled`); `appointments.oncall_declined_by` / `_at` / `_reason`. `notify($uid, $title, $msg, $apptId = null, $type = null)`. `api/notifications.php action=list` joins each row to its appointment's live details.
- **Decline** (`api/appointments.php action=decline_oncall`, roaming dentist, own approved/rescheduled visit, not checked in, reason required): sets `dentist_id = NULL`, keeps the status, notifies every active receptionist, writes a History Log line. Rescheduling an unassigned visit auto-routes and never goes back to the dentist who declined.
- **Patient:** every reschedule sends an "Appointment Rescheduled" in-app notification only. The staff-reschedule e-mail was turned off 2026-10-09 at the user's request (`emailPatientRescheduled()` is kept but not called).
- **UI:** every bell and notifications page (patient, roaming dentist, receptionist) uses clickable rows, `window.notifRow()`, which open a pop-up (`window.notifPopup()`) holding the full message and the action buttons (View Details / Decline, Reschedule / Reassign, View Appointment). There is no Open button. Patient rows show the title only. Rows that still need action sort to the top (`window.notifSortOpenFirst()`).
- **Removed:** the dentist dashboard's On-Call card, and the receptionist dashboard's Declined On-Call section and Assistant Dentists on Duty card. The receptionist bell (`#rcpBellBtn`) is the fourth exception to the patient-only bell rule.
- **Assistant Dentist (2026-10-07):** "New Patient Assigned" notifications now carry `appointment_id` + type `assistant_assigned`; the assistant bell and Patient Assignments page use the same clickable rows + pop-up (Patient / Date / Time / Service / Dentist / how assigned, View Details). Older rows with no linked visit open as a plain message.
- Example data for patient rex.carbonel is tracked in the `example_oncall_appt_ids` setting.

**Date of Birth instead of a typed Age (2026-10-08).** No form asks for an age any more. Patient age is always computed from `patient_records.date_of_birth` (`TIMESTAMPDIFF(YEAR, …, CURDATE())`):
- **Forms:** admin Add/Edit Patient (required; `api/users.php` upserts `patient_records`, `list`/`get` return `date_of_birth` + `age`; the Patients table has an Age column), dentist/receptionist **+ Create Record** (required), the receptionist Patient Details edit (now "Edit Patient Info", DOB editable by the front desk), the walk-in modal (DOB locked when on file, else entered and saved), and the booking wizard (self: locked computed Age, or a DOB input when the account has none; "someone else, new person": DOB).
- **Picker:** every one of those Date of Birth fields is the register page's **Month / Day / Year** dropdown trio, not a date input: `window.dobSelectsHtml(id, value, inputCls)` + `wireDobSelects(id)` / `setDobSelects(id, value)` / `lockDobSelects(id, on)` in `app.js`. A hidden `#id` holds `YYYY-MM-DD` ('' while incomplete or future); the Day list trims to the month; years run this year back 120 (any age, unlike register's 18+). With `{ swap: true }` (admin Edit Patient, walk-in, receptionist + dentist Patient Details), a birthdate already on file shows as a locked **Age** box ("35 years old", label reads "Age") instead of the dropdowns; a **Change** link brings the dropdowns back (hidden while locked: walk-in on-file, Patient Details until Edit). `ageFromDob()`, `dobShowsAge()` live next to it.
- **Server:** `validBirthDate()` / `ageFromBirthDate()` (`includes/functions.php`); `patientAgeFromRecord()` in `api/appointments.php` derives the booking age for `create` (self) and `walk_in`, ignoring any typed `patient_age`; `createUnlinkedPatientRecord()` takes `date_of_birth`.
- **Display:** `APPT_AGE_SQL` (`api/_bootstrap.php`) computes the live age in `appointmentDetail()`, the appointment `list` (Approval queue, dentist Queue) and `api/reports.php` logs. `appointments.patient_age` is still written as a snapshot, but it is only a fallback for an old "someone else" booking with no linked record.

**Dentist-scoped calendar fix (2026-10-08).** `action=availability&dentist_id=` now builds each day's slots with `dentistBookableSlots()` (absences + the roaming "every slot" rule) instead of reading `dentist_schedules` directly, so a roaming dentist's stale grid rows no longer close hours in the reschedule / walk-in calendars. In all three calendar day panels, a slot the dentist doesn't work reads **Unavailable**, not "Occupied" (Occupied = actually booked full).

### Payment screenshot toggle (migration 063, 2026-10)

Admin Settings → **Payment Settings** has a switch, "Require Payment Screenshot Upload for Online Payments" (`settings.enable_receipt_validation`, default `'1'`, saved via `api/settings.php action=save_receipt_validation`, returned by `action=payment_info`). On: online GCash / Bank bookings and payment resubmissions need the reference number **and** a screenshot. Off: only the reference number is required and the screenshot upload is hidden entirely (booking wizard and Resubmit Payment), and `validateReservationPayment()` in `api/appointments.php` accepts no file (stores `reservation_receipt = NULL`). The receptionist's approval queue shows "No screenshot / Verify Ref. No." for such a booking; the Ref. No. column is what staff check.

### Responsive pass (2026-10-04)

Supersedes the "820px" mentions above: the `nav:` breakpoint is now **1280px** (raised from 1024 the same day so an iPad Pro in portrait, 1024px wide, also gets the drawer, at the user's request), so phones *and* tablets (< 1280) get the off-canvas sidebar drawer (backdrop `#sbBackdrop` in `layout.php`, Escape / backdrop click close it, `aria-expanded` on `#menuToggle`), and the desktop collapse rule is guarded to `min-width: 1280px`. Tablets held upright (768-1279, portrait) get square calendar cells on the patient Calendar View so it fills the tall screen. `tailwind.php`'s "Responsive hardening" block: `html, body { overflow-x: clip }` (wide tables scroll inside their own `overflow-x-auto` card, never the page), `.min-h-screen` uses `100dvh`, `label:has(> input.sr-only)` is `relative` (the Auto-refresh switch's sr-only checkbox inside a scrolling filter row used to widen History Log by ~670px), and **44px tap targets on `pointer: coarse` only** (desktop keeps compact row buttons; calendar cells exempt). `<main>` gutter is `px-4 sm:px-5 lg:px-7`. Grids that must not grow past the screen use `minmax(0,1fr)` (Settings grid, `.cal-layout`, `.cal-grid`, `.pcal-grid`); calendars hide the day tag and shrink cells below 640px. `index.php` has a burger (`#navMenuBtn`) + right-side drawer (`#navDrawer`) below 1024px; the horizontal links show from `lg:`, and the Sign Up / Log out pill moves into the drawer below `sm:`. Audited with headless Chrome (touch emulation) at 320 / 375 / 768 / 1024 / 1440 on every role's pages: zero horizontal page overflow. **Booking calendar restyle:** `.pcal-*` cells are 1:1 squares with a gap and soft fills (Available emerald, Fully Booked rose, Closed slate + struck-through date, Selected blue border), dot legend. The patient Calendar View (`modules/patient/calendar.php`) is one `.pcal-shell` card: phones = full width, compact 1:1 squares; `sm:` (640-767) = centred `max-w-md`; `md:` (tablets, 768+) = full-width card, big 4:3 cells with Closed / Fully Booked labels, the time slots below in 3 columns; `xl:` (1280+, room left after the sidebar) = full-width 12-column split, calendar `col-span-8` with 16:10 cells, "Selected Date" time list `col-span-4`, with a hint until a date is picked. (The user found a capped `max-w-4xl` card too small with too much empty space on desktop/iPad.) **Patient Treatment & Billing** (`modules/patient/dashboard.php`): below 1280px (phones + every iPad) the history table is replaced by a card list (`#historyCards`, 1 column, 2 from `md:`), each card = date/time + total header, service title, patient · dentist, balance bar, Dentist's notes sub-box, status + View/Receipt; the table shows from `xl:`. The three summary tiles are a compact 2-up grid on phones (Outstanding spans the row, icons hidden), 3-up from `sm:`.

### Walk-in actions & No Show (migration 064, 2026-10)

- **Update 2026-10-06: walk-ins skip the approval queue.** The Walk-in Booking modal has an **Assign Dentist** picker, filled after a date + time is picked (`dutyMapFor()`: off-duty / already-full dentists disabled, in-house default). `action=walk_in` requires `dentist_id`, runs `assertDentistOnDuty()` + `assertDentistNotOverloaded()`, inserts the visit as **`approved`** (not `pending`) and calls `notifyRoamingDentistAssigned()`. So Appointments for Approval is in practice online-only, and walk-ins no longer count on the admin's Pending Schedule card. This supersedes "walk-ins enter the queue as pending" elsewhere in this file. The walk-in picker follows the online waterfall: a roaming dentist stays disabled (no label text) until every on-duty in-house dentist has `slotCapacity()-1` bookings that hour (`roamingLockedBy()` in `api/appointments.php`, `roaming_locked` on `action=dentists`, re-checked in `walk_in`). Approve / Reassign are not gated by this. The walk-in Approve / Reschedule branch in Appointments for Approval was then removed (no pending walk-ins existed locally or live): every row there shows Approve + Decline. Walk-ins use the Approved Appointments actions instead.

- **Appointments for Approval:** Decline shows only on online rows (it is the payment decline, `decline_payment`). A walk-in row shows **Approve** + **Reschedule** instead; `api/appointments.php action=decline` 422s a walk-in. The old full-cancel decline modal was removed.
- **Missed visits can be rescheduled by the patient (2026-10-12).** A `no_show` visit, or an approved/rescheduled one whose time passed with no check-in, shows on the patient Appointments page with a "You missed this appointment…" note and a **Reschedule** button (the "No Show" badge counts under All only); the "Missed appointment" notification pop-up has a Reschedule button too (hands off via `sessionStorage dgt_resched_appt`, or `window.__ptResched` when already on the page). `action=reschedule` accepts `no_show`, skips the past-date and 24-hour gates for a missed visit, sets it to `rescheduled`, clears `reminder_sent_at` / `missed_notified_at`, and the reservation fee carries over (same payment row). Both missed-visit notification texts now say this.
- **Reschedule of a pending request keeps it `pending`** (it still needs Approve, where the dentist is picked and duty-checked) and is never allowed onto a full slot, even by staff.
- **No Show:** `appointments.status` gained `'no_show'`. The Approved Appointments table shows a **No Show** button once a visit's start time has passed with no check-in; `action=no_show` (receptionist/admin) requires approved/rescheduled, not checked in, start time passed (DB clock), frees the slot, notifies the patient ("Missed appointment") and sets `missed_notified_at` so the daily sweep doesn't repeat it. Badge colour is a one-off plum; the admin Appointment page has a No Show tile + filter; the patient page hides no-show rows like past cancellations.

### Service catalogue & per-unit quantities (migration 061, 2026-10)

The old 10 "treatment area" services were permanently deleted (2026-10-02, at the user's request, local + live; their procedure lines went with them) and replaced by the clinic's finalized **21-service price list** (Adjustment ₱1,000 … X-ray ₱800), photos in `uploads/services/` under the user's own filenames. `services.quantity_unit` (NULL = single-session, always qty 1; else `tooth` / `tooth surface` / `unit` / `arch` / `quadrant` / `scan`, the list in `api/services.php` `QUANTITY_UNITS`) marks the 9 per-unit services (Bridge, Dentures, Extraction, Gingivectomy, Resto, RCT, TADS, Veneers, X-ray). `appointment_services.qty` holds how many the dentist logged; **`appointment_services.price` stays the LINE TOTAL** (qty × unit price) so every `SUM(price)` report is unchanged. The dentist's `logProcedureModal()` shows a Qty box only for per-unit services (any service can still be ticked as an extra procedure, and every price stays editable); `api/treatments.php` forces qty 1 for single-session services. The cashier invoice seeds `qty` / `unit` / unit cost from those lines; `billing.php` locks the Qty input (readonly) on single-session service rows and `api/payments.php action=settle` re-forces qty 1 for them server-side; per-unit rows and free add-on rows stay editable. **Superseded the same day for the cashier (2026-10-02): only the dentist sets services and quantities.** `billing.php` no longer has + Add Service, + Add-ons or the red × row delete, every Qty input is read-only, and the "Less: Withholding Tax (record only)" row is gone; `api/payments.php action=settle` keeps only lines that match a dentist-logged procedure (one per service, add-on rows dropped) and always uses the dentist's quantity. Same day again: the whole line-item table is read-only on the cashier screen (qty, unit, description, unit cost) and `settle` rebuilds the invoice lines from `appointment_services` alone, ignoring any client `line_items`. The admin Services form has a **Charged Per** select; prices render as "₱1,500.00 per tooth" on the admin list, patient Services page, booking/walk-in/cashier pickers.

**Update 2026-10-02: list prices are estimates only.** The patient side shows no service prices at all (Services cards, booking wizard service picker, no "per tooth"). The dentist's Services Availed modal keeps the Qty box but every price input starts blank and must be typed; `api/treatments.php` rejects a ticked procedure with no charge (no fallback to `base_price`). `base_price` still feeds the admin list and staff pickers.

### Discounts & promotions (migration 023)

Admins create service promotions in **Admin Settings** (a **Promotions & Discounts** card stacked in the Settings left column — a capped/scrolling list with a `N active · N total` chip, **+ New Discount**, per-row **Edit** and a click-to-flip **Active/Inactive** status badge). Backed by `api/discounts.php` (`GET action=list` admin — rows + the active service catalogue for the picker; `GET action=active` admin+cashier — active promos with parsed `service_ids`; `POST action=save` / `action=toggle` admin). The `discounts` table: `discount_name`, `discount_type` (`percentage` | `fixed`), `discount_value` (percent 1–100, or a peso amount), `target_scope` (`all` | `specific`), `applied_service_ids` (JSON int array of `services.service_id`, `NULL` when scope `all`), `is_active`. A promo is never deleted — toggle it off to retire it.

**Cashier engine.** `payments.php?action=invoice` returns `discounts` (the active list) + `applied_promo` (the saved promo on a re-opened bill). `modules/cashier/billing.php` shows a **Promotion / Discount** `<select>` in the left payment block, populated with the promos *eligible for the current invoice* — scope `all`, or scope `specific` sharing a `service_id` with a line item (every line row carries `data-service-id`; service rows seeded from the availed procedures and from **+ Add Service** have one, add-ons / free rows don't). Choosing one adds a **`Less: <promo name>  −₱X.XX`** row to the totals panel and feeds the running net: `Net = Total Sales − SC/PWD − Promo − Reservation credit − prior payments`. The deduction rule (mirrored in `promoDeduction()` in `api/_bootstrap.php` and the billing JS): percentage/fixed against the whole Total Sales for scope `all`, or against just the matching line items for scope `specific`; never more than Total Sales. (The print/PDF-only reference-format doc built by `billing.php`'s `buildPrintDoc()`, and `receipt.php`'s own render, both label this same figure "Subtotal Before Discount" instead — see the invoice reformat note above; the underlying computation is identical.) On **Paid**, the client sends only `promo_discount_id` + the `line_items` (each with `service_id`); the server **recomputes** the deduction and persists `payments.promo_discount_id` / `promo_discount_label` / `promo_discount_amount` (`recomputePayment()` folds `promo_discount_amount` into "covered"). `invoice_items.service_id` (also migration 023) is what lets a service-specific promo be matched back to its lines.

**Custom downpayment.** The **Installment** radio already accepts any Received Amount below the balance (`payment_type=installment` → `applied = received`, status `partially_paid`, remaining balance logged); the billing screen now relabels the totals row **"Balance after downpayment"** (= balance − received) while Installment is selected, so a ₱2,500 downpayment on ₱50,000 braces reads clearly.

**Receipts & View modal.** `appointmentDetail()` returns the promo columns, so both `modules/shared/receipt.php` (a `Less: <promo name>  −₱X.XX` row above the "Total" totals row — `billing.php`'s own live form still calls the equivalent row "Total Due", see the reformat note above) and `app.js appointmentModal()` (a `Less: <promo name>` line in the Billing block) show the deduction explicitly.

### Tables

`users` (`reset_token_hash` / `reset_token_expires_at` for password reset, migration 018; `is_verified` (migration 024) / `verify_otp_hash` / `verify_otp_expires_at` for patient e-mail verification (OTP, migration 041 — renamed from the original `verify_token_hash` link-token column); `last_seen_at` presence signal + the `dental_assistant` role value, migration 026 (the seeded assistant login it added was removed by migration 038 — assistants are non-account staff now); `prc_license VARCHAR(50)` for dentists, migration 032; **migration 028** split the single `full_name` into `first_name` / `middle_name` (optional) / `last_name` and added `must_change_password TINYINT(1)`. `full_name` stays as an **auto-maintained mirror** — every insert/update writes `full_name = personName($first,$middle,$last)` (trimmed "First Middle Last"), so the ~28 display sites keep reading it untouched. `username` is auto-generated **`first.last`** (lowercase, non-alphanumerics stripped, numeric suffix on collision) via `makeUsername()` in `functions.php` — used by `register.php`, `api/users.php` and `api/records.php`; **it is never entered by hand and is the only login credential** (e-mail login is off). Both helpers live in `includes/functions.php`), `services` (migration 019 replaced the old procedure list with the clinic's **10 treatment areas**: Restorative Dentistry, Prosthodontics, Orthodontics, Dental Implants, Pedodontics, General Dentistry, Cosmetic Dentistry, Dental Surgery, Endodontics, Dental X-Ray (renamed from "X-Ray (Panoramic, Periapical)" by migration `033`). **Migration `044`** (data-only, no schema change) appended a second, factual sentence to each of the 10 services' `description` — e.g. Endodontics gained "The treated tooth is then sealed and typically restored with a crown to protect it for long-term use." — an initial attempt at making the landing-page card and its detail-popup modal show different text by having the card show only the description's first sentence. **That still wasn't independent enough** — the card's "sentence" was always a literal prefix of the modal's full text, so the user pointed at the reference again ("look at this two") to make clear the two are meant to be **completely separate, independently-written** pieces of copy, not one truncated into the other. **Migration `045`** fixed this properly: it adds a real `short_description VARCHAR(180)` column (unrelated in wording to `description`, e.g. Endodontics' is "Root canal treatment that saves a badly infected or damaged tooth.") and is now what the landing-page card renders (`$svcCardBlurb()` in `index.php`, falling back to the description's first sentence only if a service has no `short_description` set yet); the popup modal continues to show the full `description` unchanged. `short_description` is a normal, admin-editable field — a **"Homepage Card Blurb"** input was added to the admin Services Add/Edit form (`modules/admin/services.php`), and `api/services.php action=save` reads/writes it on both insert and update. **Migration `046`** (data-only) repointed Dental X-Ray's `image` at a real uploaded panoramic X-ray photo (`dental_xray.jpg`, replacing the old `xray_panoramic_periapical.png`) and Endodontics' existing `endodontics.jpg` file was overwritten in place with a real root-canal illustration (no row change needed for that one, same filename) — both supplied directly by the user into `uploads/services/`. Since every consumer of `services.image` (landing page grid + modal, patient "Our Services", admin Services list thumbnail) reads the column dynamically, updating the one `services` row/file was enough to refresh the photo everywhere at once. **Overwriting a service image file in place (same filename) needs a cache-busting query string, or browsers keep showing the old cached copy** — the Endodontics photo swap was correct on disk/DB immediately, but showed the old image until this was added: `api/services.php`'s `GET` (no-action) response now includes a per-row `image_v` (that file's `filemtime()`, or `null` if missing), and every consumer appends it as `?v=<image_v>` — `index.php` builds the URL server-side via `asset('uploads/services/' . rawurlencode($s['image']))` (which already does its own `filemtime()` cache-busting, so it needed no separate `image_v` field), while `modules/patient/services.php` and `modules/admin/services.php` build the `<img src>` client-side from the JSON list and append `s.image_v` by hand (`IMG_BASE + encodeURIComponent(s.image) + (s.image_v ? '?v='+s.image_v : '')`) since they don't have PHP's `asset()` available at render time. Every consumer (homepage carousel, patient "Our Services", admin Services CRUD, booking/billing pickers) is `services`-table-driven so no code changed. `services.image` (a bare filename served from `/dgt_dental_clinic/uploads/services/`, uploaded via `api/services.php?action=upload_image`) — migration `020_service_images.sql` attached a photo to all 10 rows, matched by `service_name`; the homepage carousel, admin Services list thumb, and patient "Our Services" card all render it with a 🦷 / gradient fallback). `services.duration_minutes` still exists (`INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 30`) but is **no longer shown or edited anywhere** — the user had the Duration column + form field removed from the admin Services CRUD (and the duration line from the patient "Our Services" card); `api/services.php action=save` no longer reads or writes it, so it just keeps its seeded value. `appointments` (`patient_phone` per-booking contact number, migration 016; `checkin_assistant_id` / `treatment_assistant_id` — FK → `dental_assistants`, `ON DELETE SET NULL`, migration 038; `reminder_sent_at` — one-shot guard for the day-before reminder notification, migration 048; `missed_notified_at` — one-shot guard for the missed-appointment follow-up, migration 049, see the reminder/missed-appointment paragraphs above; `payment_decline_code` — structured reason slug for a `payment_declined` status, migration 056, see the Payment Decline & Resubmission paragraph above), `appointment_services` (line items), `payments` (one per appointment; wide — reservation, SC/PWD + `promo_discount_id` / `promo_discount_label` / `promo_discount_amount` (migration 023), per-method amounts, derived balance), `discounts` (migration 023 — admin-managed service promotions; see *Discounts & promotions*), `notifications`, `patient_records` (one per patient; `tooth_chart` is JSON for the FDI dental chart; `dental_age` is a free-text "Child / Adult Age" clinical note. **Since migration 043, every "for someone else" online booking creates a genuine row here too** (via `createUnlinkedPatientRecord()` in `api/_bootstrap.php`, no e-mail/login — see the *Booking "for someone else"* paragraph above) — such a patient is otherwise indistinguishable from any other account in this table or in Patient Records; there is no merge, no separate table, and no "Family Member Of" badge (that migration-042 approach was replaced). The dentist's **Patient Records** page — `#` · Name (Last, First) · Age · Contact · Email — has **+ Create Record** (`api/records.php?action=create` makes a `users` row with role `patient` + a starter `patient_records` row) and a Patient Details modal: Personal Information block, then a full-width Dental Chart & Medical History section — a 3-col grid of clinical text fields above, the full-width FDI tooth chart below — toggled editable by **Edit Dental Info** → **Save Changes** / **Cancel**, saved via `action=save`. **Role-scoped access** (`api/records.php` is `apiAuth(['dentist','admin','receptionist'])`): the **receptionist** has its own `modules/receptionist/records.php` — same directory + search + **Create Record**, but the Patient Details modal shows only Personal Information + an **editable Contact Information** block (phone/email on `users`, plus `patient_records.address`; the `emergency_contact_name` / `emergency_contact_number` columns from migration 012 still exist and the API accepts them, but were removed from the UI) + a bare **Appointment History** table (date · service · dentist · status). The server hides all clinical data from non-dentist/admin roles — `action=get` strips the clinical text fields, `tooth_chart`, and the per-visit `notes` / availed-procedure list / billing figures; `action=save` ignores clinical fields when the caller is a receptionist), `blocked_dates` (clinic-wide closed days, managed from admin Settings only), `dentist_schedules` (migration 031 — per-dentist weekly working grid; `dentist_id` + `day_of_week` (0=Sun…6=Sat) + `time_slot` + `is_active`, `UNIQUE(dentist_id, day_of_week, time_slot)`; admin-only "Dentist Schedules" page + `api/schedules.php`; duty mode itself is `users.doctor_type`; see the per-dentist-working-schedule paragraph above), `dentist_absences` (migration 040 — one-off dentist absence override for a single date, e.g. a sick day; `dentist_id` + `absence_date` + optional `reason` + `marked_by`, `UNIQUE(dentist_id, absence_date)`; checked inside `dentistBookableSlots()` so it overrides `dentist_schedules` for that date everywhere on-duty is computed; managed admin-only from the admin dashboard's Doctors on Duty drill-down via `api/schedules.php action=set_absence`; see the one-off dentist absence paragraph above), `settings` (key/value, via `setting()` / `setSetting()` — includes `reservation_fee`, and the online-payment instructions `pay_bank_details` / `pay_gcash_name` / `pay_gcash_number` / `pay_gcash_qr` still served to the patient booking flow by `settings.php?action=payment_info` — Bank Transfer + GCash only. `pay_gcash_qr` is set to `gcash_qrcode.jpg` (migration `034`; the file is committed at `uploads/payment/gcash_qrcode.jpg`) so the reservation-fee step renders the real GCash QR. `pay_bank_details` holds 3 newline-separated lines (bank / account name / account number) — currently a **demo BPI account** (`BPI (Bank of the Philippine Islands)` / `DGT One Dental Center` / `4089-0512-77`, migration `035`; swap for the real account once opened) rendered as the bank clinic-account block. The admin **Settings → Online Payment Details** card that edited those keys was removed; the values persist in the `settings` table and the `api/settings.php` `save_payment` / `upload_payment_qr` / `clear_payment_qr` actions still exist but have no UI. The admin Settings page has five cards: **Reservation Deposit**, **Clinic Time Slots** (`add_slot` / `remove_slot` — chip list + `<input type="time">`), **Backup & Restore**, **Promotions & Discounts** (migration 023 — see *Discounts & promotions*) and **Block an Operational Date**. Layout: a 2-col `items-start` grid — the first four stack in a `flex flex-col gap-4` **left column**, **Block an Operational Date** is **alone in the right column** so its blocked-dates list is the only thing that can grow. Nothing is placed full-width below the grid (an uneven `items-start` grid + a full-width card underneath opens a mid-page gap — the Promotions card was moved into the left stack for exactly this reason). Both `#blockedList` and `#discList` cap at `max-h-[420px]` and scroll (sticky `<th>`), each with a count chip beside its heading), `counter_transactions` (migration 013; `bank_branch` / `bank_account` added migration 017; migration `022` added `gcash_number` / `bank_reference` / `amount_cash` / `amount_gcash` / `amount_bank` / `received_amount` / `change_amount` / `payment_date` and `'split'` to `payment_method` — was the store for cashier unbooked walk-in services / manual adjustments from the Transaction page's "+ Add Transaction" modal; **that modal was removed 2026-09**, so the table + `counter_add` / `counter_list` endpoints persist but nothing writes or reads them from the UI), `invoice_items` (migration 014 — one row per Service Invoice line on the cashier Billing screen; `service_id` added migration 023 so a service-specific promo can match a line; seeded from the dentist's procedures, cashier may add Service / Add-on rows; `settle` rewrites them and sets `payments.total_amount` from their sum), `activity_log` (migration 029 — the admin **History Log** audit trail: `created_at`, `user_id` (FK `ON DELETE SET NULL`), `username` / `role` snapshot, `category` (`auth`/`appointments`/`records`/`billing`), `action` text, `ip`. Append-only, written by `logActivity()`; read only by `api/logs.php` for the admin page), `dental_assistants` (migration 038 — non-account chairside staff roster: `assistant_id`, `full_name`, `avatar` (reserved), `is_active`; migration 039 added `first_name` / `middle_name` (optional) / `last_name` (same `personName()`-mirror convention as `users.full_name`), `contact_number`, `email`, `years_experience`, `about` — still no username/password columns), `assistant_shifts` (migration 038 — one row per assistant per day they reported for duty: `assistant_id` + `shift_date` + `marked_by`, `UNIQUE(assistant_id, shift_date)`; written by `api/assistants.php action=set_shift`). **There is no `patient_dependents` table** — migration 042 added one for a patient's saved "family members," and migration 043 (2026-09) dropped it again at the user's request in favour of just creating a real patient account per treated person; see the *Booking "for someone else"* paragraph above. `appointments.treated_patient_id` (migration 043, nullable, FK → `users(user_id)`, `ON DELETE SET NULL` — replaces migration 042's `dependent_id` FK to the now-gone `patient_dependents` table) marks which appointments were actually for a different real patient account than the one that booked them; `appointments.patient_id` is unaffected and still means the booking account.

### Backup & restore

`api/settings.php` (`action=backup` / `action=restore`, admin only) shells out via `exec()` to `mysqldump.exe` / `mysql.exe`. `mysqlClientPath()` locates the binary at `dirname(__DIR__, 3) . '\mysql\bin\'` (the XAMPP root above `\htdocs\dgt_dental_clinic`), falling back to `C:\xampp` / `D:\xampp`. The DB password is passed through the `MYSQL_PWD` env var, never on the command line. Backup streams the dump as a file download (overriding the bootstrap's JSON `Content-Type`); restore imports an uploaded `.sql` and **overwrites the whole database**.

### Uploads

`uploads/avatars/`, `uploads/receipts/`, `uploads/services/`, `uploads/payment/` (GCash QR image — currently `gcash_qrcode.jpg`, committed; `settings.php?action=upload_payment_qr&method=gcash`, admin only, no UI) — files are moved here by the relevant API; the DB stores only the filename, and `avatarUrl()` / hard-coded `/dgt_dental_clinic/uploads/...` paths build the public URL.

## Conventions

- Absolute web paths are hard-coded as `/dgt_dental_clinic/...` throughout (app is not portable to another base path without a search-and-replace).
- No CSRF tokens; auth is session-cookie only (`SameSite=Lax`).
- Escape on output: `e()` in PHP, `esc()` in JS — always, for any dynamic value.
- Every phone / contact-number input is `<input type="tel">` (add `inputmode="numeric"` too). A global delegated listener in `assets/js/app.js` strips non-digits from any `type="tel"` field, so new phone inputs get digits-only for free — never leave one as plain text.
- **Every phone / mobile / GCash-number field must be a valid PH mobile number: exactly 11 digits, `^09\d{9}$`.** Client: `window.wirePhoneField(input, {errEl?, submitBtn?})` in `app.js` forces the attrs, shows an inline `.field-err` (`PH_MOBILE_MSG`) + red border on a bad non-empty value, disables `submitBtn`, and normalises a prefilled value to digits; `input._phoneOk(allowEmpty=true)` reports validity for the submit handler. Server: `isPhMobile($s)` / `digitsOnly($s)` / `PH_MOBILE_MSG` in `functions.php`, re-checked in `appointments.php` create + walk_in, `profile.php`, `users.php` add, `records.php` create + save, `settings.php` save_payment (`payments.php` settle's own `gcash_number` check was removed 2026-09, instructor feedback — that field no longer exists on the cashier Billing screen). Numbers are stored as bare 11-digit text. Wired on: patient booking phone, receptionist walk-in phone, admin add-user contact, dentist/receptionist Create Record + receptionist edit-contact, shared profile, cashier Billing invoice Phone. **On the account-creation forms the phone is now required, not optional** — the walk-in "New patient" modal, both **+ Create Record** modals and the admin **Add User** modal (patient / receptionist / cashier — every role whose form has the field) call `_phoneOk(false)` and the matching API 422s on a missing/invalid number; admin & dentist Add-User have no phone field and are exempt (the Assistants tab has no phone field — assistants are non-account staff).
- Keep new module pages consistent with the `$me` / `ob_start` / `$PAGE_SCRIPT` / `require layout.php` pattern and add a matching entry to `includes/sidebar.php` with the right `$ACTIVE` key.

### Printed Service Invoice = the clinic's paper pad (2026-10-07)

Supersedes every earlier "reference-format / A5 monochrome INVOICE" description above for the **printed** document. `billing.php` `buildPrintDoc()` (into `#invPrintRoot`) and `modules/shared/receipt.php` both render a copy of the clinic's own paper Service Invoice (`.si-*` classes in `includes/invoice_style.php`): "DGT ONE DENTAL CENTER CO.", VAT Reg TIN 608-196-530-00009, the Del Rey Bldg. Gov. F. Halili Ave. address, Cash Sales / Charge Sales boxes (Cash ticked when fully paid), Date, Sold To, the Registered Name / TIN / Bus. Address / SC-PWD ID box, Nature of Service / Qty / Unit Price / Amount (padded to 4 rows), the VAT box, "Received the Amount of" (reservation + counter payment, in words), the totals column (Total Sales, Less Discount = SC/PWD, a promo row and a Less: Reservation Deposit row only when present, Total Amount Due) and Cashier/Authorized Signature, laid out 1:1 with the pad photo: no outer frame, a condensed slab name (Rockwell Extra Bold, scaleX .72) left-aligned with its underline, Arial Narrow body text, the customer box as its own closed box (a small gap below it, each line starting right after its own label and running unbroken under the SC/PWD labels; built with flex rows, not a table), and the Nature of Service table (3 rows, 57/8.5/10.5/24) and VAT (46.5%) / totals (53.5%) boxes joined edge to edge. No Nº serial (the user removed it). The Date prints like the pad: "Oct. 07" on a short line sized to its text, then ", 20" and the 2-digit year, all on one text baseline. Sold To prints "Last, First Middle" via `invoiceSoldTo()` (functions.php): the treated patient's (or self-booking account's) name fields, else the free-text name with its last word as the last name; a name the cashier retyped in "Received from" prints as typed. Bottom-left under the VAT box: Received the Amount of, the BIR-accredited printer block (F.G. Vitug, booklet range, ATP); bottom-right: the totals with the cashier signature line under them. Prints on A5 portrait with `@page { margin: 0 }` (no browser URL/page-number header or footer) and 8mm sheet padding. Every box and cell line is a plain 1px (the VAT box and totals box share one edge, drawn once). Print type is 12px; leftover page height is split evenly between the Nature of Service rows and the space above the cashier signature (all of it in the rows squeezed the signature; none of it left a big blank bottom). VAT lines and Withholding Tax print blank, as the clinic leaves them on paper. The live `#invRoot` screen header uses the same name/TIN/address; its old "NOT VALID FOR CLAIMING INPUT TAXES" line was removed.

**Doctor's Fee row removed (2026-10-07).** The cashier Billing screen no longer shows "Less: Doctor's Fee (20%) (record only)". It was a thesis-only line, not part of a standard Philippine invoice. `api/payments.php action=settle` now writes `payments.doctor_fee = 0`; the column stays but nothing reads it. This supersedes the Doctor's Fee mentions in the Billing paragraphs above.

**Invoice rows grow with the services (2026-10-07).** The printed Service Invoice always shows at least 3 service rows and adds one per extra service. With 4-9 services `.si-doc` gets `si-many` (shorter rows), with 10+ also `si-dense` (10px type), so up to ~18 services still print on one A5 page. Item columns are 54/8/14/24 (Unit Price widened for 5-digit prices); the Amount line still lines up with the totals divider (`.si-tot th` 55%).

**Invoice on phones and iPads (2026-10-07).** Mobile devices have no Arial Narrow / Rockwell, so the wider fallback pushed the totals labels into the amount column. `invoice_style.php` now loads Roboto Condensed + Roboto Slab from Google Fonts as stand-ins, and on screen `.invoice-sheet` is a size container with `.si-doc { font-size: clamp(6px, 100cqw / 44, 13px) }`, so the sheet keeps the same layout at any width, just smaller. Print keeps its fixed 12px.

**Phone/tablet print fix (2026-10-07).** The print `.invoice-sheet` min-height is `min(100vh, 209mm)`, not `100vh`: mobile browsers resolve `100vh` against the screen, which stretched the sheet past one page and pushed the bottom half onto page 2.

**Drawer hidden on first paint (2026-10-07).** `tailwind.php`'s static `<style>` hides `#sidebar` below 1280px (`translateX(-100
**Drawer hidden on first paint (2026-10-07).** The static `<style>` in `tailwind.php` hides `#sidebar` below 1280px (`translateX(-100%)`, `.open` shows it). Without it the sidebar showed on phones / iPads until the Tailwind CDN built `max-nav:-translate-x-full`, then slid shut on its own.
The drawer only opens from the burger: `app.js` closes it on every `pageshow` (incl. Back / Forward restores) and exposes `window.setDrawer`, which `nav.js` calls after each page swap; `index.php`'s own menu drawer also closes on a Back / Forward restore. There are no swipe listeners.
**First-visit-after-login drawer bug (2026-10-07).** The post-login entrance animation in `app.js` (`dgt_just_logged_in`) animated `#sidebar`'s `transform`, which overrode the drawer's `translateX(-100%)` and showed it on the first portal page after login (a patient's first "My Portal" visit, since `index.php` doesn't consume the flag). The sidebar now joins that animation only at `min-width: 1280px`.

### Admin-editable GCash details and legal texts (migration 065, 2026-10-08)

- **Settings > Payment Settings** now also edits the GCash QR (upload with preview, Remove), GCash Account Name and GCash Mobile Number (`api/settings.php` `upload_payment_qr` / `clear_payment_qr` / `save_payment`; `save_payment` only writes the keys it is sent, so it never wipes `pay_bank_details`). The patient booking payment step already reads these via `action=payment_info`. Uploading a new QR deletes the previous file.
- **Settings > Legal & Policy Settings**: tabbed editor for Terms & Conditions and Privacy Policy, stored in the `legal_documents` table (`settings.setting_value` is only VARCHAR(255)). `includes/legal.php` holds the default texts (used until an admin saves), `legalDoc()`, and `renderLegal()` (escapes, then `## ` heading, `- ` bullet, `> ` notice box, `**bold**`, `*italic*`, blank line = new paragraph). API: `GET action=legal`, `POST save_legal` / `reset_legal` (admin). `terms.php` renders Terms; the new `privacy.php` sets `$LEGAL_DOC = 'privacy'` and includes it. The sign-up form's Privacy Policy link and new footer links on `index.php` point at these pages. `includes/terms_content.php` was deleted (its text moved into `legalDefault()`, with Privacy split out of Terms §2).

### Treatment Plan & Follow-up Service (migration 066, 2026-10-08)

- **Dentist:** the Services Availed pop-up (`logProcedureModal()` in `app.js`) has an optional **Recommended Follow-up Service** + **Suggested Timeframe** dropdown of common dental follow-up intervals, days / weeks / months only, shown as the plain label (no description in the option; the typical use is kept as a code comment): 3 Days (denture check), 1 Week (extraction / surgery check), 2 Weeks (sutures, next root canal session), 1 Month and 6 Weeks (braces adjustment), 3 Months (gum maintenance, implant review), 6 Months (routine check-up) (`FOLLOWUP_TIMEFRAMES` in `api/_bootstrap.php`, `FU_TIMEFRAMES` in `app.js`; an 8-option researched list and a typed-in version were both dropped). `api/treatments.php` saves it as a `treatment_plans` row (one per visit, `consultation_id` = that appointment) locked to the visit's dentist, with `due_date` = visit date + timeframe, and notifies the patient. A due date never lands on a closed day: `nextOpenClinicDate()` (`api/_bootstrap.php`) moves it past Sundays and `blocked_dates`, and when an admin blocks a date, `shiftTreatmentPlanDueDates($date)` (called from `api/settings.php action=block_date`) moves every not-completed plan due that day to the next open day. Unblocking does not move them back.
- **Patient:** the Appointments page shows a **Recommended Next Services** card (`api/treatment_plans.php?action=mine`, Pending plans only). **Schedule Now** opens the normal booking wizard with patient, service and dentist locked (`pf._plan`); it still needs the reservation fee and front-desk approval (user's choice).
- **Slot rules for a follow-up** (`planSlotProblem()` in `api/appointments.php`, and `action=availability&plan_id=`): the plan dentist must be on duty, the clinic-wide cap stays **5 per hour** (the pasted spec said 4; the user confirmed that was a typo), a **roaming** dentist must have no other live booking that hour, an in-house one must not already hold all 5. `action=create` uses the plan's dentist instead of auto-routing.
- **Lock:** approve keeps the plan dentist, Reassign is refused (unless the dentist declined it on-call), reschedule keeps the dentist and re-checks the slot rules (`&plan_id=..&exclude_id=..` on the reschedule calendars).
- **Status:** `syncTreatmentPlans()` derives Pending / Scheduled / Completed from the bookings carrying `appointments.treatment_plan_id`, so a cancelled / declined / no-show booking puts the plan back to Pending.
- **Roaming alert:** when the patient books, a roaming plan dentist gets an `oncall_assigned` bell notification ("waiting for front desk approval", no Decline while pending) plus an e-mail; approval sends the usual on-call notice too.

**Walk-ins may take the hour in progress (2026-10-08).** The walk-in calendar calls `action=availability` with `&walkin=1` (ignored for the patient role): today's hour that has started but not ended (10:20 into the 10:00 slot) stays bookable while it is under capacity; an ended hour is still "Passed". `action=walk_in` rejects only an hour that is already over. Online booking is unchanged (`ONLINE_LEAD_DAYS` = 2, `BLOCK_PASSED_SLOTS` = true, both ON, despite older notes above saying OFF). Marking a No Show releases that hour's capacity (only pending / approved / rescheduled count), so the walk-in picker shows the seat when it next loads, and `action=no_show` calls `syncTreatmentPlans()` so a follow-up plan goes from Scheduled back to Pending for the patient to rebook.

**No Show is end-of-day only; late arrivals are checked in (2026-10-09).** Supersedes "once a visit's start time has passed" above. The **No Show** button (receptionist Approved Appointments) shows only after `clinic_close` (setting, 5:00 PM) on the visit's day, or any later day; `action=no_show` 422s before that ("The patient may still arrive late today."). A patient who arrives after their booked hour is still checked in normally: the **Check In** modal (`checkInModal(id, name, slot)`) looks up the hour in progress (or the next one, e.g. after lunch) via `availability&walkin=1`. If it has room it says so and checks them in; if it is full (5 of 5), or no hours are left today, Check In is replaced by **Reschedule** (opens `reschedModal`). That capacity check is a guide only; `check_in` itself is unchanged.

**Receipt upload rules block Submit (2026-10-09).** In the booking wizard's payment step and Resubmit Payment, `gateSubmit()` keeps **Submit Request / Resubmit Payment** disabled while the uploaded receipt is being checked or while the upload box shows any error: the date must be today (a missing date is an error), the amount at least the fee (a missing amount is an error; OCR's "P" for the peso sign and "Amount ... 500.00" lines count), plus the existing portrait / readable / GCash / not AI-generated or edited checks. Each upload scans into its own scratch element and only the latest one is shown. If the OCR engine can't load, nothing blocks. Client-side only (the server can't re-read the image). `payments.receipt_warnings` (migration 067) is still written but no longer shown: the receptionist's receipt viewer error list was removed the same day.

**Reservation amount confirmed at Approve (migration 068, 2026-10-09).** The booking wizard's upload check sends the largest peso figure it read off the receipt (`receipt_amount`), stored as `payments.receipt_amount_read`. The receptionist's Approve pop-up shows **Amount Received (GCASH/BANK)**, pre-filled with that figure (else the recorded fee), with a note when it is above or below the fee. `action=confirm` takes `reservation_amount` and writes it to `reservation_fee_paid` (+ `recomputePayment()`, History Log line), so a patient who sent ₱1,000 gets the whole ₱1,000 deducted at the cashier. Overpayment beyond the bill is not refunded or tracked: `remaining_balance` floors at 0.

**Time-slot pop-up + booking draft (2026-10-10, adviser feedback).** On the patient booking calendars (booking wizard step 1, patient Reschedule, Calendar View), clicking a date opens `window.openSlotPopup()` (app.js): a centred pop-up above any modal (z-130, on `<body>`) with that day's times; Escape / X / backdrop close only the pop-up. Picking a time selects it at once; the date stays green (`.pcal-cell.picked`, "✓ 10:00 AM", "✓10AM" on phones) with a green "Selected: ... Change time / Clear" line (`.pcal-picked-box`). The old inline day panel / slot list below the calendar is gone. Calendar View's pop-up button is "Continue to Booking" (hands off via `dgt_book_prefill`); that page has no Selected line under the calendar, only the green date (user request). Drafts in sessionStorage, all reads try/catch: `dgt_book_draft` (wizard step 1 fields + `popupDay`; restored on reload; a kept booking shows no banner or Start over button (both removed 2026-10-11); Back / X clear it; kept only on an accidental click outside the dialog (`closeModal(reason)` sends `dgt:modalclose` with `detail.reason` = button / backdrop / escape / none; backdrop or none flags the draft `open:false` so "+ Request Appointment" reopens it filled in, and a reload only reopens a wizard that was still open), dropped on Back, X, Escape, submit and Start over; follow-up plan bookings are not saved) and `dgt_cal_draft` (Calendar View `{date, slot, popupDay}`). If the pop-up was open at reload it reopens; otherwise the pick shows green. The receptionist Walk-in and Reschedule calendars work the same way since 2026-10-11 (their own copy of wireReschedCal, r_* ids).

**Guided forms (2026-10-10, adviser feedback).** `window.guideForm(root, barEl, steps)` in app.js (CSS `.guide-*` in tailwind.php): an optional "Step N of M" bar (not used: the user removed it 2026-10-11, pass `null`), a pulsing indigo (#6366f1) ring on the current step (teal and amber were rejected; it must not match the teal / blue buttons); when focus moves on, the whole field (full calendar, whole upload box) is scrolled into view; a field taller than the dialog is lined up with the top (the user rejected shrinking the calendar to fit)'s control, a green border + label check on finished fields, later steps dimmed + `inert` (buttons only get `aria-disabled`, so a click still shows the validation toast). Focus moves on only after a pick-type field (select, radio, calendar, 13-digit GCash ref, 11-digit phone, DOB, receipt upload) or when a pick reveals a new field in its own step (GCash shows the reference box); typing in name / email / bank-ref boxes never jumps. Field values are getters, re-read on input/change/click and DOM changes. Used on: patient booking step 1 (patient details, service, date+time, Continue), the payment step (method, then the Upload Image button, then reference, then Submit; with the screenshot upload turned off it is method, reference, Submit; **no payment method is pre-selected any more**), Resubmit Payment (upload, reference, Submit), and the receptionist walk-in modal (patient, payment, service, date+time, dentist, Book). Not on the cashier Billing screen.

**Receipt check look (2026-10-11).** While the screenshot is read, the upload box shows a "Checking your payment screenshot…" card with a spinner, a live percentage and a progress bar (`RCPT_STATUS_HTML`, driven by the Tesseract `logger` through `tessLog`). Problems show as one row each in a red box (`#rcptWarn`, `addReceiptWarning(el, msg, kind)`): an amber "?" when something couldn't be read, a red "x" when it is wrong. The reference box now sits below the upload (`refFieldHtml()`, booking wizard and Resubmit Payment) with a note under it (`#refNote`): "Reading the reference number…", "Filled in from your screenshot" (filled only when every check passed), or "We didn't fill in the reference number…". There is no check of who the payment was sent to.

**Rescheduled rows stand out (2026-10-11).** On the receptionist dashboard, a row in Appointments for Approval or Approved Appointments whose status is `rescheduled` or that has a `previous_scheduled_date` gets an amber View button (the Receipt View button in the approval table); `isResched()` / `RESCHED_BTN` are in the page script. The row itself is not highlighted (a row tint + "Rescheduled from" line was tried and removed at the user's request).

**Walk-in picker hides the roaming dentist (2026-10-11).** In the Walk-in Booking modal's Assign Dentist list, a roaming dentist who is still locked by the waterfall (`roaming_locked`) is left out of the list entirely instead of showing greyed out. They appear once every on-duty in-house dentist has `slotCapacity()-1` bookings that hour or is off duty. The online Approve pop-up does the same, except a roaming dentist the booking was already routed to stays listed (and pre-selected). Reassign is unchanged.

**Search and filters (2026-10-11, adviser feedback).** Cashier Transaction page: a search box above Patient Transactions filters the queue live by patient name, OR number (`OR-000085`, or just `85`), reservation ref no., service or dentist; each name shows its OR number underneath, and no match reads "No pending payment records found…". `api/payments.php action=queue` now also returns `pay.reservation_reference`. Receptionist Approved Appointments and Appointments for Approval (the latter also searches e-mail, phone and ref no.): a toolbar with a patient / doctor / service search, a Month select (built from the months in the list), a single Date picker (Month and Date clear each other) and Reset Filters, plus an "N of M shown" count. Both filter the last fetch in the browser, so the filters stay applied when the table reloads.

**Online badge is slate (2026-10-11).** `src-online` in `app.js` `BADGE_C` is now violet (`#6d28d9` on `#ede9fe`): indigo looked like the blue Approved badge and slate was too plain.

**Reschedule scrolls to the reason (2026-10-12).** In the patient and receptionist Reschedule pop-ups, clicking **Confirm** in the time pop-up calls `window.scrollToReason(id)` (app.js, via the pop-up's `onConfirm`; just tapping a time does not scroll): it smooth-scrolls the Reason box into view and, with a mouse only, focuses it.

**Reschedule can't keep the same time (2026-10-11).** In every reschedule calendar (patient `reschedModal`, receptionist `reschedModal`), the visit's own date + time shows as a disabled "Current time" slot (`hooks.current` in the patient `wireReschedCal`, a 5th `current` argument in the receptionist one). `api/appointments.php action=reschedule` 422s the same date + time, except when a doctor-absence reschedule switches to another dentist.

**Services Availed pop-up is a row builder (2026-10-11, adviser feedback).** Supersedes the checkbox list above. `logProcedureModal()` in `app.js` shows the booked service as a fixed first row (with a Booked badge), a **+ Add Service** button that appends rows with a service dropdown (each dropdown hides services already on another row; the button disables while a row is still empty or every service is used), an × to remove an added row, a Qty box for per-unit services, and a price box that always starts blank (booked row included) for the dentist to type; list prices are only estimates. Price and Qty can't go negative (minus / plus / e keys blocked, a negative paste is made positive, Qty floors at 1). Line amounts, Total Charges, the reservation-fee credit and Balance to Cashier update live. The button reads **Proceed to Payment**. Submit is blocked for an empty row or a price of ₱0 or less; `api/treatments.php` also rejects a duplicate service or a price ≤ 0. The assistant list now loads in parallel, so the pop-up opens immediately (about 50 ms) and the Assisting Staff picker fills in when ready. A visit with no booked service starts with one empty row.

### Split payment priority & installment rule (migration 069, 2026-10-11, adviser feedback)

- **Split** on the cashier Billing screen fills in order **Cash, then GCash, then Bank Transfer** (`balanceSplit()` in `billing.php`): typing the Cash amount puts the rest of the balance into GCash (Bank 0); lowering GCash puts what is left into Bank. Amounts are capped at the balance, and a field locks at 0 once the fields above it cover the bill. The split fields are labelled Cash / Cash Handed Over / GCash / Bank Transfer (no numbering). **Cash Handed Over** is the cash actually given (blank = same as Cash): a summary line shows Split total, "Still to collect" or "✓ Balanced", and Cash due / Handed over / Change. Pay is blocked while the split is short; while the tendered cash is less than the cash amount the Pay button is disabled (warning "Cash Handed Over cannot be less than the cash amount") and re-enabled once fixed, without lifting a duplicate-reference lock. The client sends `split_cash` = the tendered cash, so `api/payments.php action=settle` takes the change out of the cash (unchanged server logic). In Installment the amounts are only capped, not auto-filled. Minus / plus / e keys are blocked in the split and Received fields.
- **Installment only for orthodontic bills.** `services.allows_installment` (migration 069) is 1 only for New Ortho / Braces Installation (the user narrowed it from all 7 orthodontic services, 2026-10-11); admins toggle it per service ("Allow installment payments" on the Services form). `installmentAllowed()` in `api/payments.php` is true when the bill has such a service or is already `partially_paid`; the invoice returns it as `installment_allowed`, the Installment radio is not shown at all otherwise (only Full Payment), and `settle` 422s an installment on any other bill.

**Treatment & Transaction History date picker (2026-10-11, adviser feedback).** The patient page (`modules/patient/dashboard.php`) has a "Show visits from" Year / Month / Day picker above the list. It opens on the current month; Year offers "All years" plus every year with a visit, Month and Day unlock once the level above is picked (Day lists that month's real days), and "This Month" resets it. An "N visits · <label>" count sits beside it, and an empty pick says to choose another month or year. Only the visit list (table and phone cards) is filtered; the Total Visits / Total Billed / Outstanding tiles and the Outstanding Balance banner still cover every visit.

**Reschedule reason prefilled after an on-call decline (2026-10-11, adviser feedback).** When a roaming dentist declined a visit (unassigned, `oncall_declined_at` set), the receptionist Reschedule pop-up starts the Reason box with "Dr. <name> declined the on-call assignment: <reason>" plus a small "Filled in from the dentist's decline reason. You can edit it." note. `action=get` now returns `oncall_declined_by_name` for staff.

**Advance booking is an admin switch (2026-10-11, adviser feedback; changed to 1 day the same day, so `onlineLeadDays()` returns 1 or 0 and the switch reads "Enforce 1-Day Advance Booking"; the rest of this paragraph said 2).** Supersedes the `ONLINE_LEAD_DAYS` constant: Admin Settings > Payment Settings card > **Booking Rules** > "Enforce 2-Day Advance Booking" (`settings.enforce_two_day_advance`, default `'1'`, saved via `api/settings.php action=save_lead_time`, History Log line). `onlineLeadDays()` in `includes/functions.php` returns 2 (on) or 0 (off) and drives `action=availability`'s online floor and `action=create` (now a 400, "Appointments must be booked at least 2 days in advance…"). The patient wizard and Calendar View get it as `__LEAD_DAYS__` (str_replace on `$PAGE_SCRIPT`), so a change shows on the next page load; Calendar View no longer shows the "Online bookings require at least … advance notice" note (removed from the booking wizard and Calendar View 2026-10-11). Walk-ins, staff reschedules and the receptionist calendars are never affected. With the rule off, `BLOCK_PASSED_SLOTS` still refuses an hour that already started today.

**1-hour online cutoff (2026-10-11, adviser feedback).** For the patient role only, `action=availability` closes any slot today whose start is at or before now + 60 min (`onlineCutoffHm()` in `includes/functions.php`, DB clock), returned as `soon` (`{HH:MM:true}`) and labelled "Too soon" in the patient calendars; `day_over` counts them too. `action=create` refuses such a slot with a 400. Only reachable when the 2-day rule is off. Staff calendars (walk-in, receptionist reschedule) are exempt.

**Roaming-decline indicator on rescheduled visits (2026-10-11).** For staff, the shared appointment View modal's "This appointment was rescheduled." callout adds a red "Roaming dentist declined" pill + the dentist's name only (the reason is already the Reschedule reason) whenever `oncall_declined_at` is set (the oncall_* columns are kept after the reschedule). The receptionist Approved Appointments Doctor cell shows an amber "Roaming Dr. <name> declined" line once the visit has a dentist again. Patients never see it.

**Base prices shown again on the public/patient side (2026-10-11).** Supersedes "the patient side shows no service prices at all": the `index.php` service cards show "Starts at ₱X (per <unit>)" under the blurb, the service modal repeats it in a box with "Note: Prices shown are baseline amounts (\"starts at\"). The final cost may vary based on the actual condition, severity, or additional units required as determined by the dentist during treatment.", and the patient Services page cards show the same price + note. Only `base_price` exists (no max), so it is worded as a starting price. The booking wizard service picker shows the shorter "Name · starts at ₱X/<unit>", with the price note as an amber callout (info icon) above the dropdown.

**Booking confirmation card on step 3 (2026-10-12).** The online booking wizard's "Appointment Submitted" step (`submittedModal()` in `modules/patient/appointments.php`) shows a solid navy summary card (`.submitted-card` in `includes/patient_booking_style.php`, an outline calendar SVG `ICON_CAL_CARD` + Name: / Branch: Bulacan / Service: / Time: (a one-hour range via `visitRange()`) / Date:, labels and values in two aligned columns) in place of the old Patient / Service / Date / Contact list. Only the notes block below is centred (its numbered list left-aligned). A wider two-column, no-scroll version was tried and reverted at the user's request, and under the auto-check box a Note list (time may adjust, minors need a guardian, new ortho patients bring one valid ID), two blue uppercase lines (take a screenshot; arrive on time or tell us) and a thank-you line. Copied from the clinic's existing booking confirmation, but the pending-approval status, badge and 5 s poll stay.

**Booking wizard Concern is optional (2026-10-11).** Labelled "Concern (optional)"; it is its own guided step after the date + time (focus and scroll move there once the time is confirmed), sharing the step with Continue to Payment, which has `noLock` so it stays usable with the box left blank.

**Booking wizard service picker is a custom list (2026-10-11).** A native `<option>` cannot colour part of its text, so `#b_service` stays as an `sr-only` select (all form logic reads it) and the shared `window.svcPickerHtml(id, services, selectedId, inputCls, placeholder)` + `window.wireSvcPicker(id)` in `app.js` draw `#<id>Btn` + `#<id>List` (role=listbox, arrow keys / Enter / Escape): name on the left, grey "starts at" + the price in green (`text-ok`) on the right. The guided-form step pulses `#<id>Btn`. Also used by the receptionist Walk-in modal (`w_service`).

**Upload step scrolls to the reference box (2026-10-11).** `guideForm` fields accept `reveal` (a later element to bring into view as well, while keeping the field's own top visible); `uploadField()` reveals the `#p_ref` block, so after picking GCash / Bank the payment step (and Resubmit Payment) scrolls down to show the upload box and the reference number together.

**Walk-in guided steps fixed (2026-10-11).** Picking an existing patient now moves focus on (the step has `auto: true`); no payment method is pre-selected (Cash used to be checked, so the Payment Method step was skipped), picking one moves on (Cash to Service, GCash / Bank to the reference box), and Book & Schedule toasts "Choose a payment method." if none.

**Guided forms scroll the next field to the top (2026-10-11).** `focusField()` in `guideForm` now always scrolls the pop-up so the next field sits 12px below the top of the scroll box (clamped at the bottom), even when it is already on screen, instead of the old "nearest" scroll. The `reveal` option is no longer needed for this.

**Walk-in Concern is optional too (2026-10-11).** Same as online: "Concern (optional)" with a placeholder, its own guided step after Assign Dentist (focus + scroll move there once the time is confirmed and a dentist is set), sharing the step with Book & Schedule (`noLock`).

**Walk-in modes keep their own payment + service (2026-10-11).** Besides the date + time, `setMode()` now saves and restores the payment method, reference no., service and concern per mode (`modeForm`, `readForm()` / `writeForm()`), so switching to New patient starts those guided steps fresh instead of showing Existing patient's picks as already done.

**Sign-up phone field is back, required fields marked (2026-10-11).** The phone field was removed and then restored the same day at the user's request: `login.php` Create Account requires Phone Number again (`isPhMobile()` server check, `phoneOk()` client gate). Required fields show a red `*`: a `.req-mark` span after First Name / Last Name / Email / Phone / Password / Confirm Password, placed by JS just after the placeholder text (canvas text measure, re-run on resize / font load) and hidden once the field has a value; Birthdate's label and the Terms checkbox line carry a red `*` too. `action=create` still saves a self-booking's phone to `users.contact_number` when the account has none.
