Sizing (`#regForm` vh clamps) was enlarged again after the middle name was removed (inputs `2.6vh - 6.5px` padding, `1.8vh - 3.5px` gap, room left for the one-line phone error, logo `7.6vh`, form 500px wide) and still fits 1280x600 through 1920x960 without a scrollbar.

**Middle name removed everywhere (2026-10-11).** Supersedes every "First / Middle (optional) / Last" mention above. No form asks for a middle name any more: sign-up, admin Add / Edit User and Edit Assistant, dentist + receptionist + Create Record, the walk-in New patient fields, and the user's own Profile. Every API that saved one (`api/users.php`, `records.php`, `profile.php`, `assistants.php`, `login.php`) now sets `$middle = ''`, `personName()` ignores its `$middle` argument (so `full_name` is "First Last"), and the invoice Sold To prints "Last, First". The existing middle names were cleared (`middle_name = NULL`, `full_name` rebuilt) on local (3 rows) and live (8 rows). The column stays, and backups of the old values are in the session scratchpad. Old `appointments.patient_name` snapshots were not changed.

**Faster receipt scan (2026-10-11).** `assets/js/vendor/tesseract/lang-data/eng.traineddata.gz` is now the tessdata_fast English model (~2 MB, was the ~11 MB 4.0.0 model; benchmarked on real GCash receipts: same reference / amount reads, ~30% quicker). In `modules/patient/appointments.php`: the worker lives on `window.__dgtTessWorker` (one per tab, survives nav.js re-runs; a failed load is forgotten so the next upload retries), `cachePath: 'dgt-ocr-fast-v1'` keeps the model in IndexedDB, the engine warms up on page idle (skipped on Save-Data / 2G), `recognize()` asks for text only (no hOCR / TSV / blocks), and if the engine still isn't ready after `OCR_LOAD_WAIT_MS` (15 s, slow connection / server) the scan gives up and the reference box says "We couldn't read your screenshot right now. Please type the reference number." so Submit isn't held back. The old 11 MB model is backed up in the session scratchpad (`fastdl/eng.traineddata.gz`).

**Performance pass 1: first-visit weight of the public pages (2026-10-11).** Measured on a simulated slow mobile connection (1.6 Mbps, 150 ms RTT). Landing page went from first paint 7.4 s / usable 9.6 s / load never finished in 12 s to 1.8 s / 1.9 s / 3.8 s; login page load 3.6 s to 2.0 s. Changes: `assets/img/pic_login-sm.webp` (720 px WebP, 50 KB) replaces the 2000 px, 1.8 MB `pic_login.png` on login / reset_password / verify; `dgtbulacanbranch-web.jpg` (92 KB, was 733 KB) and `process-bg-web.jpg` (1400 px, 166 KB, was 438 KB) on `index.php`, whose below-the-fold images (process backdrop, branch photo, team photos, CTA photo, footer flower) are now `loading="lazy" decoding="async"`. The login `<video>`s are `preload="none"` with no `autoplay`: a small script starts the 8.6 MB clip only after `window.load` (+300 ms), never on Save-Data / 2G / 3G or reduced motion (the poster stays), and buffers the crossfade standby copy 3 s after the first one plays. Originals are kept. Measured but not yet fixed (next passes): the Tailwind Play CDN (render-blocking third-party script, about 0.45 s of in-browser CSS compile per full page load at 4x CPU), and the per-page inline CSS/config + unminified `app.js` (141 KB). Database/API time is not a bottleneck at this size (5 to 16 ms per call, calls already run in parallel).

**SEO basics (2026-10-11).** `robots.txt` (repo root = live site root) blocks `/modules/`, `/api/`, `/config/`, `/includes/`, `/logs/`, `/migrations/`, `/uploads/receipts/`, `/uploads/avatars/`, verify / reset / change-password / logout, and points at `sitemap.xml` (home, login, terms, privacy, live URLs hard-coded). `includes/seo_meta.php` `seoMeta($title, $desc, $path, $index = true)` prints the meta description, robots, canonical, Open Graph and Twitter tags; used by `login.php`, `terms.php` / `privacy.php`, and `verify.php` + `reset_password.php` (those two `noindex`). Link previews use `assets/img/og-image.jpg` (1200x630, logo card + name on DGT navy), also now on `index.php` (`$ogImageAbs`). A phone-only sticky "Book an Appointment" bar (`#mCta`) was added the same day and then removed at the user's request; don't re-add it.

**Footer social dock (2026-10-11).** Facebook / Instagram / TikTok are `.soc-dock` circle buttons under the footer brand column's description (moved there from the bottom bar the same day) (56px, 50px on phones, inline SVG icons). Each `.soc-btn` holds a `.soc-face` circle with `overflow:hidden` whose `::before` brand layer (`--soc-bg`) slides up from `translateY(101%)` to 0 on hover / keyboard focus (`cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)`, .45s), clipped by the round edge (the resting outline is an inset box-shadow, not a border, so a filled circle shows no seam or ring); the icon turns white, lifts and scales, and a label pill (outside the clip) springs up above (`cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1)`). Timing knobs `--soc-fill` / `--soc-pop` / `--soc-lift` on `.soc-btn`. Colours: Facebook #1877f2; Instagram its real 45deg gradient (#f09433 to #bc1888, asked for explicitly, an exception to the no-gradient rule); TikTok #010101 with a soft cyan #00f2fe / magenta #fe0979 glow underneath (a sideways ring glow read as a line on the edge, removed) and a plain white icon (the cyan / magenta split on the icon read as a line beside it, removed) (`.soc-tiktok`). Reduced motion keeps only the colour change.
**Footer bottom bar (2026-10-11):** copyright on the left, Terms & Conditions + Privacy Policy on the right, ending flush with the line (where the social icons were before they moved under the brand description). From 768px up `#toTop` floats up (`body.foot-on`, set by an IntersectionObserver on `#footBar`) while the bar is on screen; on phones both lines are centred and the links get `mb-16` so the button never covers them.

**Clean URLs, no ".php" (2026-10-11).** `.htaccess`: `/home` serves `index.php` (and `/index.php` 301s there); any other GET for `page.php` 301s to `/page` (query string kept); `/page` is served by `page.php` when that file exists. POSTs and `/api/` calls are never redirected (a redirect would drop a POST body); both forms work for them. Every first-party page link, `roleHome()`, the sidebar `$navByRole` hrefs, form actions, header/notification links, `seoMeta()` paths, e-mail links, `robots.txt` and `sitemap.xml` now use the extensionless form (`/dgt_dental_clinic/modules/patient/appointments`, `/home` on live). Write new page links without `.php`. Do not touch `require` paths or the `SCRIPT_NAME` check in `requirePasswordChanged()`: `SCRIPT_NAME` is still the real `.php` file after the rewrite.
The receptionist's home page (sidebar "Appointments") is served at `/modules/receptionist/appointments`: an `.htaccess` alias to the unchanged `modules/receptionist/dashboard.php`, and `/modules/receptionist/dashboard(.php)` 301s there. `roleHome('receptionist')`, the sidebar and `RCP_DASH` in `app.js` use the new address.
Likewise the dentist's "My Schedule" page is served at `/modules/dentist/myschedule` (alias to the unchanged `modules/dentist/dashboard.php`; `/modules/dentist/dashboard(.php)` 301s there). `roleHome('dentist')`, the sidebar and the on-call e-mail link in `api/appointments.php` use it.

**Receipt checks switch (2026-10-14).** `RECEIPT_CHECKS` at the top of `modules/patient/appointments.php`'s page script. It is `true` (all the upload checks above run). It was briefly set to `false` the same day: then the upload only previews the screenshot, with no checks, no warning box, no reference auto-fill and no Submit block.

**Forgot Password rejects unknown e-mails (2026-10-15).** Supersedes the "always returns the same generic message (no account enumeration)" rule above. `api/request_password_reset.php` now returns `{ok:false}` with "No account is registered with this e-mail address." (404) for an unregistered or archived e-mail, and "Please enter a valid e-mail address." (422) for a bad format; `login.php`'s Forgot Password modal shows the error under the field and stays on step 1 (the resend button shows it too). Chosen by the user knowing it reveals which e-mails have accounts.
The same day `api/request_verification.php` (verify page + sign-up "Resend it") got the same treatment: bad format 422, no active patient 404, already verified 409 with `verified:true` (verify.php adds a Sign in link). Also, a code e-mail that fails to send on a real host (not localhost, where `dev_otp` is still returned) is now reported instead of claimed as sent: `MAIL_SEND_FAILED_MSG` in `includes/mailer.php`, a 503 from request_password_reset / request_verification / resend_email_otp, and `mail_failed` from update_pending_email and verify_email (the Add User / walk-in OTP widget shows it and doesn't cache the result).

**GCash reference locked to the screenshot, one screenshot per booking (migration 070, 2026-10-17).** While the screenshot upload is on (and `RECEIPT_CHECKS` is true), the GCash Reference No. box in the booking wizard and Resubmit Payment is greyed out and read-only (`refLocked()` in `modules/patient/appointments.php`). A new upload clears it, and it is filled only from a screenshot that passed every check; otherwise the note asks for a clearer screenshot (or to upload again if the reader couldn't load). Bank references stay typed (bank receipts vary too much to read). Server side, `validateReservationPayment()` stores the receipt image's SHA-256 in `payments.receipt_hash` and refuses (409) an image another payment already used (`receiptImageInUse()`, which hashes older rows from their files the first time it runs; a resubmission skips its own row). Reported case: reusing a screenshot and changing one digit of the reference used to pass.

**Bank Transfer QR code, admin-editable (2026-10-17).** Admin Settings > Payment Settings has a **Bank Transfer Details** block under GCash Details, built the same way: a QR preview with Upload New QR / Remove QR (`api/settings.php upload_payment_qr` / `clear_payment_qr` now take `method=bank`, stored as `settings.pay_bank_qr`, returned as `bank_qr` by `payment_info`) and Bank Name / Account Name / Account Number fields that save the 3 lines of `pay_bank_details`. The QR JS in `modules/admin/settings.php` is one `wireQr(method, prefix)` for both. The patient booking wizard's Bank Transfer panel shows the QR above the account box. Current values (local + live): the clinic's MariBank InstaPay QR (`uploads/payment/qr_bank_maribank.png` on live), MariBank / Claire Mennin Tagon / ****4850, replacing the old demo BPI account.

**Patient booking guide no longer scrolls (2026-10-17).** `guideForm(root, bar, steps, opts)` takes `opts.noScroll`: the next step still gets the pulsing ring (and focus, with `preventScroll`), but the form is never scrolled to it. Used by the patient booking wizard (step 1), the payment step and Resubmit Payment. The receptionist walk-in modal still scrolls.

**Patient Appointments: status dropdown, date filter, 6 per page (2026-10-17).** Supersedes the "`.tab-row` (All / Pending / …)" tab buttons on `modules/patient/appointments.php`: a **Status** `<select>` (`#apptStatus`, each option shows its count, payment-declined still counts under Pending) and a **Date** input (`#apptDate`, shows that day's appointments only), with a **Clear filters** button once either is set. Cards show 6 per page up to 3 columns, and 2 full rows when a wide screen fits 4+ columns (8, 10, ...; `perPage()` mirrors `.appt-grid`'s 340px auto-fill, and a ResizeObserver re-pages on resize / sidebar collapse, keeping the first card in view), so the last row never has empty spots; `#apptPager` under the grid has "Showing 1–6 of N", Previous / page numbers (first, last, and the ones next to the current page) / Next, and is hidden when one page is enough. Changing a filter goes back to page 1; a reload after an action keeps the page (clamped).

**Slots left on the booking calendars (2026-10-17).** Reverses the earlier "no text on an open day" rule: every bookable date shows "N slots left" / "1 slot left" (`info.open` from `action=availability`, i.e. how many time slots that day still have a free chair), in `cellInfo()` of `modules/patient/calendar.php`, the patient booking / reschedule calendar (`modules/patient/appointments.php`) and the receptionist walk-in / reschedule calendar (`modules/receptionist/dashboard.php`). Fully Booked and Closed are unchanged. Below 640px the day tags stay hidden, as before.

**Treatment & Transaction History is paginated (2026-10-17).** `modules/patient/dashboard.php` shows 10 visits per page (`PER_PAGE`) in both the desktop table and the phone/tablet cards, with the same pager as My Appointments (`#histPager`: "Showing 1–10 of N", Previous / page numbers / Next, hidden when one page is enough). Changing the Year / Month / Day picker or "This Month" goes back to page 1.

**Patient health & emergency info, profile-first login (migration 071, 2026-10-17).** `patient_records.allergies VARCHAR(500)` (NULL = not answered, "None" = no allergies). My Profile (`modules/shared/profile.php`, patients only) has a **Health & Emergency Information** card: Date of Birth (the shared DOB picker with `swap`, so it shows the Age), Emergency Contact Name + Number (PH mobile) and Allergies, all required, saved by `api/profile.php action=health` (upserts `patient_records`). `patientProfileMissing($uid)` (functions.php) lists what is still blank (Contact Number, Date of Birth, Emergency Contact, Allergies); while anything is missing, a patient's sign-in (`login.php`, and the post-registration auto sign-in in `api/verify_otp.php`, via `patientLandingUrl()`) lands on My Profile, which shows a "Please complete your profile" box naming the missing items and outlines those fields. Once complete, sign-in goes to Appointments as before. Allergies are clinical (in `api/records.php` `$TEXT_FIELDS`, so hidden from the receptionist / assistant): the dentist sees them as a red alert at the top of Patient Details (editable Allergies field, plus an Emergency Contact box), the shared appointment View and the Services Availed pop-up (`window.allergyAlertHtml()` in app.js: red for real allergies, green "none reported", amber "not provided yet, ask before treatment"). `attachPatientAllergies()` (`api/_bootstrap.php`) adds `patient_allergies` (the treated person's) to `action=list` / `action=get` rows for dentist and admin only.

**Date of Birth moved to Personal Information, locked once saved (2026-10-17).** On My Profile the patient's Date of Birth now sits in the Personal Information card (beside Role), not the Health & Emergency card. Once a birthdate is on file it shows only as the Age ("23 years old", label "Age") with no Change link. While none is on file the Month / Day / Year dropdowns show, with a note that only the clinic can change it later, and Save Changes saves it (`api/profile.php action=update` sets `patient_records.date_of_birth` only when it is still empty; a later value is ignored). `action=health` no longer reads or writes the birthdate. Staff can still correct it from Patient Details / Edit Patient.

**Click outside a filled-in pop-up keeps it open (2026-10-17).** `openModal()` in `app.js` tracks whether the user has typed or picked anything in the dialog (trusted `input` / `change` events, plus clicks on a calendar day, a custom-list option or a payment card) in `#modalRoot.dataset.dirty`. While dirty, a click on the dark backdrop no longer closes the pop-up: the dialog just gives a small shake (the "Your entries are kept" toast was removed at the user's request) An untouched pop-up still closes on a backdrop click, and X / Cancel / Back / Escape always close. Applies to every pop-up in the app (booking wizard, walk-in, reschedule, Add User, etc.).

**Receptionist Patient Details: one Age field (2026-10-17).** The editable birthdate (shown as "Age", the DOB picker with `swap`) moved from Contact Information into Personal Information beside Name, replacing the plain Age box that duplicated it. Contact Information is now Contact Number / Email Address, then Address across both columns. Edit Patient Info still shows the Change link to correct the birthdate.

**Age on every role's My Profile (migration 072, 2026-10-17).** `users.date_of_birth DATE NULL` holds the birthdate for staff roles (patients keep using `patient_records.date_of_birth`). My Profile's Personal Information shows Date of Birth beside Role for every role: the Month / Day / Year dropdowns until one is saved (Save Changes, `api/profile.php action=update`, written only while still empty), then a locked "Age" box with no Change link. Required (red `*`) only for patients. Staff birthdates have no other editor yet.

**Staff are 30+, and Add User asks for a Date of Birth (2026-10-17).** `STAFF_MIN_AGE = 30` + `staffBirthDate()` in `includes/functions.php` (validBirthDate plus the age check, "Staff must be at least 30 years old."). Admin Users > Add (admin, dentist, receptionist, cashier, assistant) now has a required Date of Birth "(30 years or older)" saved to `users.date_of_birth` (`api/users.php action=add`, `api/assistants.php action=add`); Edit User / Edit Assistant Profile have it optional (blank keeps the saved one), so the admin can correct a staff birthdate. `api/users.php` list returns `date_of_birth` / `age` from patient_records for patients, else users. The DOB picker takes `opts.minAge` (`dobSelectsHtml`): the Year list starts at this year minus minAge and a younger date never counts as picked; staff My Profile uses it too (patients have no minimum).

**5 patients an hour: 4 in-house + 1 roaming, enforced everywhere (2026-10-17).** `dentistHourCap()` in `api/appointments.php`: an in-house dentist may hold at most `slotCapacity() - 1` (4) bookings in one hour while a roaming dentist is on duty then (all 5 only when none is); a roaming dentist's cap is 5. Used by `assertDentistNotOverloaded()` (Approve / Reassign / reschedule) and `action=dentists` (`slot_cap`, `slot_full`), so the receptionist picker shows the in-house dentist as Full at 4. `action=reschedule` now refuses a full hour for staff too (no override any more), and moving a visit into an hour where its in-house dentist already has 4 re-routes it to the roaming dentist via `assignAppointmentDentist()` (follow-up plan visits keep their dentist). Before this, reschedules could leave the in-house dentist with all 5 (live had 5 on Oct 15, 4:00 PM).

**Update (2026-10-17):** the Date of Birth label in Add / Edit User and Edit Assistant Profile is just "Date of Birth *" (the "(30 years or older)" note was removed at the user's request; the Year list and server still enforce 30+). It is now required on Edit too (client + `api/users.php action=update` + `api/assistants.php action=update`), so editing an older staff account needs its birthdate filled in.

**Own details greyed out on "For Myself" and existing walk-ins (2026-10-17).** Booking wizard step 1 (`lockSelf()` in `modules/patient/appointments.php`): with "For Myself", First Name, Last Name and Phone Number are filled from the account and read-only (grey, `!bg-dgt-ink-50`); the phone stays editable only when the account has none. "For Someone Else" unlocks and clears them. Walk-in modal (`lockPhone()` in `modules/receptionist/dashboard.php`): picking an existing patient with a number on file fills and locks the phone; Change / New patient unlocks and clears it.


**My Profile required fields (2026-10-17).** Red stars on First Name, Last Name, Email, Date of Birth / Age (every role) and Contact Number (patient, receptionist, cashier, assistant). `api/profile.php action=update` now requires a valid e-mail for everyone and a contact number for those four roles; the page blocks Save Changes until they and a missing birthdate are filled in. Username and Role are read-only, so no star.

**Pagination on the receptionist tables and Patient Records (2026-10-17).** Shared `window.pageSlice(list, state, perPage, nav, onChange)` in `app.js` (returns `{rows, from}`, draws the "Showing 1–10 of N" + Previous / numbers / Next bar into a `<nav>`, hidden when one page is enough). Used at 10 per page by Appointments for Approval (`#af_pager`) and Approved Appointments (`#sf_pager`) on the receptionist page (a filter change goes back to page 1, a reload after an action keeps the page), and by Patient Records (`#recPager`) for the receptionist, dentist and assistant (a new search goes back to page 1).

**Approved Appointments order (2026-10-17).** `sortSched()` in `modules/receptionist/dashboard.php`: today's visits first (by time), then upcoming days (soonest first), then past days still open (most recent first).

**Patient Records fill big screens (2026-10-17).** On 1536px+ screens the records table uses 14.5px text and taller rows (`2xl:` classes on `$tableCls`). Rows per page are no longer a flat 10: `window.fitRows(table)` in `app.js` works out how many rows fit below the table on the current screen (minimum 10), re-fitted on resize (`window.__recFit`). Receptionist, dentist and assistant Patient Records.

**Approval queue: Approve / Decline inside the receipt (2026-10-17).** Appointments for Approval has no Receipt column any more. A row with a receipt screenshot shows only **View** in Actions; the receipt pop-up's footer holds Close / Decline / Approve, so staff look at the screenshot first. A row without one (screenshot upload turned off) shows Approve + Decline directly, with "No screenshot, verify Ref. No." above them. The Approve pop-up no longer has the "Amount Received" field: `action=confirm` is sent without `reservation_amount`, so the recorded fee stays as booked (`payments.receipt_amount_read` is still written, nothing shows it).

**Patient Details appointment history is paginated (2026-10-17).** The Appointment History table inside the Patient Details pop-up (receptionist, dentist, assistant Patient Records) shows 5 visits per page via `window.pageSlice()` (`#histBody` + `#histPager`, `drawHist()`).

**Walk-in guide no longer scrolls (2026-10-17).** The receptionist Walk-in Booking modal's `guideForm` now passes `{ noScroll: true }` like the patient booking forms: the pulsing ring marks the next step, the modal is never auto-scrolled.

**Missed visits can't be rescheduled again (2026-10-17).** Reverts the 2026-10-12 "Missed visits can be rescheduled by the patient" change: `action=reschedule` accepts only pending / approved / rescheduled and refuses a patient on any past visit; the patient Appointments page hides `no_show` rows (like past cancellations) and shows no Reschedule on a missed visit; the "Missed appointment" notification pop-up has no Reschedule button (the `data-pt-resched` / `dgt_resched_appt` / `window.__ptResched` hand-off was removed); both missed-visit notification texts now say "Please contact the clinic or book a new appointment online." The receptionist's No Show button is unchanged. The 3 existing live No Show visits (#785, #787, #788, walk-ins) were deleted with their payment + notification rows (activity log kept; backup in the session scratchpad `noshow_backup.txt`); local had none.

**Services Availed shows the starting price (2026-10-17).** In `logProcedureModal()` each service row shows a small grey "Starts at ₱X (per unit)" line under the name (from `services.base_price`, updated when an added row's service changes); the add-row dropdown lists names only. Reference only: the Amount box still starts blank for the dentist to type.

**Dark-mode dropdown lists (2026-10-17).** `tailwind.php` now gives every `<option>` / `<optgroup>` a dark list (`#16263c` / `#e9edf1`, disabled `#8a98a4`) in dark mode, since a transparent dark `<select>` made Chrome draw the list white with light text. A zero-specificity `:where(textarea, select, input…)` rule also makes form boxes without dark: classes transparent instead of the browser's grey fill. The Services Availed Consultation Notes box got its own dark classes.

**Dentist My Schedule: Queue only, paginated (2026-10-17).** The Recently Completed card (and its `loadDone()` / billing-badge code) was removed from `modules/dentist/dashboard.php`. The Queue (today's schedule) shows 10 patients per page via `window.pageSlice()` (`#queuePager`, `renderQueue()`). Completed visits are still in the patient's Patient Details history.

**Tooth chart updates from logged procedures (2026-10-18).** In the Services Availed pop-up (`logProcedureModal()` in `app.js`), Extraction, Resto and Root Canal rows show an FDI tooth picker (permanent 18-48, plus a Baby teeth toggle for 51-85; a picked tooth fills blue via `.svc-tooth[aria-pressed="true"]` in `tailwind.php`, since the CDN build has no `aria-pressed:` variant). At least one tooth is required for those rows. Each line sends `teeth`; `api/treatments.php` validates them (`isFdiTooth()`), marks them on the treated patient's `patient_records.tooth_chart` (`toothChartEffect()` in `api/_bootstrap.php`: Extraction = `missing`, Resto / Root Canal = `cured`; other teeth on the chart are kept) in the same transaction, and adds "Extraction (Bunot): teeth 16, 26" to the visit's Treatment notes. For per-tooth services (Extraction, Root Canal) the Qty is the number of teeth picked (locked); Resto is per tooth surface, so its Qty stays typed. Other per-unit services (Veneers, Bridge, X-ray, …) have no chart marking and no picker.

**Tooth statuses follow the services (migration 073, 2026-10-18).** The dentist Patient Details chart states are now `resto` (R, "Resto / Pasta", blue), `rct` (RCT, "Root Canal", green) and `missing` (X, "Extracted", red), replacing Light-cured (LC) / Cured (C) / Missing (M). Logging Resto marks `resto`, Root Canal marks `rct`, Extraction marks `missing` (`toothChartEffect()` + `toothEffect()` in app.js). `TOOTH_STATES` in `api/records.php`, `CYCLE` / `TOOTH_LABEL` in `modules/dentist/records.php`, `.t-*` / `.sw-*` in `includes/records_chart_style.php`. Migration 073 turned old `lc` / `cured` marks into `resto` (1 chart on live, none local).

**Tooth chart redrawn for non-dentists (2026-10-18).** `modules/dentist/records.php` `toothBoard()` now draws each tooth as an SVG shape by type (`TOOTH_SHAPE`: incisor / canine / premolar / molar; `toothInfo()` derives type, jaw and side from the FDI number), upper teeth flipped so roots point up. Status is drawn on the tooth: Resto = blue filling on the crown, Root Canal = green line down the root, Extracted = dashed outline + red X (plus the R / RCT / X tag). Two sections (Adult teeth, Baby teeth (children)) with Upper / Lower jaw labels, "Patient's right / left" (hidden on phones), a Molars / Premolars / Canine / Front caption between the jaws (hidden on phones), a hover/aria title like "Tooth 16: upper right first molar · Resto / Pasta (filled)", an edit hint, and a legend in plain words. CSS in `includes/records_chart_style.php` (`.tooth-svg .tb/.tf/.tr/.tx`, `.tooth-sec`, `.tooth-band`), dark mode included.

**Services Availed tooth picker uses the same drawings (2026-10-18).** `window.toothInfo(t)` / `window.toothSvg(t, status)` now live in `app.js` (moved out of `modules/dentist/records.php`, which aliases them), and the `.tooth-svg` colour rules moved to `includes/tailwind.php`. In `logProcedureModal()` each tooth button shows its shape + number, rows are labelled Upper / Lower with "Patient's right / left" (hidden on phones, where each jaw half wraps as a block), and the header just says "Click the tooth / teeth you treated". A picked tooth (`aria-pressed`) shows that result drawn on it and tints by effect (`.fx-resto/.fx-rct/.fx-missing`), replacing the old solid blue fill.

**Service durations are shown again (migration 074, 2026-10-18).** Reverses the 2026-09 "duration is not shown anywhere" rule, at the user's request. `services.duration_minutes` is the usual time per visit, or per unit for a per-unit service. Admin Services has a "Usual Time" column and a "Usual Duration (minutes)" field (5 to 480; `api/services.php action=save` validates and saves it). Patients see "Usual time: about 1 hr" (with "per tooth" etc. for per-unit services) on the patient Services cards, the `index.php` service cards and modal, and in the booking / walk-in service picker list. Text comes from `fmtDuration()` (functions.php) / `window.fmtDuration()` (app.js). Migration 074 set starting estimates for all 21 services (Consult 30, Cleaning 45, Braces install 90, X-ray 15, ...); the admin can change them.

**Admin Services: Usual Time cell + Category filter (2026-10-18).** The Usual Time column now has its cell ("30 min", with "per tooth" etc. on a small second line). A Category `<select>` (`#svcCat`) next to the search box lists only categories an active service is in, with counts ("General Dentist (8)"), and filters the table together with the name search; `#svcCount` shows "N of M shown" while a filter is on.

**Admin Appointments: no wrapped badges / dates (2026-10-18).** On laptop widths the Type badge broke into "Walk- / In" and the date split onto two lines: the page's own `BADGE` const lacked `whitespace-nowrap`. It has it now (Type + Status badges), and the Date and Phone cells are `whitespace-nowrap`. At 1366px the table still fits without a horizontal scroll (only long patient names wrap).

**No "Change" link on a saved birthdate (2026-10-18).** `dobSelectsHtml(..., { swap: true })` no longer renders the `_chg` Change button: once a Date of Birth is on file, every form (admin Edit Patient / Edit User / Edit Assistant, receptionist + dentist Patient Details, walk-in, My Profile) shows only the locked Age box. `wireDobSelects()` switches to the Age view whenever `_agev` exists; `lockDobSelects()` no longer toggles a Change link. A wrong birthdate now has to be fixed in the database.

**Staff minimum age is now 18 (2026-10-18).** `STAFF_MIN_AGE` changed from 30 to 18 in `includes/functions.php` and `modules/admin/users.php` (the Year list in Add / Edit User, Edit Assistant and staff My Profile now starts 18 years back; `staffBirthDate()` rejects anyone younger with "Staff must be at least 18 years old."). Supersedes the "Staff are 30+" note above.

**Walk-in new patients: ages 1 to 80 (2026-10-18).** In the Walk-in Booking modal's "New patient" mode the Date of Birth Year list only covers ages 1 to 80, (no hint text; `setDobRange()` in app.js rebuilds the Year list; `dobSelectsHtml` gained `opts.maxAge` / `data-maxage`). `api/records.php action=create` enforces it server-side when `walk_in` is sent (`walkInBirthDate()` + `WALKIN_MIN_AGE` / `WALKIN_MAX_AGE` in functions.php): "A new walk-in patient must be 1 to 80 years old." Existing patients and the + Create Record modals keep the full range.

**Account holders are 18 to 80 (2026-10-18).** Sign-up (`login.php`: Year list 2008 back to age 81's year, plus a server check "Account holders must be 18 to 80 years old.") and every account the admin adds (all roles, patients included) or a staff member enters on My Profile now use the same range. `staffBirthDate()` checks `STAFF_MIN_AGE` (18) to `ACCOUNT_MAX_AGE` (80): "The account holder must be 18 to 80 years old." Edits use `editBirthDate($dob, $saved)`: a birthdate already on file stays valid even outside the range (it is locked in the forms anyway), only a newly entered one must be 18 to 80 (`api/users.php action=update`, `api/assistants.php` update). Client: `dobField()` in `modules/admin/users.php` applies `minAge 18 / maxAge 80` only while the field is empty; staff My Profile passes `__MAX_AGE__`. Walk-in new patients stay 1 to 80, + Create Record and patient My Profile have no limit.

**Senior citizen / PWD discount + name rules (migration 075, 2026-10-18).**
- **Cashier Billing:** ticking the SC/PWD discount shows a "Discount For" select. **Senior Citizen (60+)** is offered only when the patient's live age (`patient_age`) is 60 or more; otherwise only **PWD** shows and the checkbox reads "Less: PWD Discount (20%)". Senior asks for the OSCA / Senior Citizen ID No. (required, 3 to 20 letters / digits / hyphens; OSCA numbers are set by each city, no national format) and an optional Sr. Citizen TIN (BIR format, 9 digits + optional 3- or 5-digit branch: 123-456-789[-000|-00000], auto-dashed). PWD asks for the PWD ID No. in the DOH/NCDA registry format RR-PPMM-BBB-NNNNNNN (e.g. 13-7405-000-0001234; older 3-digit sequence accepted), auto-dashed. `api/payments.php action=settle` re-checks all of it (`validTin()`, `validPwdId()`, `validOscaId()`, `SC_MIN_AGE` in functions.php) and stores `payments.sc_pwd_type` ('senior' / 'pwd') + `sc_tin` (migration 075; `sc_pwd_id` holds the OSCA or PWD ID). The printed invoice puts the TIN on its TIN line and the ID in "SC/PWD … ID #".
- **Names:** first / last names may only use letters (accents, ñ), spaces, hyphens, apostrophes and periods, start with a letter, 2 to 50 characters. Typing filter: `window.cleanName()` + a delegated input listener in app.js on inputs whose id ends in `_first` / `_last` (and `u_rfirst` / `u_rlast`) plus the invoice "Received from" (`#f_from`, which may also have a comma); `login.php` sign-up has its own copy. Server: `validPersonName()` / `PERSON_NAME_MSG` in functions.php, checked in `login.php` register, `api/users.php` add + update, `api/records.php` create, `api/profile.php` update, `api/assistants.php`, and `createUnlinkedPatientRecord()` (booking for someone else).

**Update (2026-10-18): no age limit on the SC/PWD discount.** At the user's request, the cashier "Discount For" select always offers both Senior Citizen and PWD (Senior is the default), and the checkbox always reads "Less: SC/PWD Discount (20%)". `api/payments.php action=settle` no longer checks the patient's age. The ID / TIN format checks stay. `SC_MIN_AGE` is still defined in functions.php but nothing uses it.
The cashier Billing screen now always shows an "SC / PWD Discount" select (None / Senior Citizen / PWD) under Payment Date, kept in sync with the "Less: SC/PWD Discount (20%)" checkbox: picking Senior / PWD ticks it and shows that ID field, None unticks it, and ticking the box with None picked selects Senior. Before, the select and ID fields only appeared after the checkbox was ticked, which users missed.
**Update (2026-10-18, later): the 60+ rule is back for Senior only.** Supersedes "no age limit" above. The SC / PWD Discount select always shows None + PWD; "Senior Citizen (60+)" is added only when the patient's live age is 60 or more (`seniorOk()`), and the checkbox reads "Less: PWD Discount (20%)" otherwise. Ticking the box with None picked selects Senior (60+) or PWD (under 60). `api/payments.php action=settle` again refuses a senior discount under `SC_MIN_AGE`.

**Main admin can't be archived (2026-10-18).** `ownerAdminId()` (functions.php) = the lowest active admin user_id (local admin.demo, live raffy.tamayo2). `api/users.php action=toggle_active` refuses archiving yourself (422) and the main admin (403, "The main admin account cannot be archived."); any admin can still archive other admins. `action=list` returns `me_id` + `owner_admin_id`, and the Admins tab simply has no Archive button on those rows (no label; the user rejected a "Main admin" / "You" tag). Reset Password is not restricted.

**Transaction page: no OR number under the patient name (2026-10-18).** At the user's request the small grey "OR-000548" line under each name in Patient Transactions was removed. The search box still matches OR numbers.
**Billing: Received from + Phone Number are read-only (2026-10-18).** `#f_from` and `#f_phone` on the cashier Billing screen are `readonly` (grey like Date), filled from the booking. The invoice's Sold To therefore always comes from the patient record.

**Patient booking limits (2026-10-18).** `PATIENT_MAX_ACTIVE = 3` and `ONLINE_MAX_MONTHS = 6` in `includes/functions.php` (`patientActiveBookings()`, `onlineLatestDate()`). A patient account may hold at most 3 upcoming appointments (pending / approved / rescheduled / payment declined, dated today or later; bookings for someone else count too): `action=create` refuses a 4th (422) and the "+ Request Appointment" wizard checks `action=booking_quota` first, showing an "Appointment Limit Reached" pop-up instead of opening. Patients can't book or reschedule more than 6 months ahead: `action=availability` marks those days `past` (greyed) for the patient role, `action=create` (400) and `action=reschedule` (422) refuse them. Staff, walk-ins and staff reschedules are not limited.
**One appointment per day (2026-10-18).** A patient account may have only one live booking per date (`patientBookedDates()` in functions.php; bookings for someone else count). `action=create` and a patient's `action=reschedule` refuse a second one (422); `action=availability` returns `mine: true` for those days (the patient reschedule calendar passes `&exclude_id=<its own id>` so its own day stays open), and the patient calendars show them as a red "Booked" day that can't be clicked. Staff bookings are not limited.

**Baby teeth only for children (2026-10-18).** The dentist Patient Details chart shows the "Baby teeth (children)" section only when the patient is 12 or younger, has no birthdate on file, or already has a baby tooth marked (`wantsBaby()` / `showBaby` in `modules/dentist/records.php`). The Services Availed picker (`logProcedureModal()` in app.js) shows its "Baby teeth" toggle only for age 12 or younger or unknown.
**Update (2026-10-18, later): the 3-booking limit is per day.** Supersedes "at most 3 upcoming appointments" above: `patientActiveBookings()` now counts the account's online bookings MADE today (`DATE(created_at) = CURDATE()`, not cancelled), whatever their visit date. The 4th booking of the day is refused ("You can only make 3 bookings per day. Please try again tomorrow."), and the pop-up says the same. The 6-month and one-per-day rules are unchanged.
**Update (2026-10-18, later still): 1 booking per day.** `PATIENT_MAX_ACTIVE` is now 1: a patient account can make one online booking per day ("You can only make 1 booking per day. Please try again tomorrow.").
**Update (2026-10-18, final): no booking-count limit; pop-up on a booked day instead.** Supersedes the 3-upcoming / 3-per-day / 1-per-day booking-count limits above: `PATIENT_MAX_ACTIVE`, `patientActiveBookings()` and `action=booking_quota` were removed, and "+ Request Appointment" opens the wizard directly again. What stays: the 6-month limit and one appointment per date. A day the patient already has a booking on shows red "Booked" in the patient calendars (Calendar View, booking wizard, patient Reschedule) and clicking it opens `window.dayTakenPopup()` ("Appointment Already Booked", via the new `window.noticePopup(title, msg)` in app.js, z-140 so it sits above an open modal).

**Patient "My Dental Chart" (2026-10-18).** New patient page `modules/patient/dental_chart.php` (sidebar "My Dental Chart", key `records`, after Treatment & Transactions), backed by `api/my_record.php` (`apiAuth(['patient'])`). Read-only: the allergy banner (or "not added yet, add them on My Profile"), the tooth chart drawn like the dentist's (same `window.toothSvg` + `records_chart_style.php`, `<div>` teeth instead of buttons, baby teeth only for age 12 or younger / no birthdate / a baby tooth marked) with the status legend, and a "Dentist's Notes" card listing only the filled clinical fields in plain labels (Oral Hygiene, Gum Condition, Bite, dentures, Abnormalities, General Condition, Medical History) plus "Last updated by". A "Whose chart" select appears when the account has booked for other people (`?person=` limited to treated_patient_ids from the account's own bookings). Nothing is editable; the dentist still edits from Patient Records.
**Update (2026-10-18, later): it is the full record now, "My Dental Records".** The page (still `modules/patient/dental_chart.php`, sidebar "My Dental Records") shows everything the dentist's Patient Details has, read-only: Personal Information (name, age, gender, contact, email, address, emergency contact + number), Dental Chart & Medical History (every clinical field, "Not recorded" when empty, plus the tooth chart and legend), and Treatment History (date, procedures, dentist, status, total; 10 per page, rows open the shared appointment View). `api/my_record.php` returns `patient`, `record` (incl. address / emergency contact) and `history` (same query as `api/records.php action=get`).
**Update (2026-10-18):** the allergy banner was removed from the patient My Dental Records page at the user's request; allergies show only in the Allergies field there. The dentist-side alerts are unchanged.
**Update (2026-10-18): allergy banners removed everywhere.** At the user's request the red / yellow / green allergy banners are gone from the dentist Patient Details, the shared appointment View and the Services Availed pop-up (`window.allergyAlertHtml` / `allergyIsNone` deleted). Allergies are still collected on My Profile and shown as the Allergies field in Patient Details and My Dental Records.
**Update (2026-10-18):** the Treatment History table was removed from the patient My Dental Records page (the patient has visits on Treatment & Transactions); `api/my_record.php` no longer returns `history`.

**15-minute allowance between visits (migration 076, 2026-10-18).** Supersedes the hourly slot list (and migration 053's): `time_slots` is now `10:00,11:15,13:00,14:15,15:30`, i.e. 1-hour visits with a 15-minute gap (10:00-11:00, 11:15-12:15, lunch, 1:00-2:00, 2:15-3:15, 3:30-4:30), 5 slots a day. Migration 076 also moved every dentist weekly-grid cell to the new start (11:00 to 11:15, 14:00 to 14:15, 15:00 to 15:30; 16:00 / 17:00 rows deleted) and moved upcoming bookings at removed times (live: #841 11:00 to 11:15, #832 / #833 16:00 to 15:30, all rex.carbonel test bookings). Applied local + live. Slot ranges are still start + 1 hour (`slotRange()`), which handles minutes.
**Correction (migration 077, 2026-10-18): slots end on the hour.** Supersedes 076's list: `time_slots` = `10:00,11:15,13:15,14:15,15:15,16:15` (10:00-11:00, 11:15-12:00, lunch, 1:15-2:00, 2:15-3:00, 3:15-4:00, 4:15-5:00), 6 a day. A visit now ends at the next full hour: `slotEndHm()` in functions.php (reminder text, walk-in "hour in progress", passed-slot check) and the JS `slotRange` / `visitRange` / receptionist `addHour` use `(H+1):00`, not start + 60 min. 077 remapped grids (13:00 to 13:15, 15:30 to 15:15, re-added 16:15 wherever 15:15 is worked) and upcoming bookings (live: #842 and #831 to 13:15, #832 / #833 back to 16:15).

**Upfront fee notice in the Terms (2026-10-18).** Section 4 of the default Terms (`includes/legal.php`) opens with a notice box: "Appointment fee: ₱500.00 per booking", paid upfront, non-refundable, forfeited on cancel or no-show, credited toward the bill. The No-Show bullet in section 3 names the fee too. The amount is the `{{reservation_fee}}` placeholder, which `renderLegal()` replaces with the current Settings > Reservation Fee, so it follows any change. Admins editing the Terms can keep or move that placeholder. `LEGAL_DEFAULT_UPDATED` is now 2026-10-18.

**Cashier Transaction page is paginated (2026-10-18).** Patient Transactions shows 10 rows per page via `window.pageSlice()` (`#txPager` under the table: "Showing 1–10 of N", Previous / page numbers / Next, hidden when one page is enough). Row numbers continue across pages; typing in the search box goes back to page 1; Refresh keeps the current page.

**Revenue Reports: no Specialist or Status (2026-10-18).** At the user's request the Patient Transactions table on `modules/admin/reports.php` is now # · Name · Service · Date of Service · Cost (6/34/30/16/14 %), the PDF / Preview document is # · Name · Service · Date of Service · Method · Amount (5/30/29/16/9/11 %), the transaction pop-up drops its Specialist and Status rows, and `report_print.php` matches. The "By dentist" breakdown is unchanged.
Same day: the Method column was also removed from the PDF / Preview document (now # · Name · Service · Date of Service · Amount, 5/34/33/16/12 %) and the "Payment method" row from the transaction pop-up. The "By payment method" breakdown stays.
Same day: the Revenue Reports Patient Transactions table shows 10 rows per page via `window.pageSlice()` (`#revPager`, `drawRows()`); a new period / date range goes back to page 1. Generate PDF and Preview Report still list every transaction in the period.

**RULE: paginated tables fill big screens (2026-10-18).** Every paginated data table sizes itself like Patient Records: its table class string ends with `2xl:text-[14.5px] 2xl:[&_td]:py-3.5 2xl:[&_th]:py-3` (bigger text and taller rows on 1536px+ screens), and its rows per page come from `window.fitRows(table)` (as many rows as fit below the table, minimum 10) instead of a flat 10, re-paged on resize with `window.fitOnResize(fn)` (app.js; one global handler, the callback must no-op once its table is gone). Applied to Patient Records (receptionist / dentist / assistant, which still use their own `window.__recFit`), Revenue Reports (`#revTable`), the dentist Today's Schedule queue (`#queueTable`) and the cashier Transaction page (`#txTable`). New paginated tables must follow it. Small in-modal lists (e.g. the 5-row Patient Details history) are exempt.

**Admin dashboard auto-refresh is always on, every 10 s (2026-10-18).** The "Auto-refresh 30s" toggle beside "Dashboard Overview" was removed; the operational cards now refresh silently every 10 seconds with no control (`scheduleOps()` in `modules/admin/dashboard.php`). Supersedes the 30 s toggle mentions above.
Same day for the admin **Appointment** page (`modules/admin/appointments.php`): both Auto-refresh 30s switches were removed; the list reloads silently every 10 s (`window.__apptTimer`, stops once the page is left), with no "Data refreshed" toast, and rows the admin has ticked stay ticked across a refresh.

**Admin dashboard cards: keep the original style (2026-10-18).** Bigger card text (30-40px numbers), all six cards in one row, two rows capped at 8/12 width, and a 2x-tall row with the duty cards stacked on the right were all tried on wide screens and rejected; the user wants the original look back: 26px numbers, 13px labels, 4 cards on top and Doctors / Assistant Dentists on Duty on their own row, at every width.
Then (same day, user-approved): the cards **scale with the screen width** past 1366px. A `<style>` block in `modules/admin/dashboard.php` sets `--g = max(0px, 100vw - 1366px)` on `#opsCards`, and `.ops-tile` (padding, min-height), `.ops-chip` (icon box + svg), `.ops-num` (number), `.ops-lbl` (label), `.ops-sub` (Online / Walk-in line) grow by `calc(base + var(--g) * k)`, each capped (number 26 to 34px, label 13 to 16px, sub 12 to 14px, padding 16 to 26px, min-height 124 to 168px, chip 36 to 46px). At 1366px and below the original sizes are unchanged; the 4 + 2 layout stays.
The same scaling applies to the six status cards on the admin **Appointment** page (`#countGrid` in `modules/admin/appointments.php`: `.cnt-tile` padding 16 to 24px, `.cnt-ico` 34 to 42px, `.cnt-lbl` 11 to 13px, `.cnt-num` 26 to 34px, growing past 1366px).

**RULE: the whole app grows with the screen (2026-10-18).** On screens wider than a 1366px laptop, everything in the app (text, icons, buttons, cards, tables, padding, pop-ups) gets bigger as the resolution goes up, so pages keep their laptop proportions instead of looking stretched and empty. How: a pre-paint script in `includes/layout.php` sets `--uiz` on `<html>` (`1 + (innerWidth - 1366) * 0.15 / 554`, between 1 and 1.25: 1.15 at 1920px, 1.25 from about 2290px; first shipped at 1.25 / 1.3 and the user found it too big) and `window.uiZoom`, updated on resize; `includes/tailwind.php` applies `zoom: var(--uiz)` to `#app-content > .page`, `#topHeader`, `#modalRoot > [role="dialog"]`, `.slot-pop > div` and `#toastRoot` (never when printing). The sidebar keeps its fixed width. At 1366px and below nothing changes; phones and tablets are unaffected. Public pages (index / login / terms…) don't use it. Gotchas inside a zoomed box: `vh` / `vw` sizes are multiplied by the zoom, so divide them, e.g. `min-h-[calc((100vh-150px)/var(--uiz,1))]` (done in `admin/schedules.php`, the receipt image in `receptionist/dashboard.php`, modal bodies and the slot pop-up); code that turns mouse `clientX` deltas into `scrollLeft` divides by `window.uiZoom` (dashboard chart drag-pan). Elements appended to `<body>` (chart tooltips, theme menu) are not zoomed, so `clientX` positions still line up. This supersedes the earlier card-only `sz-*` / `.ops-*` / `.cnt-*` scaling (the CSS for those was removed; any leftover `sz-*` class names are inert).

**Admin User Accounts: badges and row buttons stay on one line (2026-10-18).** In `modules/admin/users.php` the status badge ("Active Roaming" / "Active In-House") and the Reset Password / Archive buttons are `whitespace-nowrap`, and the Actions cell is `flex-nowrap`, so they no longer break onto two lines (seen after the page zoom made text bigger). The table scrolls sideways inside its card if a window is too narrow.

**From / To date ranges can't be reversed (2026-10-18).** `window.wireDateRange(from, to)` in app.js: once From is picked, To's picker greys out earlier days (`min`), and From can't go past To (`max`); a typed reversed date is pulled to the other end. Used on Revenue Reports, the admin Appointment page and Audit Logs (called before each page's own change listeners). Server backstop: a reversed range is swapped in `reportWindow()` (functions.php), `api/reports.php` action=logs and `api/logs.php`. Use it on any new From / To pair.

**Admin Appointment page: no Export CSV / Remove Selected, paginated (2026-10-18).** At the user's request the Export CSV and Remove Selected buttons were removed from `modules/admin/appointments.php`, along with the row checkboxes and Select all that only served them (`api/reports.php` `logs_csv` / `delete_logs` still exist, with no UI). The list is paginated with `window.pageSlice()` (`#logPager`, shared by the desktop table and the phone / iPad cards): desktop rows per page come from `window.fitRows()` (min 10), cards show 10. A filter or sort change goes back to page 1; the 10 s auto-refresh keeps the current page.

**Admin Services, User Accounts and Audit Logs are paginated (2026-10-18).** Each uses `window.pageSlice()` with `window.fitRows()` rows per page (min 10) and `window.fitOnResize()`: `#svcPager` (search / category change goes to page 1), `#userPager` (search or a role tab change goes to page 1), `#logPager` (`renderRows()`; row `data-log` indexes stay absolute). Audit Logs' Auto-refresh 30s toggle was removed: like the dashboard it reloads silently every 10 s (`window.__logTimer`, no toast), keeping the current page. `fitRows()` now keeps its count per window size once measured on real rows (so every page of a table shows the same number of rows), and if it only saw the "Loading…" row it re-pages once through the page's resize handler.

**Bank Transfer: QR only, account name DGT One Dental Center (2026-10-18).** The bank QR image was cropped to just the code (`uploads/payment/qr_bank_dgt.png`; the MariBank logo and the "CLAIRE MENNIN TAGON: MariBank(****4850)" caption are gone; original kept in the session scratchpad) and `pay_bank_qr` points at it (local + live). The account-name line of `pay_bank_details` is now "DGT One Dental Center". The patient booking wizard's Bank Transfer panel shows only the QR and "Account name: DGT One Dental Center"; the bank name and account number stay in Settings but are no longer shown to patients. Note: the QR's own data still belongs to the same MariBank account, so a banking app may show the account holder's real name after scanning.

**More payment methods + Settings layout (migration 078, 2026-10-18).** Admin Settings' right column: the Block an Operational Date card no longer stretches (its Blocked Dates list is capped at 330px, about 5 rows, and scrolls); a new **More Payment Methods** card sits below it, sized to its content (list capped at 330px, scrolls), and Backup & Restore moved under it (from the left column) so both columns end level; Payment Settings (left) and Backup & Restore (right) are flex-1, so whichever column is shorter stretches its last card and the two always end on the same line. There the admin adds extra online payment options besides GCash and Bank Transfer (e.g. Maya, another bank): name (required, unique, not GCash / Bank / Cash), account name, account number, optional QR, a "Shown to patients / Hidden" switch, Edit and Delete (delete is refused once a booking used it; turn it off instead). Stored in the new `payment_methods` table; `extraPaymentMethods()` / `extraPaymentMethod('m<id>')` in `api/_bootstrap.php`; `api/settings.php` actions `payment_methods` (GET, admin), `save_payment_method`, `toggle_payment_method`, `delete_payment_method`, and `upload_payment_qr` / `clear_payment_qr` accept `method=m<id>`. `payment_info` returns the active ones as `methods`. `payments.reservation_method` is now VARCHAR(20) (`'m<id>'` for an added method) with a new `reservation_method_label` name snapshot. Patients: each active method is an extra card on the booking payment step (and Resubmit Payment keeps it), its panel shows its QR / account name / account number, and its reference number must be 6-30 letters, digits or hyphens (`refRule()` in `modules/patient/appointments.php`; server check in `validateReservationPayment()`). The screenshot rules are the same as Bank Transfer (no GCash-content check, reference typed, not locked). The receptionist's payment badge and the appointment View use the label.

**Admin Settings layout: every card full width (2026-10-18).** Supersedes the two-column Settings layout notes above (left/right columns, Backup moved under More Payment Methods, flex-1 stretching): the user rejected both the canvas gap and the stretched-card empty space. `modules/admin/settings.php` is now one column of full-width cards, each with its parts side by side inside: Payment Settings (Reservation Fee | Screenshot toggle | Booking Rules, 3 columns from `lg:` with dividers), Payment Methods (one card: GCash and Bank as two halves, Save GCash Details right under its fields, then a "More Payment Methods" section with its Add button and list below a divider), Clinic Time Slots (add-a-slot | current slots), Block an Operational Date (form | Blocked Dates list, list capped at 330px), Backup & Restore, Promotions & Discounts, Legal & Policy. Never pair cards of different heights in one row again.

**User Accounts: role dropdown (2026-10-18).** The row of role tab buttons above the card (Doctors / Assistant Dentists / Receptionists / Cashiers / Patients / Admins) was replaced by a `<select id="userRole">` at the start of the toolbar, before Add and View Archive. Changing it sets `role`, goes back to page 1 and reloads (the Add button label follows, e.g. "Add Cashier"). `#userTabs` and `$tabCls` are gone. Supersedes "role tab change" mentions above.

**Role Type in Add / Edit User; change an account's role (2026-10-18).** The Add pop-up on User Accounts starts with a **Role Type** select (`roleField()` in `modules/admin/users.php`); changing it redraws the form for that role, keeping the typed name / e-mail / phone, and after saving the list switches to that role (`showList()`). The Edit pop-up (row click) has the same select: picking another role redraws that role's fields and the button reads "Change Role & Save", moving the **same account** (username, password, history kept) instead of making a new one, e.g. a patient hired as a cashier. Not shown for your own row or the main admin. Server: `api/users.php action=update` takes `role`; it refuses changing your own role or the main admin's, a patient who still has upcoming live bookings (as booker or treated person) and a doctor who still has upcoming assigned visits; moving into staff needs age 18+ (birthdate falls back to `patient_records` for a former patient); moving into Assistant Dentist creates or reuses a `dental_assistants` roster row linked to the login, moving out of it archives that row and unlinks it. The Edit Assistant pop-up has the select too (only when the assistant has a login). History Log line "Changed the role of X from A to B". `currentUser()` (auth_middleware.php) now syncs `$_SESSION['role']` to the account's current role, so the holder lands on their new role's pages on their next request.

**Network feedback layer (2026-10-18).** One app-wide layer in `assets/js/app.js` (before `window.api`) so actions never look frozen: `window.netBusy(on)` drives `#netBar`, a 3px teal indeterminate bar at the top (CSS in `tailwind.php`), shown only after 200 ms; `window.fetch` is wrapped so every non-GET call to `/api/` (window.api and raw upload fetches) counts, and the button clicked within 1.5 s before it gets `aria-busy="true"` (spinner via `button[aria-busy]::before`, repeat clicks swallowed in a capture listener, so nothing is sent twice; `disabled` is never touched, chained calls keep it busy, `data-close` buttons are never marked). GET polling is not tracked, so the 10 s refreshes don't flash the bar. `window.api()` aborts after 30 s ("The server is taking too long…") and turns a dropped connection into "You are offline…" / "Could not reach the server…"; `offline` / `online` events toast. `assets/js/nav.js`: the sidebar item highlights on click, the content dims only after 150 ms, `#app-content` gets `aria-busy`, and a newer click aborts the older page load (sequence number) so a slow earlier page can never replace the one the user moved to; a failed load restores the previous highlight.

**Dentist Schedules: per-date hours on a calendar (migration 079, 2026-10-19).** The weekly grid on `modules/admin/schedules.php` is now titled "Usual Weekly Hours" and stays each In-House dentist's usual pattern. Below it a **Calendar** card (month view, Mon–Sat, ‹ › / This Month) shows each date's hours ("6 slots" + range, Off, Absent, Closed; a date changed from the usual week is amber with a "Changed" tag). Clicking a date (today or later, not a Sunday / blocked date) opens a pop-up with that day's time slots as on/off toggles (Whole day / Day off shortcuts, "Reset to usual hours" when changed); the change applies to that date only. Stored in `dentist_day_schedules` (dentist_id, work_date, slots CSV, '' = off). `dentistDayOverride()` (functions.php) is read by `dentistBookableSlots()` after the absence + roaming checks, so every duty / booking check follows it, and the online-booking union in `api/appointments.php action=availability` uses it too. API: `api/schedules.php` GET `action=days&dentist_id=&from=&to=` (usual / effective slots, edited, absent, closed, this dentist's live bookings per slot) and POST `action=save_day` ({dentist_id, date, slots[]} or {reset:true}; owner only, no past / Sunday / blocked dates; refuses turning off a time the dentist already has live bookings at; saving the usual hours deletes the row; History Log line). Saving the weekly grid or switching doctor reloads the calendar.

**Dentists manage their own availability; admin is read-only (migration 080, 2026-10-19).** Supersedes the admin-editing parts of the per-date hours note above and the admin dashboard's Mark absent / Mark present buttons (both removed).
- **Dentist:** new page `modules/dentist/availability.php` ("My Availability", sidebar for In-House dentists only, inserted by `includes/sidebar.php`; a roaming dentist is redirected home). A month calendar: click a date (today or later) for a pop-up with **Custom / Half-Day** (time toggles) or **Full Day Off**, plus Reset to usual hours, then **Save Availability**. Below it the editable Usual Weekly Hours grid (Save Weekly Hours). If booked patients are inside hours being turned off, a confirm pop-up explains what will happen first.
- **API (`api/schedules.php`):** `save_day` / `save_schedule` are dentist-only and always for the caller (`dentist_id` forced to self; admin gets 403 "read-only"); booked times may now be turned off. `days` works for the admin (any dentist) and the dentist (self). `set_absence` was removed. After a save, `processScheduleConflicts()` checks the dentist's upcoming live bookings (not checked in, hour not over): still inside working hours = untouched; outside = moved to the least-busy on-duty roaming dentist with room that hour (under 5) -> `appointments.sched_change_status = 'reassigned'`, else `'needs_reschedule'` (follow-up plan visits always flagged). `sched_change_at` / `sched_change_from` record when / which dentist. A flagged booking whose hour is turned back on is cleared ("Appointment Kept" notice). Online booking follows at once (the day's slots come from the same duty check).
- **Alerts:** patient gets an in-app `schedule_change` notification + e-mail with the spec's wording ("...You will now be attended to by Dr. X..." / "Dr. X modified their schedule for <date>. Please log into your patient portal to pick a new date/time slot..."); a reassigned approved visit sends the roaming dentist the usual on-call notice; every active receptionist gets a `schedule_conflict` notification (receptionist bell, now titled "Front Desk Alerts", and its page). The admin gets nothing (History Log lines only). No SMS (none in this app).
- **Patient:** a `needs_reschedule` booking comes back from `action=list` with `doctor_absent = true`, so the card shows the "doctor unavailable" notice, the 24-hour gate is lifted and the Reschedule pop-up offers another dentist; `action=reschedule` / `action=assign` clear the flag.
- **Receptionist:** an "Action Needed: Dentist Schedule Change" card at the top of the Appointments page (hidden when empty; `action=sched_changes`, receptionist only): patient, tappable phone, date/time, dentist change, status (Needs new time / Moved to roaming), how long ago, "Call the patient" after 3 hours, and Reassign / Reschedule / Flag for Reschedule (or Remind Patient) / Mark Reviewed / View. `action=sched_flag` (sets needs_reschedule + notifies the patient in-app and by e-mail) and `action=sched_dismiss`.
- **Admin:** Dentist Schedules is read-only (no quick-fill, no Save, no calendar clicks, a "Read-only" tag).

**Admin Dentist Schedules page removed (2026-10-19).** At the user's request `modules/admin/schedules.php` was deleted (local + live) and its sidebar entry removed; the admin has no schedule view at all now. `api/schedules.php`'s admin `list` read was removed and `action=days` is dentist-only (own hours). Dentists manage hours only from My Availability. Supersedes the "Admin: read-only" bullet above.

**My Availability: weekly grid removed (2026-10-19).** At the user's request the "Usual Weekly Hours" card and its Save button were removed from `modules/dentist/availability.php`; dentists change hours only date by date through the calendar pop-up. The existing `dentist_schedules` grid still supplies each date's usual hours. `api/schedules.php action=save_schedule` still exists but has no UI.

**Patient notifications are e-mailed too; payment receipts by e-mail (2026-10-19).** `notify()` (functions.php) now also queues an e-mail copy (`mailShell`, title + message, "Open My Account" button) whenever the recipient is an active patient with a valid e-mail; staff notifications stay in-app only. A 6th argument `$email = false` skips it when the caller sends its own e-mail. Mail is queued (`queueEmail()`) and sent by `flushQueuedEmails()` on shutdown after `fastcgi_finish_request()` / `litespeed_finish_request()`, so SMTP never slows a request. The separate schedule-change e-mails were removed (notify covers them). After a cashier payment (`api/payments.php action=settle`, full or installment), `emailPaymentReceipt()` e-mails the receipt: receipt no., date, patient, dentist, each billed line, discounts, reservation deposit, amount paid + method, change, remaining balance, and a "View / Print Receipt" link to `modules/shared/receipt`. `mailShell()` takes an optional 6th `$extraHtml` block for that table.

**No "Treatment completed" notification (2026-10-19).** At the user's request `api/treatments.php` no longer sends the patient a "Treatment completed … proceed to the cashier" notification (or its e-mail) when the dentist logs a procedure. The "Recommended follow-up" notice and the cashier's receipt e-mail are unchanged.

**E-mails sent as UTF-8 (2026-10-19).** `sendAppMail()` now sets PHPMailer `CharSet = 'UTF-8'` (+ base64). It was on PHPMailer's ISO-8859-1 default, so the peso sign in receipt / notification e-mails showed as "â‚±".
The auto-built plain-text copy of each e-mail also runs `html_entity_decode()`, so `&amp;` / `&#8369;` show as real characters there and in the inbox preview.

**TEMPORARY: "Open on Sundays" switch (2026-10-19).** Admin Settings > Booking Rules has "Open on Sundays (temporary)" (`settings.sunday_open`, default '0', `api/settings.php action=save_sunday_open`, History Log line). On: `isClinicClosed()` no longer closes Sundays, `action=availability` opens them, and dentists work their **Saturday** grid on Sundays (`scheduleDow()` in functions.php, used by `dentistBookableSlots()` and the online union); roaming dentists work every slot as usual. Client side `window.SUNDAY_OPEN` (set in `includes/layout.php`) un-hatches Sundays in the staff calendars and lifts the Sunday checks in `wireClinicDate()` and the booking wizard. Not changed: the dentist My Availability calendar (still Mon-Sat), the Terms text, and blocked dates still close a Sunday. The user will remove this later: delete `sundayOpen()` / `scheduleDow()`, the setting UI + action, and every `SUNDAY_OPEN` check.

**Admin Settings: tabs + split sections (2026-10-19, third pass, supersedes the earlier Settings layouts).** `modules/admin/settings.php`: an `<h1>` "Settings", a row of underlined tabs (`#setNav` `[data-sec]`, active = blue + underline, scrolls sideways on phones, open tab kept in `sessionStorage['dgt_settings_sec']`), and one `section[data-panel]` at a time: Booking & Fees, Payment Methods, Hours & Closed Dates, Promotions, Terms & Privacy, Backup. Each group is a 2-column row (`lg:grid-cols-[260px_1fr]`): title + short description on the left, a white rounded card with the controls on the right (stacks on phones). Switch rows save right away; text-field cards (Reservation fee, GCash, Bank) have a footer Save that stays disabled until a field changes (`wireDirty(btnId, ids)` / `markSaved(btnId)` in the page script), showing "Unsaved changes" and then "✓ Saved". Restore sits in its own red-bordered card. All element ids are unchanged. Don't put a display class on the `section[data-panel]` elements (they use the `hidden` attribute).

**Backup / restore work on live (2026-10-19).** Live Hostinger has `exec()` disabled and no XAMPP `mysqldump.exe`, so the old backup / restore always failed there. `api/settings.php` now falls back to plain PHP when the CLI tools can't run (`canExec()` + `mysqlClientPath()`): `phpDump()` streams `SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS=0`, then `DROP TABLE IF EXISTS` + `SHOW CREATE TABLE` + 500-row `INSERT` batches per base table (one statement per line, ending in ";"); `phpRestore()` runs a dump line-statement by line-statement (skips USE / CREATE / DROP DATABASE, refuses files with DELIMITER). Local XAMPP still uses mysqldump / mysql. The Download button now fetches the file and shows a toast on error instead of opening a raw JSON page. A download writes an "Downloaded a database backup" History Log line. Verified: local PHP dump restored into a scratch DB matched every table and row count; on live the PHP dump produced all 25 tables.
**Update (same day):** the left description column is gone. Each group is now one card with its title + description as the card header (`$hdrCls`, actions like "+ New discount" on the header's right), cards stacked 20px apart, and the whole Settings page (title, tabs, cards) is centred at `max-w-[1080px]` so switches and fields stay close to their labels on wide screens. The Formatting help box on Terms & Privacy was removed at the user's request.
**Update (same day, "all closer"):** spacing tightened: cards 12px apart, card header / row / body padding `py-3`, footers `py-2`, normal-height inputs, 80px QR previews, smaller page title and tabs. The Reservation fee is a single row (label left; fee box + Save on the right, "Unsaved changes" beside it), no footer.
**Update:** the Settings page is no longer centred: it stays `max-w-[1080px]` but left-aligned next to the sidebar (user request).
**Update:** the 1080px cap was removed too; Settings cards now span the full content width (user: too much empty space on the right).
**Update:** on 1536px+ screens (`2xl:`) pairs of cards sit side by side (`grid 2xl:grid-cols-2 2xl:items-start`): Reservation + Booking Rules, GCash + Bank Transfer (More Payment Methods full width below), Clinic Time Slots + Closed Dates, Backup + Restore. Promotions and Terms & Privacy stay full width. Below 1536px it is one card per row. Cards are top-aligned, so the shorter one of a pair ends earlier (accepted by the user).
**Update (2026-10-19): back to one card per row, without stretching.** The 2xl side-by-side pairs were removed (user request). Cards still span the full width, but their insides no longer spread out: card headers and rows are a grid (`minmax(0,640px)` / `minmax(0,560px)` text column, then the control right after it, `sm:justify-start`), so switches, the fee box and header buttons sit next to their labels; field grids are capped (GCash 2 x 340px, Bank 3 x 300px, Closed Dates reason 380px); footers are left-aligned (Save under the fields, "Unsaved changes" after it).
**Update (2026-10-19, final): flat "settings list" style from the user's reference.** Supersedes the per-card layouts above. Each tab is one white panel (the `section[data-panel]` itself: rounded border, `px-6 py-5`, groups `mt-8` apart); inside it groups have no box, just a bold title + small italic description, then rows with the label + italic hint on the left and the switch / fee box on the right (`justify-between`, full width). GCash / Bank fields are capped and their Save sits left under them; lists (more payment methods, blocked dates, promos) and the Terms textarea keep a thin rounded border.
**Update (same day):** the Reservation fee box + Save, Download .sql, and the Restore file picker + Restore Database now sit under their label (`$stackRow`), not at the far right; group headers put their count / action ("3 dates", "+ New discount", "+ Add payment method") right after the description instead of at the right edge. Booking Rules switches stay on the right.
**Update (same day):** descriptions and hints are no longer italic; the Closed Dates count ("3 dates") is a small grey pill right next to the "Closed Dates" title.
**Update (same day):** the Booking Rules switch rows are capped at 860px (`sm:max-w-[860px]` on `$rowCls`), so each switch sits just past its text instead of at the panel's right edge.
**Update (same day):** the white panel behind each tab was removed too (user request, like the reference): sections sit straight on the page background (`pt-6 [&>*+*]:mt-8`), only inputs, lists and the Terms box keep their own borders.
**Update (same day):** the only white cards left on Settings are the lists (blocked dates, promotions, more payment methods) and the Terms text box (`bg-white shadow-sm`, dark `navy-800`); everything else sits on the page background.
**Update (same day):** the Settings tab buttons carry the `tab` class (opts out of the global button hover shadow / press) plus `outline-none`, so hovering or clicking a tab shows no box, only the underline.
**Update (same day):** the bell dropdowns' "Mark all read" links (`includes/header.php`, all four bells) carry `tab` + `outline-none`, so hover / click shows no box, just an underline.
**Update (same day):** Settings content is 15% bigger than the rest of the app: `#setPage { zoom: min(1.15, calc(1.3 / var(--uiz, 1))) }` in the page `<style>`, so with the app-wide `--uiz` zoom the page never exceeds 1.3x (1366px: 1.15, 1920px: 1.3 total). Pop-ups are not affected (they live in #modalRoot).
**Walk-in New patient layout (2026-10-19).** The Email Address field (`#w_nf4`) spans both columns, so the New patient rows are First | Last, Email (full width), Date of Birth | Phone Number, with no empty half-row next to Phone.

**"For someone else" new person: ages 1 to 80 (2026-10-19).** In the booking wizard, when "For Someone Else" + "+ New Person" is picked, the Date of Birth Year list only covers ages 1 to 80 (`setDobRange('b_dob', 1, 80)` in `syncFields()`; back to the full range for "For Myself"). `createUnlinkedPatientRecord()` (`api/_bootstrap.php`) refuses other ages: "The patient must be 1 to 80 years old." (uses `WALKIN_MIN_AGE` / `WALKIN_MAX_AGE`).

**Sidebar section headings (2026-10-19).** `includes/sidebar.php` groups each role's links under small uppercase headings (`$navSections`, rendered as `.nav-sec`): admin Overview / Clinic / Management / Reports / System; dentist My Schedule / Patients; receptionist Front Desk / Patients; assistant Today / Patients; cashier Billing / Schedule; patient Booking / My Records / Clinic. Links follow the section order (so the dentist's Calendar now sits under My Schedule, above Patient Records); a link not listed falls into "More". In the collapsed rail each heading becomes a short divider line. The link list scrolls on its own (`.nav-scroll`, `min-h-0 overflow-y-auto`) on short screens, so the profile and Logout stay pinned at the bottom.

**Renamed to "Dental Records" (2026-10-19).** The patient page `modules/patient/dental_chart.php` (sidebar link, page title and heading) is now "Dental Records" instead of "My Dental Records".

**Dentist sidebar heading renamed (2026-10-19).** The dentist section above My Schedule / My Availability / Calendar is now "Appointments" (it repeated "My Schedule").

**Dentist sidebar: one heading per link (2026-10-19).** Supersedes the "Appointments" heading: Today (My Schedule), Working Hours (My Availability, in-house only; the heading hides with it), Schedule (Calendar; renamed from Month View), Patients (Patient Records).

**One appointment per day is per person (2026-10-20).** Supersedes "one appointment per date per account": `patientBookedDates($uid, $excludeId, $treatedId)` counts "For Myself" bookings (`booking_for <> 'other'`) when `$treatedId` is 0, else that treated person's bookings. So an account can book itself and someone else on the same day; the same person still can't have two the same day (a brand-new person has nothing to block). `action=create` checks the person being booked, `action=reschedule` the moved booking's own person, and `action=availability` takes `&for=other&person=<id>` (or reads the person from `exclude_id` / `plan_id`) for the `mine` "Booked" days. The booking wizard reloads the calendar via the new `setExtra()` when For Myself / For Someone Else or the Person select changes, and drops a picked day that turns Booked. `dayTakenPopup(other)` wording updated. Calendar View still marks only the account's own days.

**Patient Information Record, required before booking (migration 081, 2026-10-20).** Supersedes the My Profile "Health & Emergency Information" card and its sign-in gate. New page `modules/patient/health_form.php` (title "Patient Information Record", not in the sidebar; linked from My Profile and Dental Records "Update Patient Information Record"), backed by `api/health_form.php` (`apiAuth(['patient'])`, `action=get` / `action=save`) and the shared question list + checks in `includes/health_form.php` (required by `functions.php`): Sex, Date of Birth (only while none is on file), emergency contact, Previous Dentist + Last Dental Visit, Physician, 7 Yes/No medical questions (4 ask "what / when / which" on Yes), allergies checklist (Local Anesthetic, Penicillin / Antibiotics, Sulfa, Aspirin, Latex, Other) or None, women-only pregnant / nursing / birth control (shown for Female), blood type and usual blood pressure (each with "I don't know"), the conditions checklist from the clinic's paper form or "None of these", and a "true and complete" confirmation. Every item is required (client `firstMissing()` scrolls to the gap, server `healthFormValidate()`); Bleeding Time and the physician's office details were left out. Saving stores the JSON in `patient_records.health_form` + `health_form_at` and fills the dental record: `gender`, `date_of_birth` (if empty), emergency contact, `allergies` (`healthFormAllergyText()`, "None" when none) and `medical_history` (`healthFormSummary()` lines, replacing the previous summary and keeping anything the dentist added below it). Gate: `healthFormDone()`; `patientLandingUrl()` sends a patient to the form until it is answered (then My Profile only if the phone is missing; `patientProfileMissing()` now checks only Contact Number); `modules/patient/appointments.php` and `calendar.php` redirect there; `api/appointments.php action=create` refuses with "Please complete your Patient Information Record before booking." First save goes on to Appointments, later saves to Dental Records. `nav.js` now shows the redirected address (`res.redirected`) after an in-app redirect. The dentist Medical History box is taller (200px). `api/profile.php action=health` still exists but nothing calls it.

**One phone number per account (2026-10-20).** `phoneInUse($phone, $excludeUid)` / `phoneChangeTaken($phone, $uid)` + `PHONE_TAKEN_MSG` ("This phone number is already used by another account.") in `includes/functions.php`. Checked on sign-up (`login.php`; a pending unverified sign-up with the same e-mail keeps its own number), admin Add / Edit User (`api/users.php`), + Create Record / walk-in New patient and the Patient Details contact edit (`api/records.php`), My Profile (`api/profile.php`) and assistants (`api/assistants.php`). Edits only check a NEW number (`phoneChangeTaken()`), so accounts that already shared one before this rule can still save. A person booked "For Someone Else" shares the booker's number on purpose, so booker and those people never block each other (`appointments` link), and `createUnlinkedPatientRecord()` is not checked. A self-booking no longer copies its phone onto the account when another account uses it. Existing live duplicates on 2026-10-20: 09170000006 (janette.ganolon, associate.dentist) and 09479581294 (clairemennin.tagon, clairemennin.tagon2).

**Child / Adult Age removed (2026-10-20).** The `dental_age` field is gone from the dentist Patient Details, the patient Dental Records page and `api/records.php` `$TEXT_FIELDS` / `api/my_record.php` (the real age comes from the birthdate). The column stays in the database, unused. On the dentist form Abnormalities is now full width under the 6 boxes; on the patient page it fills the third box of the second row.

**Patient Dental Records hides clinical findings (2026-10-20).** Bite (Occlusion), Gum Condition (Periodontal), Oral Hygiene and Abnormalities are no longer shown on the patient's Dental Records page, and `api/my_record.php` no longer returns them (they need the dentist's examination). The page shows Upper / Lower Denture side by side (`.pt-clinical`, 2 columns), then Allergies, General Condition and Medical History full width, plus the tooth chart. The dentist's Patient Details still has every field.

**Patient Information Record: dentures + general condition (2026-10-20).** Dental History now asks "Do you wear an upper denture?" / "a lower denture?" (Yes needs "Since when?", stored as `denture_upper_since` / `denture_lower_since`), and Medical History opens with "How would you describe your general health?"
