(`HF_GENERAL`: Excellent / Good / Fair / Poor). All required. Saving writes `patient_records.denture_upper` / `denture_lower` ("Since 2019" or "None", `healthFormDentureText()`) and `general_condition`, and adds both to the Medical History summary. Like Allergies, a new save overwrites what is in those boxes.

**Yes / No can be cleared (2026-10-20).** On the Patient Information Record, clicking the already-chosen Yes or No again unselects it (pointerdown remembers if it was on, click clears it and fires change, so an "If yes" box hides again). Sex is unchanged.

**General Medical Status replaces the general-health rating (2026-10-20).** Supersedes the Excellent / Good / Fair / Poor question, "Are you in good health?", "Have you ever been hospitalized?" and the Name of Physician field. A "General Medical Status" card asks 4 required Yes / No screening questions (`HF_STATUS`): under medical treatment or a physician's care (Yes: condition + physician's name), serious illness / operation / hospitalization in the past 2 to 5 years (Yes: explain), prescription / over-the-counter drugs / supplements (Yes: list), bad reaction to local anesthesia, antibiotics or other medicines (Yes: which medicine and what happened). Tobacco and alcohol / drugs moved to a "Habits" card. `healthFormStatusText()` writes the 4 answers, one per line, into `patient_records.general_condition` and the Medical History summary. Older saved answers with the removed keys are simply ignored.

**Only My Profile until the form is answered (2026-10-20).** Treatment & Transactions (`modules/patient/dashboard.php`), Dental Records (`dental_chart.php`) and Services (`services.php`) now redirect to the Patient Information Record while `healthFormDone()` is false, like Appointments and Calendar View; `api/my_record.php` answers 403. My Profile, Notifications and the form itself stay open.

**Patient Information Record redesign (2026-10-20).** `modules/patient/health_form.php` layout only (questions, ids and saving unchanged): from `xl:` a sticky "Your progress" panel on the left (percent, bar, a section list with a green check per finished section, click to scroll); below `xl:` a progress card on top. Each section is a card with an icon chip, short hint and a "Done" badge; Yes / No questions are full-width tiles stacked one per row (2 columns was rejected) with segmented Yes (teal) / No (navy) buttons (radios are `sr-only`, click again to clear still works); Sex is two big toggle buttons; allergies and conditions are chip checklists (conditions 4 columns on 2xl, "(N selected)" count) with a green "None" chip; a sticky bottom bar shows "N of M sections complete" + the Save button. When a birthdate is already on file, Personal & Emergency Contact is 3 columns from `lg:` (Sex full width above the two contact fields below that), so no half-empty row. Inline SVG icons (`FI` + `svg()` / `withIco()` in the page script): male / female on the Sex buttons, a check / x on Yes / No (Sex can be cleared by clicking it again too, `data-clear`), and a left-edge icon in every text box (user, phone, tooth, pen); selects have none (the browser indents their open option list by the padding) and the None chips have no icon. Each section header icon has its own colour (`SEC_TINT`): personal blue, dental teal, medical status rose, habits amber, allergies orange, women pink, blood red, conditions violet, confirm emerald. `sectionsDone()` / `progress()` in the page script drive it; the women-only section only counts when Female is picked.

**My Profile no longer links the Patient Information Record (2026-10-20).** The "Patient Information Record / View / Update" card was removed from `modules/shared/profile.php` (redundant, user request); the form is reached from Dental Records' "Update Patient Information Record" link (and the booking gate).

**Readable Medical History summary (2026-10-20).** `healthFormSummary()` now writes the Patient Information Record as groups (DENTAL HISTORY, GENERAL MEDICAL STATUS, HABITS, ALLERGIES, FOR WOMEN, BLOOD, MEDICAL CONDITIONS), each an all-caps heading with one "• answer" per line and a blank line between groups. The dentist's Medical History box grows to fit it (`fitHistory()`, 200 to 640px); the patient Dental Records page shows the all-caps headings in bold (`fmtNote()`). General Condition (`healthFormStatusText()`) likewise lists "• question: YES / No", a Yes item's details on the next line, and a blank line between items; the dentist box grows to fit (`fitCondition()`, 72 to 320px). The 2 live records already answered were rewritten to the new layout with a one-off runner (local had none).

**Patient Information Record: locked after submit, history kept, 6-month review (migration 082, 2026-10-20).** Supersedes the "summary goes into Medical History" parts above.
- **Log:** every submission is a new `health_form_logs` row (answers JSON, readable summary, `submitted_at`); nothing old is overwritten. `patient_records.health_form` / `health_form_at` still hold the latest (prefill + state). Migration 082 turned each existing answer into its first log entry and took the copied summary back out of `medical_history`.
- **States** (`healthFormState()` in `includes/health_form.php`, DB clock, `HF_REVIEW_MONTHS = 6`): first (never answered), locked (submitted, read-only until `review_from`), due (6 months passed). `api/health_form.php action=save` refuses a locked record (409). `healthFormReady()` = submitted and not due: Appointments / Calendar View / `action=create` / `patientLandingUrl()` send a due patient back to the form to review it (other patient pages only need `healthFormDone()`).
- **Patient form page:** locked = green "Submitted on … and locked" banner, every field disabled, no save bar; due = amber "Time to review your record" banner, prefilled, confirm box unticked, button "Submit Updated Record". After saving it goes to Appointments.
- **Dentist:** Patient Details shows a read-only "Patient Information Record" card ("Submitted · Locked", an Entry select with every submission, newest first; `api/records.php action=get` returns `health_logs` for dentist / admin only). Medical History / Notes is now only the dentist's own notes (placeholder asks for what the patient clarified at the exam). A new submission still refreshes allergies, dentures, General Condition, sex and emergency contact.
- **Patient Dental Records:** shows the latest entry read-only with its date and the next review date (`api/my_record.php` returns `health_logs` + `health_state`); the header button reads "View Patient Information Record" or "Review & Update…" when due.
- **Summary text (2026-10-20):** `healthFormSummary()` has no "PATIENT INFORMATION RECORD (answered …)" title line (the entry date shows beside the card) and no GENERAL MEDICAL STATUS group (those answers are in the General Condition box). Existing log summaries were rebuilt (live 2 entries).
- **General Condition box removed (2026-10-20, later):** supersedes the "Summary text" bullet above. The 4 General Medical Status answers (medical treatment, serious illness, medications, anesthesia reactions) are back in `healthFormSummary()` as a GENERAL MEDICAL STATUS group, so they live only in the locked intake log. The General Condition box was removed from the dentist Patient Details and the patient Dental Records page; `api/health_form.php` no longer writes `patient_records.general_condition` and it is out of `api/records.php` `$TEXT_FIELDS` / `api/my_record.php` (column kept, unused). Live log summaries rebuilt.
- **Renamed (2026-10-20):** "Dental Chart & Medical History" is now "Dental Chart & Clinical Notes" and the "Medical History / Notes" box is "Clinical Notes" (dentist Patient Details + patient Dental Records). The column is still `medical_history`.
- **Dentures + allergies off the record card (2026-10-21):** the Upper / Lower Denture and Allergies boxes were removed from "Dental Chart & Clinical Notes" (dentist Patient Details + patient Dental Records); they are in the Patient Information Record. The patient page now shows only Clinical Notes + the tooth chart. `api/records.php action=save` now skips any clinical field the form did not send, so the intake-filled denture / allergy values are kept (still hidden from the receptionist).
- **Abnormalities beside Gender (2026-10-21):** on the dentist Patient Details, Abnormalities now fills the 2 columns next to Gender (`.rec-span2`) instead of its own full row, so there is no empty gap; below 861px it takes the full row.
- **"Consultation Notes" renamed "Visit Notes" (2026-10-21):** the dentist's per-visit note box in the Services Availed pop-up (`logProcedureModal()`) is now "Visit Notes" (hint: "Saved to this visit only…"), shown as "Visit notes (dentist)" in the appointment View and "Visit notes" on the patient's Treatment & Transactions list. Storage is unchanged (`dentist_notes` → the `Treatment notes:` line in `appointments.notes`). Clinical Notes stays the patient's long-term chart note.
- **Manual GCash reference when the screenshot can't be read (2026-10-21):** the locked GCash Reference No. box (booking wizard + Resubmit Payment) now opens for typing (`setRefManual(true)`) when the screenshot passed its checks but no reference was read, or the reader couldn't load; the note reads "The reference number could not be read. Please use the manual input below." (`REF_UNREAD_MSG`, also used on the Bank / other-method boxes). A new upload locks and clears it again. A screenshot with problems keeps it locked. Every reference box (screenshot upload on or off) also shows an amber "Note: Before submitting, please double check the details and the reference number so your request is not declined."
- **Double-check note only when the reference can't be read (2026-10-21):** supersedes "every reference box also shows" above. With the screenshot upload on, the amber "Before submitting, please double check…" note (`#refCheckNote`) stays hidden and appears only together with "The reference number could not be read…" (`setRefNote()` toggles it on `REF_UNREAD_MSG`); a new upload hides it again. With the upload off it always shows, since the reference is always typed.
- **Locked-reference hint removed; My Availability button renamed (2026-10-21):** the grey "This is read from your screenshot and cannot be typed…" line under the locked GCash box is gone (`#refHint` stays hidden until manual mode, where it shows "Type the 13-digit reference number…"). On the dentist My Availability pop-ups the "Save Availability" buttons now read "Save Changes".
- **My Appointments paging fix (2026-10-21):** on wide screens Next jumped back to page 1: changing page toggled the scrollbar, the ResizeObserver saw the ~15px width change, the column count flipped between 3 and 4 (6 vs 8 per page) and re-paged. The observer now ignores width changes under 24px (`GRID_W`), and `.appt-grid` gets an explicit `repeat(COLS, minmax(0,1fr))` from the same count, so the grid always matches the page size and cards fill rows left to right.
- **Appointment cards min width 330px (2026-10-21):** `.appt-grid` and `gridCols()` now use 330px (was 340px), so a 1920px monitor (zoom 1.15) fits 4 columns = 8 per page; a 1366px laptop stays at 3 columns = 6 per page.
- **GCash recipient check (2026-10-21):** in the booking wizard + Resubmit Payment, a GCash screenshot's "+63 9•• ••• 4252" line must end in the last 4 digits of Settings' GCash number (`pay_gcash_number`), else the upload box shows "This payment wasn't sent to the clinic's GCash number (ending in 4252)" and Submit stays blocked. The reader often turns "9••" into "Yee", so any "+63" line counts. If no such line is read, a 'soft' row ("Couldn't read the recipient's number…") shows in an amber box without blocking Submit (`hasBlockingWarning()`; soft rows also don't stop the reference auto-fill). Bank / other methods are not checked.
- **Reservation fee counts as revenue on the day it's paid (migration 083, 2026-10-21):** supersedes "revenue is recognised on paid_at, fully_paid, total - discount". `payments.reservation_paid_at` (set at online booking, payment resubmission and the walk-in counter) and `reservation_confirmed_at` (set at online Approve; walk-ins at once; cleared on resubmit). `revenueLinesSql()` (functions.php) gives one row per fee (day paid, once confirmed, kept even if the visit is later cancelled or a no-show, since it is non-refundable) and one per settled bill (rest of the bill = total - discount - confirmed fee, floored at 0, on the settlement day). Used by Revenue Reports (`api/reports.php action=revenue`, fee rows read "Reservation fee: <service>"), `report_print.php`, and the dashboard Revenue Summary + its drill-down. Unapproved / declined online fees don't count. The backfill marked fees confirmed for walk-ins and approved / rescheduled / completed / no-show / checked-in visits (cancelled ones excluded, since we can't tell if they were approved).
- **OR / Invoice Setup, cashier only (migration 084, 2026-10-21, adviser feedback):** new cashier page `modules/cashier/or_setup.php` (sidebar "OR / Invoice Setup", under Billing) + `api/or_series.php` (`apiAuth(['cashier'])`: GET `status`, POST `create {start,end}`, `realign {next}`, `reset`). Tables `or_series` (start_no / end_no / next_no / digits, status active / used_up / closed; one active at a time) and `or_issued` (one row per receipt: or_no UNIQUE, payment, amount, `day_seq` = transaction # that day); `payments.or_number` = the latest OR given to the bill. Helpers `orActiveSeries()` / `orStatus()` / `orIssueNext()` in `api/_bootstrap.php`. A new range can't include an already-issued number; saving a new series closes the current one; Re-align moves the next number within the range (spoiled / skipped receipt); Reset closes the series; the last number used marks it used_up. **Every Pay (full or installment, incl. a ₱0 reservation-covered bill) needs an active series:** the Billing screen shows "Transaction #N today · Next OR No. 000305 · N left" (`#orBar`, Pay disabled with a setup link when there is none), Pay first opens "Check the Receipt in the Printer" (`confirmOr()`), and `api/payments.php action=settle` requires `or_number` to equal the series' next number (409 if not) and issues it (History Log line names the OR No. + transaction #). The printed invoice itself is unchanged (the pad already has its own number). Admin has no access to this page.
- **OR number on the printed invoice (2026-10-21):** the printed Service Invoice (billing.php `buildPrintDoc()` + `modules/shared/receipt.php`) shows "OR No. 000305" on its own line under the F.G. Vitug printer block, bottom left (`.si-pfoot` / `.si-orno` in `invoice_style.php`). It is the bill's `payments.or_number`; an unpaid Print Preview shows the series' next number (`printOrNo()`), and an old bill with no OR shows nothing.
- **Transaction search is name only (2026-10-21):** the cashier Transaction page search box (`#txSearch`, placeholder "Search patient name") now matches the patient name only; OR number, reference no., service and dentist no longer match.
- **OR Setup lists are paginated (2026-10-21):** Today's Transactions (`#todayPager`) and Series History (`#histPager`) on `modules/cashier/or_setup.php` show 10 rows per page via `window.pageSlice()`; a reload keeps the page. The count beside Today's Transactions is the full day's total. `api/or_series.php` now returns up to 500 past series (was 50).
- **Voided Receipts, admin only (migration 085, 2026-10-21, adviser feedback):** new admin page `modules/admin/voids.php` (sidebar "Voided Receipts", under Reports) + `api/or_voids.php` (`apiAuth(['admin'])`: GET `lookup&or=`, GET `list`, POST `void {or_no, reason_code, reason_text}`). Reasons: Wrong Printing / Paper Jam, OR Number Mismatch, Damaged / Chewed-up Receipt, Customer Transaction Cancelled, Other (typed, required); the confirm pop-up also needs "I have checked the physical receipt" ticked. Table `or_voids` (one row per voided number: OR No., transaction #, issued time, patient, amount, cashier and admin names, reason); `or_issued` gained `pay_cash` / `pay_gcash` / `pay_bank` (each receipt's split, saved by `orIssueNext()`) and `voided_at`. **Voiding an issued receipt takes that payment back off the bill** (method amounts reduced, `or_number` back to the bill's previous receipt or NULL, `payment_date` / received / change cleared when it was the only receipt, then `recomputePayment()`), so it drops out of revenue and the bill returns to the cashier's Transaction queue to be paid again on a fresh receipt; only a bill's latest receipt can be voided. A blank sheet that was never issued (must be inside an OR series) can be voided too. A voided number can never be issued: `orIssueNext()` 409s, Re-align refuses it, a new series can't include it, and `orStatus()` returns `next_voided` so Billing (`#orBar`, Pay locked) and OR Setup warn the cashier to re-align. The Billing bar's link is now "Re-align Next Number" (opens the Re-align pop-up on OR Setup via `sessionStorage dgt_or_realign`); OR Setup's Today's Transactions marks voided rows. There is no cash-drawer / end-of-shift report in the app, so nothing else to exclude. History Log line per void.
- **Billing: no printer confirm, reservation ref shown (2026-10-21):** supersedes the "Check the Receipt in the Printer" step in the OR Setup note: Pay now sends the next OR number straight away (`confirmOr()` removed; the Billing bar still shows the next OR No. and the server still checks it). On an online booking, the "Less: Reservation Fee Paid" row on the cashier Billing screen shows the reservation payment's reference beside it, e.g. "(GCash Ref: 9258403963429)" (`resvRefNote()`, screen only, `no-print`); walk-ins show nothing.
- **Cashier can add services at checkout again (migration 086, 2026-10-21, adviser feedback):** supersedes "only the dentist sets services" for additions. The Billing screen has **+ Add Service** under the line items (unpaid bills only): each added row (`addExtraRow()` in `billing.php`) is a dropdown of active services not already on the bill (`service_catalog` from `action=invoice`), a Qty box editable only for per-unit services, a typed Unit Cost (starts at `base_price`, must be above 0, minus / plus / e blocked) and an × to remove it. The dentist's own lines stay read-only. Pay sends `extra_services [{service_id, qty, unit_cost}]`; `api/payments.php action=settle` checks each (active, not already on the visit, price > 0, qty forced 1 for single-session), adds them to the invoice and total, and inserts them into `appointment_services` with `added_by` = the cashier (new column; NULL = logged by the dentist), so they show on the visit's record and future installments. The History Log line lists "cashier added <service> <amount>". Also fixed: after a full payment the OR bar now redraws to "Paid on OR No. …".
- **Void columns (2026-10-22):** the admin Voided Receipts History table no longer has a Transaction # column (the Find Receipt card still shows it). The cashier OR Setup Today's Transactions table shows a Status column (VOIDED red / Valid green) only when a receipt that day was voided (`#todayStatusTh`); the inline VOIDED tag next to the OR No. was removed.
- **Cashier + Add Service button matches the dentist one (2026-10-22):** dashed teal outline, teal bold text (same classes as `#t_add` in `logProcedureModal()`).
- **Same-day walk-in cut-off (2026-10-22):** new walk-ins for today are refused once the DB clock passes `walkinCutoffHm()` (last slot start minus 1 hour, 3:15 PM with the 4:15 PM last slot; booking AT 3:15 is still allowed). `action=walk_in` 422s with `walkinCutoffMsg()`; `action=availability&walkin=1&walkin_new=1` (the walk-in calendar only) marks today closed with that message and returns `walkin_closed` / `walkin_cutoff`, and the Walk-in modal shows the amber warning above the calendar (`#w_cutoffNote`). The check-in late-arrival lookup does not send `walkin_new`, so already-booked patients are checked in and served until closing.
- **Duplicate patient prevention (2026-10-22), composite check:** sign-up (`login.php`) is blocked when (first + last name, any case, AND birthdate) match an active patient (`existingPatientRecord()` in functions.php; same name with a different birthdate is a different person and is allowed) OR the e-mail OR the mobile number already belongs to an account (a pending unverified sign-up with the same e-mail still resumes) sets `$regError = DUPLICATE_RECORD_MSG` and shows a "Record already exists" pop-up (`#dupOverlay`: message, clinic phone / e-mail / address, Close, and **Forgot Password / Recover Account**, which opens the Forgot Password modal with the e-mail filled in via `window.openForgotPw(addr)`); no account is created. Staff creates (`api/records.php action=create` = walk-in New patient + Create Record, `api/users.php action=add` for patients) refuse a taken e-mail with "Record already exists for this e-mail address. Search for the patient under Existing patient / Patient Records…"; a taken phone there still shows `PHONE_TAKEN_MSG`.
- **Walk-in New patient duplicate check (2026-10-22):** `api/records.php action=create` with `walk_in` refuses (409, `duplicate: true`) the same composite as sign-up: first + last name AND birthdate (`existingPatientRecord()`), OR the e-mail, OR the mobile number; the message names the existing patient and says to switch to Existing patient (a staff account match says to use different details). The Walk-in modal shows it in a `window.noticePopup()` "Record already exists" pop-up. + Create Record (no `walk_in`) keeps the old e-mail / phone checks.
- **Revenue report range ends at the last transaction (2026-10-22):** the Generate PDF / Preview document (`docTo()` in `modules/admin/reports.php` `buildDoc()` + the PDF filename) and `report_print.php` end the range at the latest transaction date in the period (Oct with the last payment on Oct 20: 10-01-2026 – 10-20-2026); with no transactions, a still-running period ends at today. The start date and the query window are unchanged.
- **"Invoice #" replaced by the OR number (2026-10-22):** the internal payment_id is no longer shown as "Invoice #N". New payment / void log lines name the OR No. and link by "Appointment #N" instead; `api/logs.php` rewrites older stored lines on display only ("Invoice #N" becomes "OR No. <payments.or_number>", or is dropped when the bill has no OR; activity_log rows are not changed) and returns `target.or_no` (Audit Logs detail shows "Appointment #847 · OR No. 000301"). The receipt page title, the receipt e-mail and the cashier Transaction Logs use `payments.or_number` only; the old OR-<payment_id> number is gone everywhere (a bill with no OR shows "Service Invoice" as the receipt title, "—" in Transaction Logs, and the receipt e-mail drops its OR No. row and subject number).
- **Billing line-item Amount heading right-aligned (2026-10-22):** `.inv-lineitems th.col-amt { text-align: right }` in `includes/invoice_style.php`, so "AMOUNT" lines up with the peso amounts on the cashier Billing screen.
- **Brute-force lock (migration 087, 2026-10-23):** table `login_attempts` (scope, ident, ip, created_at) + `throttleLocked()` / `throttleFail()` / `throttleClear()` / `throttleWaitText()` in `includes/functions.php` (`THROTTLE_MAX_FAILS` 5 per username / e-mail, `THROTTLE_MAX_IP_FAILS` 20 per IP, `THROTTLE_WINDOW_MIN` 15; DB clock; fails open if the table is missing). Sign-in (`login.php`, scope `login`, by username): the 4th and 5th wrong passwords warn "N attempts left", the 5th locks sign-in for that username for 15 minutes (even the right password is refused, "Too many failed sign-in attempts. Please try again in N minutes…"), History Log line "Sign-in locked…"; a successful sign-in clears the count. The 6-digit codes get the same lock per e-mail: `api/verify_otp.php` (scope `verify_otp`) and `api/check_reset_otp.php` + `api/reset_password_otp.php` (scope `reset_otp`), answering 429 "Too many wrong codes…". Rows older than a day are pruned now and then.
- **1-day advance fallback (2026-10-23):** `onlineLeadDays()` now falls back to 1 day (was a stale 2) if the `enforce_two_day_advance` setting can't be read.
- **Usual Blood Pressure removed (2026-10-23):** the Patient Information Record's Blood card now asks only Blood Type (`HF_BLOOD_PRESSURE` and the `blood_pressure` answer are gone; older locked entries keep their saved line).
- **Denture questions removed too (2026-10-23):** the Patient Information Record no longer asks about upper / lower dentures (form, `healthFormValidate()`, summary; `healthFormDentureText()` deleted; `api/health_form.php` no longer writes `denture_upper` / `denture_lower`). Every saved intake summary (`health_form_logs.summary` + `patient_records.health_form`) was rebuilt from its answers so Dental Records and Patient Details no longer show the denture or blood pressure lines (live 9 entries, local none). The old answers stay in the answers JSON.
- **For Someone Else is children only (2026-10-23):** online bookings for someone else are only for children 1 to 17 (`OTHER_MAX_AGE` / `OTHER_ADULT_MSG` in functions.php). The booking wizard's new-person Date of Birth covers ages 1 to 17, a note under the choice says adults can create their own account, and the Person list hides people now 18+. Server: `createUnlinkedPatientRecord()` refuses 18+, and `action=create` refuses re-picking a person now 18+ (a dentist's follow-up plan for them is exempt). Supersedes the 1-to-80 range.
- **"For Someone Else" renamed "For My Child" (2026-10-24):** the booking wizard choice reads For My Child, the picker is labelled Child with "+ Add a Child", and `OTHER_ADULT_MSG` / `dayTakenPopup()` say "For My Child" / "your child". Internal values (`booking_for = 'other'`) are unchanged.
- **Grace period, end-of-day No-Show, walk-in priority, forward-only patient reschedule (migration 088, 2026-10-24, capstone logic doc):** supersedes "No Show is end-of-day only" (manual button) and "a cancelled seat reopens for everyone".
  - **Delayed / Standby:** `markDelayedAppointments()` (`api/_bootstrap.php`) stamps `appointments.delayed_at` on today's approved / rescheduled visits not checked in `GRACE_MINUTES` (15, functions.php) after their start, notifies the patient ("Running late") and logs it as user `system` (`systemActor()`). Status stays approved / rescheduled; the badge is derived (`window.apptStatusBadge` / `opStatus` in app.js, `statusOf()` on the patient page, `stKey()` on the admin Appointment page): Delayed / Standby (orange) or, once checked in, Arrived (Late) (teal).
  - **Seats:** capacity queries use `SEAT_SQL` (live status AND `(delayed_at IS NULL OR checked_in_at IS NOT NULL)`), so a delayed patient's seat goes to walk-ins. The patient portal (`action=availability` for the patient role, `create`, patient `reschedule` via `slotIsFull(..., $online = true)`) uses `ONLINE_SEAT_SQL`, which also counts `seat_released_online = 1` rows: `action=cancel` sets it on an approved / rescheduled online booking, so that seat is offered to walk-ins only, never online again.
  - **Arrived (Late):** the receptionist Check In pop-up for a delayed patient (`data-delayed`) lists today's hours that haven't ended and still have room ("Seat in"); none = Reschedule. `action=check_in` requires `seat_slot` for a delayed row, refuses a full / ended hour, moves `time_slot` there (re-routing to another on-duty dentist via `assignAppointmentDentist()` when the dentist is full or off then; follow-up visits keep theirs) and logs "arrived late (booked X), seated at Y". Reschedule clears `delayed_at`.
  - **End-of-day No-Show batch:** `processEndOfDayNoShows()` sets every approved / rescheduled visit never checked in to `no_show` once the day is over (any earlier date, or today after `clinic_close`), notifies (unless `missed_notified_at` is set), syncs treatment plans and logs it as `system`. Arrived (Late) patients are checked in, so they are left for the front desk. Both sweeps run via `runAppointmentSweeps()` at the top of every `api/appointments.php` and `api/dashboard.php` request (no cron). `sendMissedAppointmentNotices()` was removed, and so was the receptionist's manual No Show button (`action=no_show` still exists, no UI).
  - **Patient reschedule is forward only:** a date after the current appointment date (and at least tomorrow); the same date only when the dentist became unavailable. Server message: "You can only move this appointment to <date> or later. To move it to an earlier date, please contact the clinic." The patient reschedule calendar's `minDate` follows it. Staff reschedules are unrestricted (still capped at 5 per hour).
  - **Manual No Show button kept (2026-10-24, user request):** the receptionist's Approved Appointments row shows **No Show** once the patient is Delayed / Standby (or the visit's day has passed) and not checked in; `action=no_show` allows it when `delayed_at` is set or the day is over ("No Show can only be marked once the patient is 15 minutes late" otherwise). The end-of-day batch still marks anyone left after closing.
  - **Reschedule note (2026-10-24):** the patient Reschedule pop-up shows an amber note between the calendar and the Reason for Rescheduling box: "You can only move this appointment to <first allowed date> or later. If you want an earlier date than your appointment (or a fully booked day), please contact the clinic at 0976 448 4252 or customerservice@dgtonedentalph.com."
- **Minor walk-ins under a parent / guardian account, own login at 18 (migration 089, 2026-10-24):** supersedes the `walk-in-minor-booking-workflow` pattern (child's own account with the parent's e-mail).
  - **Schema:** `users.guardian_user_id` (FK users, ON DELETE SET NULL) = the parent / guardian account managing a minor; `users.adult_account_at` = when the child, at 18, got their own login. Backfilled from past "for someone else" bookings (booker's e-mail or none); anyone already 18+ was stamped so no surprise login is sent.
  - **Walk-in modal:** in New patient, a birthdate under 18 shows a **Parent / Guardian** box (`#w_guard`): Existing account (searches adults 18+) or New account (First / Last / Email / Date of Birth 18 to 80, created via `api/records.php action=create` with `guardian: true`, which uses `staffBirthDate()`), plus a required Relationship. The child's own e-mail field hides and the phone becomes "Parent / Guardian Phone Number" (filled + locked from an existing guardian). Field order is now First, Last, Date of Birth, Phone, then Email (adults) or the guardian box. The new guardian's login is shown once (`showCreds(name, creds, child)`); a created guardian stays picked so a retry doesn't make a second account.
  - **Server:** `action=walk_in` with `booking_for: 'other'` books under the guardian (`patient_id`) for an existing linked child (`treated_patient_id`, must have that `guardian_user_id`) or a new one (`new_patient`, created last via `createUnlinkedPatientRecord($in, $by, $guardianId)` with the guardian's phone + e-mail; 1 to 17; duplicate name + birthdate = 409). An existing minor picked directly in Existing patient is re-routed to their guardian. A guardian with a birthdate under 18 is refused. Online "For My Child" now also sets `guardian_user_id` to the booking account.
  - **At 18:** `processDependentsTurning18()` (in `runAppointmentSweeps()`) gives a linked child who has turned 18 a fresh temporary password (`must_change_password = 1`, `is_verified = 1`, `adult_account_at`), clears the guardian's e-mail copied onto them, notifies the guardian (in-app + e-mail, type `dependent_adult`) with the username and temporary password to hand over, sends the child a welcome notice, and logs it as `system`. On first sign-in the child lands on My Profile to set a password and add their own e-mail and phone. Their records and history stay on their account.
- **Money inputs show thousands separators (2026-10-24):** every money input box is `<input type="text" inputmode="decimal" data-money>` (cashier Billing Received Amount, the split Cash / Cash Handed Over / GCash / Bank boxes and added-service Unit Cost; admin Services Base Price; Settings Reservation fee and the discount value, which is `data-money="nopad"`; the walk-in reservation fee; the dentist's Services Availed price boxes). A capture listener in `app.js` adds commas as you type (keeping the caret in place), blocks keys other than digits and ".", allows at most 2 decimals, and pads to ".00" when the box loses focus. Always read these boxes with `window.moneyVal(el)` and fill them with `window.moneyStr(n)` (`window.moneyFormat(str)` formats a typed string): API payloads stay plain numbers, never strings with commas. Displayed amounts already use `peso()` (PHP `number_format`, JS `toLocaleString`). Quantity / duration / years boxes stay `type="number"`. New money inputs must use `data-money`.
- **Cash Sales vs Charge Sales + "Received the Amount of" text (2026-10-24):** supersedes "Cash ticked when fully paid" and "Received the Amount of = reservation + counter payment, in words" in the printed-invoice notes. Both prints (cashier Billing `buildPrintDoc()` / `printFigures()`, and `modules/shared/receipt.php` via `invoicePrintFigures()` in `api/_bootstrap.php`) work out, for the receipt being printed: reservation, prior payments (earlier receipts), today's amount (cash / GCash / bank split) and the remaining balance. Nothing left = **Cash Sales** ticked, Total Amount Due **0.00**, a "Less: Reservation Deposit" row only on the first receipt. Something left = **Charge Sales** ticked, "Less: Reservation Deposit / Down Payment" = today's payment (+ the reservation on the first receipt), Total Amount Due = remaining. A later receipt shows "Less: Previous Payments" (earlier receipts + the reservation). The "Received the Amount of" line comes from `formatReceivedAmountText()` (PHP in `_bootstrap.php`, JS in billing.php): one payment = "Five Hundred Pesos Only (₱500.00) via GCash (Ref: 123…)", several = "Fifteen Thousand Pesos Only (₱15,000.00: ₱500 via GCash Ref: …, ₱5,000 Cash, ₱9,500 via GCash Ref: …)" (Cash pieces have no "via" or ref; whole amounts drop ".00"). The online reservation is a piece (with its method and reference) only on the first receipt. The latest receipt's split comes from `or_issued` (`invoiceLastReceipt()`, sent as `last_receipt` by `api/payments.php action=invoice`; pre-085 receipts fall back to the payment mode). Print Preview of an open bill describes the payment being entered (OR No. = the series' next number); a settled bill prints its last receipt. **Installments now print too:** after an installment Pay the Billing screen re-renders and prints that receipt (it used to only reload). There is no Check payment method; bank pieces read "via Bank Transfer" (the payer's bank isn't recorded).
- **OTP boxes no longer block paste (2026-10-24, Lighthouse Best Practices):** the 6-digit code boxes (login.php Forgot Password + sign-up verification, verify.php) used to `preventDefault()` the paste event and fill the boxes themselves, which Lighthouse fails as "Prevents users from pasting into input fields" (Best Practices 88). The paste handler now only records the pasted digits (`box._paste`); `takePaste(box)` spreads them over the boxes on the following input event, or on a `setTimeout(0)` when the browser inserted nothing (box already full). Never `preventDefault()` a paste on an input again.
- **SEO on app pages (2026-10-24, Lighthouse):** every `modules/**` page scored SEO 58 because robots.txt blocked `/modules/` (is-crawlable) and there was no meta description. `includes/layout.php` now prints a meta description ("<page title> in the DGT One Dental Center - Bulacan clinic portal…") and a canonical link (current URL, no query), and robots.txt no longer blocks `/modules/` (only `/modules/shared/receipt`). App pages stay private because they need a login: a crawler is redirected to the sign-in page. Supersedes "robots.txt blocks /modules/" in the SEO basics note.
- **Total Amount Paid on the printed invoice (2026-10-26):** both prints (billing.php `buildPrintDoc()`, `modules/shared/receipt.php`) add a "TOTAL AMOUNT PAID" row under Total Amount Due = reservation fee + earlier receipts + this receipt (`resv + prior + today` from `invoicePrintFigures()` / `printFigures()`), so a fully paid receipt (Total Amount Due 0.00) still shows how much was paid.
  **Update (same day): totals order.** After the VAT / Withholding rows both prints now read: Less: Reservation Deposit (when paid), Less: Previous Payments (earlier receipts only, shown when any), Less: Payments Received Today (this receipt), TOTAL AMOUNT PAID, then TOTAL AMOUNT DUE last. Replaces the Cash/Charge-specific "Reservation Deposit / Down Payment" row; Cash Sales / Charge Sales ticking is unchanged.
  **Labels (same day):** on both prints "TOTAL SALES" now reads "TOTAL TREATMENT" and "TOTAL AMOUNT DUE" reads "REMAINING BALANCE (TOTAL DUE)". The live Billing screen labels are unchanged.
  **Wrap fix (same day):** `.si-tot th` (invoice_style.php, screen + print + the `.due` row) is `white-space: normal` now, so long labels like "Less: Payments Received Today" and "REMAINING BALANCE (TOTAL DUE)" wrap to 2 lines inside their cell instead of running past the column line.
  **Remaining Balance only on installments (same day):** both prints show the "REMAINING BALANCE (TOTAL DUE)" row only when a balance is still owed (Charge Sales). A fully paid receipt ends at "TOTAL AMOUNT PAID", which then gets the bold `due` row style.
- **Excess reservation returned as change (migration 090, 2026-10-26):** when the reservation fee paid online is more than the final bill (e.g. ₱1,000 deposit on a ₱700 visit), Pay on a full payment hands the difference back. `api/payments.php action=settle` computes `max(reservation + earlier payments - bill after discounts, 0)`, stores it in `payments.reservation_refund` and adds it to `change_amount` (History Log line "returned ₱300.00 excess reservation as change"). Installments never refund. The Billing screen labels it "Change (excess reservation)"; both prints add "Less: Excess Reservation Returned (Change)" and Total Amount Paid nets it out (`refund` in `invoicePrintFigures()` / `printFigures()`); the receipt e-mail says "Change (excess reservation)". `revenueLinesSql()` adds a negative `refund` row on the settlement day (method cash), so Revenue Reports / the dashboard count only what was kept; the report and drill-down queries use `rl.amount <> 0` (was `> 0`). Voiding the receipt resets `reservation_refund` to 0. Supersedes "Overpayment beyond the bill is not refunded or tracked" (migration 068 note).
- **Appointment View billing labels (2026-10-26):** in `appointmentModal()` (app.js) "Payments logged to date" now reads "Less: Payments received" (the minus means it is subtracted, like the other "Less:" lines), and a bill with an excess reservation returned shows "Add: Excess reservation returned +₱300.00" so the block still adds up to the remaining balance.
- **Revenue Reports "View Transaction" (2026-10-26):** the transaction pop-up's button now reads "View Transaction" (was "View Appointment") and opens the shared View modal titled "Transaction #N"; `window.appointmentModal(id, { title })` takes an optional title (default "Appointment").
- **Password eye shows the state (2026-10-26):** `pwToggleBtn()` now starts with the slashed eye (password hidden) and `password-toggle.js` swaps to the open eye once the password is visible (it used to show the action instead: open eye = "click to show"). The aria-label still names the action (Show / Hide password).
  Tested 2026-10-26 in headless Chrome with a throwaway patient (sign in, register, My Profile before and after a real password change, Forgot Password): every eye toggles password/text with the matching icon. `tailwind.php` also hides Edge's built-in `::-ms-reveal` / `::-ms-clear` buttons, which showed a second eye that toggled on its own.
- **Approval queue shows the requested time (2026-10-26):** the receptionist's Appointments for Approval column is now "Date & Time": the date with the chosen time (`fmtTime(a.time_slot)`, e.g. 10:00 AM) on a line under it, bold DGT blue (same size as the date) so it stands out.
- **2 places per hour: 1 in-house + 1 roaming (2026-10-26).** Supersedes every "5 per hour / 4 in-house + 1 roaming / 5 chairs" rule above. `slotCapacity()` (api/appointments.php) returns 2. `dentistHourCap()`: each dentist may hold 1 booking an hour while a dentist of the other type (in-house vs roaming) is on duty then, both places when nobody of the other type is. So the online waterfall (`assignAppointmentDentist()`, `$hourlyCap = slotCapacity() - 1` = 1) gives the first booking in an hour to the in-house dentist, the second to the roaming one, then the hour is full; the walk-in roaming lock (`roamingLockedBy()`) opens once the in-house dentist has 1; `planSlotProblem()` uses `dentistHourCap()` for an in-house follow-up; `processScheduleConflicts()` (api/schedules.php) moves a patient to a roaming dentist only while they hold fewer than 2. Calendars, "slots left", reschedule and check-in seat checks all read `slotCapacity()`, so they follow automatically. Verified locally (rolled-back test: Raffy, then Mark Joseph, then Full). No live or local upcoming hour held more than 2 bookings when this shipped.
- **Rescheduling a visit the roaming dentist declined (2026-10-26):** the receptionist Reschedule calendar sends `&declined_id=<id>` when the visit has no dentist and `oncall_declined_at` is set. `action=availability` (staff only) then keeps an hour only while an on-duty dentist other than the one who declined still has a place (`dentistHourCount() < dentistHourCap()`, the visit itself left out); every other hour shows as Unavailable, and the range opens from today (it used to fall back to the online lead time because the visit has no dentist). `action=reschedule` still refuses such an hour server-side. Tested on local with seeded visits (deleted): with Raffy holding 1 patient at 10:00 AM, 10:00 AM disappears for the declined visit and stays visible without the parameter.
- **Patient Information Record: 3-month review, dentist can edit (2026-10-26).** `HF_REVIEW_MONTHS` is 3 (was 6), so a submitted record locks for 3 months, then the patient must review it before booking again. A dentist can now update it: Patient Details > Patient Information Record has an **Edit Record** button (**Fill In Record** when never answered) opening `modules/patient/health_form?patient=<id>` (the same form page, `checkAuth(['patient','dentist'])`, mode `staff`: a "You are updating X's record" banner, never locked, confirm box reads "checked with the patient during the visit", saving returns to Patient Records). `api/health_form.php` accepts the dentist role with `?patient=` / body `patient_id`; a dentist save is a new `health_form_logs` row with `submitted_by` = the dentist (earlier entries kept), refreshes allergies / sex / emergency contact like a patient save, logs "Updated the Patient Information Record of X", and keeps the patient's own review clock (`health_form_at = COALESCE(health_form_at, NOW())`). `healthFormLogs()` returns `by_name` / `by_role`; the dentist's Entry list reads "Oct 26, 2026 · updated by Dr. X" or "by the patient", and the patient's Dental Records badge reads "Updated by Dr. X on …". Tested locally with a throwaway patient (deleted).
- **Calendar "slots left" counts patient places (2026-10-26):** the day tag in all three booking calendars (patient Calendar View, booking wizard / patient Reschedule, receptionist walk-in / Reschedule; `cellInfo()`) now adds up every hour's free places from `action=availability`'s `left` map, so an empty 6-hour day reads "12 slots left" (2 an hour, in-house + roaming) instead of "6". Hours that have passed or are too soon count 0.
- **Time pop-up shows places per hour (2026-10-26):** each free hour in `window.openSlotPopup()` now reads "2 of 2 slots left" / "1 of 2 slots left" (`window.slotsLeftText(n)`, from `action=availability`'s `left[slot]`) instead of "Available"; picking a time shows "✓ Selected" and the others keep their count (`data-sub`). The per-hour number is one constant, `SLOT_CAPACITY` (includes/functions.php, 2), returned by `slotCapacity()`, used by `processScheduleConflicts()` and exposed to the page as `window.SLOT_CAP` (layout.php). Change the clinic's per-hour places there.
- **Dentist types straight into the Patient Information Record (2026-10-26):** supersedes "Edit Record opens the question form". In the dentist Patient Details, **Edit Record** turns the latest entry into a text box (Save Record / Cancel); `api/health_form.php action=save_text` (dentist only, `patient_id` + `summary`) saves the typed text as a new `health_form_logs` entry under the dentist's name (answers copied from the previous entry, earlier entries kept, review date unchanged) and updates `patient_records.health_form`'s summary. Typed text does not refresh the Allergies / sex / emergency-contact columns. A never-answered record still shows **Fill In Record**, which opens the question form.
- **Per-tooth conditions, quick tags and notes (migration 091, 2026-10-26, thesis defense):** supersedes the click-to-cycle tooth chart and the 3 states (Resto / Root Canal / Extracted). Conditions are Healthy / Normal (no key), Decayed / Caries (`caries`, D, brown spot), For Extraction (`extract`, FX, orange arrow), Missing / Empty Slot (`missing`, X), Restored / Filled / Pasta (`resto`, R), Root Canal Treated (`rct`) (`TOOTH_STATES` in `api/_bootstrap.php`, `window.TOOTH_STATUS` in app.js). New table `tooth_records` (patient, tooth, status, tags '|' list from `TOOTH_TAGS` (12 quick tags, same list in app.js), note ≤500, appointment_id, visit_date, recorded_by): one row per tooth per visit, the latest row = that tooth's current tags / note, older rows are its history. `patient_records.tooth_chart` stays the status map. `saveToothEntries()` / `toothRecordsFor()` in `_bootstrap.php`. The board, legend, tooth pop-up (`window.toothDetailPopup`: condition buttons, quick-tag chips, optional note, earlier records for that tooth; z-140 above the modal), chart state (`window.toothChartState`) and `window.toothBoardHtml` / `toothLegendHtml` / `toothHistoryHtml` live in app.js. **Dentist Patient Details:** clicking a tooth opens the pop-up (read-only with its records while not editing; editable after Edit Dental Info, saved with Save Changes as `tooth_entries`, under today's date); a purple dot marks teeth with tags / a note; a "Tooth Records by Visit (N)" list sits under the legend. `api/records.php action=save` no longer touches `tooth_chart` unless an old client sends it. **Perform Procedure:** opens "Update Tooth Chart" first (`window.toothChartStep`; `api/records.php action=chart&appointment_id=` / POST `save_teeth`, dentist's own visit only, saved under the visit's date), then "Save Chart & Continue" / "Continue to Services" opens Services Availed. Logging Extraction / Resto / Root Canal still marks those teeth and now also sets that visit's tooth record status (tags / note kept). The patient Dental Records page shows the new conditions (no tags / notes).
- **More quick tags, grouped (2026-10-26):** the tooth pop-up's quick tags are now 33 in 3 groups (`window.TOOTH_TAG_GROUPS` in app.js, same flat list as `TOOTH_TAGS` in `_bootstrap.php`): Symptoms (sensitive to cold / sweets, pain when biting / at night, gum swelling, bleeding gums), Findings (deep cavity, fractured, loose, plaque / tartar, stain, gum recession, abscess, old / temporary filling, root stump, impacted, wisdom tooth erupting, bracket loose, wire poking) and Recommended, matching the services (needs cleaning / filling (Resto) / root canal / extraction / X-ray / bridge / denture / veneer / whitening / gum treatment / surgery, braces adjustment, monitor next visit). All 12 earlier tags are kept. `saveToothEntries()` refuses a tooth whose tags exceed the 500-character column.
- **Tooth pop-up from the Services Availed picker (2026-10-26):** in `logProcedureModal()`, picking a tooth on an Extraction / Resto / Root Canal row opens `window.toothDetailPopup()` for that tooth, with the condition locked to the service's result (Missing / Resto / Root Canal; the other condition buttons are greyed out via `lockStatus`) and the tooth's latest tags / note prefilled (from `records.php?action=chart`, fetched when the modal opens). Applied tags / notes show a purple dot on the tooth button. Right after `treatments.php action=log` succeeds they are saved with `records.php action=save_teeth` under this visit, for teeth still picked. Clicking a picked tooth again unpicks it and drops its note.
- **Tooth chart again after the procedure (2026-10-26):** once `treatments.php action=log` succeeds, `logProcedureModal()` opens `window.toothChartStep({ appt, after: true, onDone })`: titled "Update Tooth Chart After Procedure", buttons Skip / Done ("Save Chart & Finish" once a tooth changed); saving uses `save_teeth` under the same visit. The dashboard reloads (onDone) after Skip or Done. Flow is now chart, Services Availed, chart.
- **Update (2026-10-26): the second tooth chart comes before payment.** Supersedes "Tooth chart again after the procedure". In `logProcedureModal()`, **Proceed to Payment** now validates the services and switches the same pop-up to "Update Tooth Chart: <name>" (`#t_chart`; the services form `#t_form` is hidden, not lost) with the teeth just treated already marked (pending, with the tags / note from the tooth pop-up). Buttons: **Back to Services** (chart edits kept) and **Save & Proceed to Payment**, which logs the procedure (`treatments.php action=log`) and then saves every chart change with `save_teeth` under this visit. `toothChartStep()` still takes `after` but nothing passes it now.
- **Update (2026-10-26, later): the chart is a button, not a forced step.** Supersedes the note above: Proceed to Payment logs the visit straight away again. The Services Availed footer is Cancel / **Update Tooth Chart** (solid blue `bg-accent` with a tooth icon, so it stands out from the green Proceed) / Proceed to Payment; Update Tooth Chart switches the pop-up to the chart (picked teeth already marked), with Back to Services and Proceed to Payment. Chart changes (and tooth pop-up tags / notes) are saved with `save_teeth` right after the log, whichever screen Proceed was pressed on.
- **Totals on the tooth chart screen (2026-10-26):** the Update Tooth Chart view inside Services Availed now ends with the same Total Charges / Less: reservation fee paid / Balance to Cashier block as the services screen (copied from `#t_total` / `#t_net` each time the chart redraws).
- **No tooth chart before Services Availed (2026-10-26):** **Perform Procedure** now opens Services Availed directly; the "Update Tooth Chart" pop-up that opened first was removed (`modules/dentist/dashboard.php`). The chart is still reachable from the **Update Tooth Chart** button inside Services Availed, and from Patient Details.
- **Services Availed is one two-column pop-up (2026-10-26, adviser feedback):** supersedes the Update Tooth Chart button and its separate chart screen. `logProcedureModal()` (app.js) puts the patient's tooth chart on the left (`#t_chartCol`, sticky on wide screens, stacked on top below `lg:`) and the services / visit notes / follow-up / assistant / bill on the right (`#t_form`); the footer is just Cancel / Proceed to Payment. Clicking a chart tooth opens the tooth pop-up; setting it to Resto, Root Canal or Missing (when it wasn't already that) puts the tooth on that service's row, adding the row if needed (`onChartApply()` / `rowFor()`); any other condition takes it off. Extraction / Resto / Root Canal rows no longer show their own tooth grid (kept hidden as the row's record, `.svc-pick`): they show "Teeth: 36, 42" or a hint to mark the tooth on the chart. Chart edits are saved with `save_teeth` right after the log, as before. Width override + tighter board in `includes/records_chart_style.php` (`.proc-split`, up to 1340px).
- **Tooth chart moved to a slide-over drawer (2026-10-26, adviser feedback):** supersedes the two-column Services Availed pop-up. `logProcedureModal()` (app.js) is one column again (services, visit notes, follow-up, assistant, bill) with a "Tooth Chart" bar at the top ("N teeth updated this visit" + a blue **Edit Tooth Chart** button); the "Edit on chart" / "Edit Tooth Chart" links on Extraction / Resto / Root Canal rows open it too. The drawer (`#t_drawer`, appended to `<body>`, z-125: above the modal, below the tooth pop-up `.tooth-pop` z-140) slides in from the right (`.tooth-drawer .dr-panel` translateX, 300 ms, CSS in `includes/records_chart_style.php`; none under reduced motion) over a dark backdrop and holds the board + legend. Tooth changes apply to the service rows at once (same `onChartApply()`); Done, X, a backdrop click or Escape slide it out (Escape closes the tooth pop-up first, never the whole Services Availed pop-up); Tab stays inside it. A chart change marks the pop-up dirty, the drawer is removed on `dgt:modalclose`, and a missing-teeth validation error opens it. `.proc-split` CSS removed.
- **Tooth chart is a side panel of the Services Availed pop-up (2026-10-26, later, adviser feedback):** supersedes the body-level slide-over drawer above. `logProcedureModal()` wraps the modal in `.proc-wrap` (`.proc-main` = the normal head / body / foot) plus `#t_panel` (`.t-panel`, absolute on the dialog's right edge). The standalone "Tooth Chart" card is gone: a compact teal **Edit Tooth Chart** button (count badge, reads "Hide Tooth Chart" while open) sits in the Services Performed header row. Opening (`isChartOpen` / `setChart()`, class `chart-open`) widens the dialog to `min(1440px, 100% - 32px)` and slides the 560px panel in (`records_chart_style.php`; below 1024px the panel covers the form instead). Panel: header "Tooth Chart" + patient name + Done (teal) + X; body = the board in a rounded bordered box; footer = 2-column legend + "Marked this visit" list (`CS.entries()`). Changes apply to the rows at once; Done / X / Escape close it and return focus to the button (Escape closes a tooth pop-up first). The inactive panel is `inert`. Footer buttons: Cancel is a plain text button, Proceed to Payment is teal (`DONE_BTN`).
- **Tooth chart handle on the pop-up's edge (2026-10-26, later still):** supersedes the "Edit Tooth Chart" button in the Services Performed row (removed; the row header is "Services Performed / Amount" again). A 40px teal strip (`#t_chartBtn.t-handle`: chevron, tooth icon, vertical "Tooth Chart" text, count badge) is the left edge of `#t_panel` and always shows on the dialog's right edge (the panel sits at `translateX(100% - 40px)`, `.proc-main` keeps `margin-right: 40px`). Clicking it slides the chart out and widens the dialog (`min(1480px, 100% - 32px)`), clicking it again slides it back; the chevron flips. Only the panel content (`#t_pBody`) is `inert` while closed. Done / X / Escape still close it; the row "Edit Tooth Chart" links still open it.
- **Tooth chart pull tab is small (2026-10-26, latest):** supersedes the full-height 40px strip. `#t_chartBtn.t-handle` is now a 28px x ~76px teal tab (chevron, tooth icon, count badge) sticking out of the dialog's right edge, vertically centred (`left: 100%`; tucked inside the edge below 640px). The panel sits in `.t-clip` (clips the slide, rounded right corners), and for this pop-up only the dialog and its `.modal-viewport` are `overflow: visible` so the tab isn't cut off. Open width is `min(1480px, 100% - 80px)` so the tab stays on screen. Click the tab to slide the chart out, click it again to slide it back.
- **Tooth chart pull tab: top + amber (2026-10-26):** the tab now sits near the top of the dialog's right edge (`top: 14px`, level with the title) and is amber (`#f59e0b`, hover `#d97706`, navy icons, navy count badge) so it stands out from the teal buttons and the dark backdrop.
- **Tooth chart pull tab: DGT Blue (2026-10-26):** supersedes the amber colour. The tab is `bg-dgt-blue-500` (hover `-600`) with white icons, a white ring and a white count badge (blue text), so it stays in the theme but differs from the teal buttons. Until it is first opened (`t-seen` class added by `setChart()`) it pulses a soft blue glow 3 times (`t-tab-pulse`, off under reduced motion).
- **Update (2026-10-26):** the tooth chart tab's glow (`t-tab-pulse` / `t-seen`) was removed at the user's request; the tab is plain DGT Blue with a white ring.
- **Tooth chart panel sizing (2026-10-26):** the × next to Done was removed (Done, the blue tab and Escape close it). The panel is a share of the widened dialog instead of 560px (`--tp-w` 52%, 60% from 1600px; dialog open width `min(1760px, 100% - 80px)`), so teeth grow on big screens (about 34px wide at 1366, 59px at 1920, 72px at 2560). Tooth size is also capped by the panel body's height (`.t-pbody` is a size container; `max-width: clamp(26px, (100cqh - 205px) / 2.8, 86px)`, a smaller formula when baby teeth show) so the chart fits with no vertical scrollbar, and the board is centred vertically. The tooth-type caption no longer ellipsizes in the panel ("Canine" instead of "C…"), and the legend goes 3 columns once the panel is 680px+ wide.
- **Update (2026-10-26):** the Services Availed footer buttons are back to their original colours (Cancel = `BTN_GHOST` outlined, Proceed to Payment = `BTN_OK` green). The panel's Done button stays teal.
- **Tooth pop-up: conditions can be deselected (2026-10-26):** in `window.toothDetailPopup()`, clicking the chosen condition again clears it back to Healthy / Normal (when not read-only or status-locked). In Services Availed, applying that takes the tooth off its Extraction / Resto / Root Canal row.
- **Update (2026-10-26): deselecting returns to the saved condition, not Healthy.** Supersedes the note above: clicking the chosen condition again sets it back to the tooth's condition as loaded (`baseStatus`, from `toothChartState().orig`), e.g. a Resto tooth marked Missing goes back to Resto. Clicking the saved condition itself does nothing; Healthy / Normal is still its own button.
- **Update (2026-10-26): Healthy is no longer auto-highlighted.** In `toothDetailPopup()` a `picked` flag decides which condition button looks chosen: a tooth with no condition opens with nothing chosen, Healthy / Normal lights up only when clicked, and deselecting (clicking the chosen one again) leaves nothing chosen for a healthy tooth (the saved condition, e.g. Resto, is shown again otherwise). Apply still saves the tooth's condition as shown (unchanged when nothing is chosen).
- **Tooth pop-up Apply + "changed" count (2026-10-26):** `toothDetailPopup()`'s Apply button is disabled (greyed, tooltip) until the condition, a tag or the note differs from how the tooth opened (`syncApply()`; a status-locked pop-up from a service row can always apply). `toothChartState().apply()` drops a tooth from `pending` when it is set back to exactly its loaded condition + tags + note, so reverting a change removes it from the tab's count, the "Marked this visit" list and the save.
- **Update (2026-10-26):** the "Open Edit Tooth Chart and set the treated tooth to … *" hint under Extraction / Resto / Root Canal rows was removed (the line is empty until teeth are picked, then shows "Teeth: … Edit on chart"). Proceed to Payment still refuses such a row with no teeth and opens the chart.
- **Update (2026-10-26):** the Services Performed "Amount" heading has `pr-[47px]` (row border + padding + the × slot + gap) so it lines up with each row's ₱ amount.
- **Tooth chart opened from a header pill (2026-10-26, adviser spec):** supersedes the pull tab on the dialog edge (removed, and the dialog clips normally again). `#t_chartBtn` is a light-teal pill in the Services Availed modal header, before the close X: "🦷 Tooth Chart" / "🦷 Close Tooth Chart" plus the count badge. Opening widens the dialog to `min(1760px, 100% - 32px)` and slides the panel out of its right inner edge (divider line). Panel header: "Tooth Chart: <patient>", the one-line "Click a tooth to record its condition…" hint and a teal Done (focus goes to Done on open, back to the pill on close). Centre: the board in a light rounded card. Bottom: a 2-column legend of colour-coded condition badges (`.t-lg-*` in records_chart_style.php, same colours as the tooth boxes, dark mode too) and "Marked this visit". The footer Cancel / Proceed to Payment keep their original colours (user request).
- **Update (2026-10-26): tooth chart pill restyled.** `#t_chartBtn` is now solid DGT Blue (`bg-dgt-blue-500`, hover `-600`, dgt-blue-700 `#0a457e` while open via `#t_chartBtn[aria-expanded="true"]` in records_chart_style.php) with white text, an inline SVG tooth icon instead of the 🦷 emoji, and a white count badge with blue text.
- **Update (2026-10-26):** the tooth chart legend's Healthy / Normal badge now has its own `t-lg-healthy` class (light grey fill, grey border, dark outline on the tooth icon) instead of plain white, which looked faint. Grey, not green, so it doesn't look like Root Canal.
- **Services Availed: no "Starts at" line, tooth-chart prompt (2026-10-26):** supersedes "Services Availed shows the starting price" (2026-10-17): the grey "Starts at ₱X per tooth" line under each service is gone (`fromHtml` / `FROM_CLS` removed). An Extraction / Resto / Root Canal row with no tooth marked yet shows an amber dashed button (tooth icon, "Mark the tooth on the Tooth Chart", "Set each treated tooth to Missing / Resto / Root Canal. The Qty follows the teeth you mark.", "Open Tooth Chart →"; class `svc-chartlink`, so it opens the panel). Once a tooth is marked it turns back into "Teeth: 36 · Edit on chart".
- **Update (2026-10-26): Starts at back, Qty free, chart never adds a row.** Supersedes the "no Starts at line" part of the note above and the "Qty is the number of teeth picked (locked)" rule (2026-10-18): the "Starts at ₱X per tooth" line is back; the Qty box on Extraction / Root Canal is typed by the dentist like any per-unit service (`syncTeethQty()` only shows the "Teeth:" summary; `api/treatments.php` no longer overwrites qty with the tooth count). Marking a tooth Resto / Root Canal / Missing on the chart only attaches it to a matching row already on the bill; it never adds a service row (`rowFor()` removed). The amber prompt now reads "Set each treated tooth to Missing / Resto / Root Canal on the chart." Fixed too: the `tooth-pop` class was on `noticePopup` instead of `toothDetailPopup`, so Escape in the tooth pop-up also closed the chart panel.
- **Visit Notes fill in from the tooth chart (2026-10-26):** in Services Availed, every tooth changed on the chart adds a line to Visit Notes (`syncVisitNotes()` / `toothNoteLine()` in `logProcedureModal()`, run from `draw()`), under a "Tooth chart:" heading after anything the dentist typed: "- Tooth 46 (lower right first molar): Missing / Empty Slot. Symptoms: Pain when biting. Findings: Deep cavity. Recommended: Needs extraction. Note: …" (tags grouped by `TOOTH_TAG_GROUPS`). The block is rewritten as teeth change while it is still exactly what was generated; once the dentist edits it, it is left as they wrote it. The box grows to fit (72 to 260px). Saved as the visit's Treatment notes like any typed text.
- **Update (2026-10-26): Visit Notes tooth lines, one item per line.** Each tooth is now "- Tooth 43 (lower right canine, eye tooth): Decayed / Caries." with Symptoms / Findings / Recommended / Note each on its own line below, and a blank line between teeth (nested brackets in the tooth name become a comma). The Visit Notes box grows to fit all its text with no inner scrollbar, both when lines are filled in and while the dentist types (`fitNotes()`, no height cap).
- **Update (2026-10-26):** a tooth with no condition picked no longer gets a Visit Notes line: deselecting its condition removes that tooth's whole entry (tags and note too), and the "Tooth chart:" heading goes once no tooth has a condition. The tags / note still save to the tooth record.
- **Update (2026-10-26):** in the tooth pop-up (`toothDetailPopup()`), deselecting the chosen condition now also clears every quick tag and the note, so on Apply the tooth goes back to exactly how it was loaded (dropped from the changed-teeth count, "Marked this visit" and Visit Notes). Supersedes "The tags / note still save to the tooth record" in the note above.
- **Already missing vs extracted this visit (2026-10-26):** in Services Availed, setting a tooth to Missing (when it wasn't Missing before) shows a required "Was this tooth pulled today?" choice in the tooth pop-up (`toothDetailPopup({ askExtract: true })`, passed by `CS.open(t, ro, cb, extra)`; Apply stays disabled until picked): **Extracted this visit (Bunot)** puts the tooth on the Extraction row; **Already missing before this visit** only records it on the chart, never on the bill. The choice is kept as `extracted` on the pending entry (remembered on reopen; teeth on the Extraction row are always `extracted: true`), and Visit Notes reads "Missing / Empty Slot (extracted this visit)" or "(already missing before this visit)". A tooth already Missing on the saved chart is never put on the Extraction row. The Patient Records chart editor doesn't ask.
- **Update (2026-10-26): the "done today?" question covers Resto and Root Canal too.** Setting a tooth to Missing, Restored / Filled / Pasta or Root Canal Treated (when it wasn't that before) asks "Was this tooth pulled today?" / "Was this filling done today?" / "Was this root canal done today?" (`EX_Q` in `toothDetailPopup()`), for when the dentist records what they find while checking before the procedure. Done this visit = on that service's row (if it's on the bill; otherwise a toast says to add the service); already there before = chart only, not billed. Visit Notes adds "(done this visit)" / "(already there before this visit)". The choice is still stored as `extracted` on the entry.
- **For Extraction is the bunot condition; Missing is record-only (2026-10-26, user choice).** Supersedes the Missing parts of the two notes above. In Services Availed the Extraction service is linked to **For Extraction** (`toothEffect()` returns `extract`): setting a tooth to For Extraction always asks "Was this tooth pulled today?" (even if it was already planned for extraction at an earlier visit): **Pulled this visit (Bunot)** puts it on the Extraction row, and on Proceed to Payment it is saved as **Missing** (the entries sent to `save_teeth` turn `extract` + `extracted: true` into `missing`; `api/treatments.php` already marks Extraction teeth Missing); **Not yet, extract later** keeps it For Extraction on the chart, not billed. **Missing / Empty Slot** no longer asks anything and is never billed. Visit Notes: "For Extraction (pulled this visit)" / "(to be pulled at a later visit)". The amber row prompt reads "Set each treated tooth to For Extraction on the chart." Resto / Root Canal unchanged.
- **Update (2026-10-26): "done this visit" adds the service and sets the Qty.** Supersedes "it never adds a service row" above: choosing Pulled / Filled / Root canal done this visit in the tooth pop-up puts the tooth on that service's row, adding the Extraction / Resto / Root Canal row when it isn't on the bill yet (`rowFor()` is back; toast "Resto (Pasta) added to Services Performed for tooth 26. Enter its amount."). "Already … before this visit" / "Not yet" never add or bill anything. The row's Qty follows the number of teeth on it whenever that number changes (`row.dataset.tc` in `syncTeethQty()`), for every linked service (Resto too); the dentist can still type over it, and the next tooth change resets it to the count.
- **Services Availed row layout (2026-10-26):** service rows are top-aligned (`items-start`, `gap-x-3`, `px-3 py-2.5`) so the Qty box, Amount box, line total (`leading-8`) and × line up with the service dropdown (or the booked service's name, `pt-1.5`) instead of centring against the dropdown + "Starts at" pair; rows are 12px apart (`#t_rows` `gap-3`).
- **New condition "Extracted / Bunot" (EXT), separate from Missing (2026-10-26):** `extracted` added to `TOOTH_STATES` (`api/_bootstrap.php`) and `window.TOOTH_STATUS` (app.js), drawn as a dashed fuchsia outline with an arrow lifting out of the socket (`.tq`, `.t-extracted`, `.t-lg-extracted` in tailwind.php / records_chart_style.php), with its own legend entry "Extracted (EXT): pulled at this clinic"; Missing's legend is now "Missing (X): no tooth when first seen". Supersedes "saved as Missing" in the For Extraction note above: a tooth pulled this visit (For Extraction + Pulled, or Extracted + Pulled) goes on the Extraction row and is saved as **Extracted**, and `toothChartEffect()` now marks Extraction teeth `extracted`. Picking Extracted in Services Availed asks "Was this tooth pulled today?" (Pulled this visit / Pulled at an earlier visit, the latter record-only). Older extractions saved before this stay Missing. Panel hint: "Work done this visit (Resto, Root Canal, Extraction) is added to the bill."
- **Past extractions converted (2026-10-26):** a one-off script (session scratchpad `extracted_backfill.php`, run once, deleted from the server) turned teeth that were pulled at the clinic before the Extracted condition existed from `missing` into `extracted`. Only teeth proven by a completed visit with an Extraction service count (that visit's `tooth_records` rows, or its "Extraction …: teeth 16, 26" notes line), and the chart only changed where it still said missing. Live: Rex Carbonel teeth 17, 28, 44 (chart + 2 tooth_records rows). Local: nothing to convert. Other Missing teeth stay Missing.
- **Update (2026-10-26):** Extracted / Bunot uses the same orange as For Extraction (no purple, user request); it differs by its dashed outline, the arrow lifted out of the socket and the EXT tag.
- **Extracted condition removed again (2026-10-26, user request):** supersedes the "Extracted / Bunot (EXT)" notes above. There is no `extracted` state any more (`TOOTH_STATES` / `TOOTH_STATUS`, CSS removed). A tooth pulled at the clinic keeps the **For Extraction (FX)** mark (legend "For Extraction (FX): to be removed / pulled at this clinic"), which keeps it apart from **Missing (X): no tooth when first seen**; `toothChartEffect()` marks Extraction teeth `extract`, and Pulled this visit is saved as `extract` (no conversion). The For Extraction question and billing are unchanged. Data: Rex's teeth 17, 28, 44 went extracted → extract on live (chart + 2 tooth_records rows, one-off `extracted_to_fx.php`, deleted from the server); local had none.
- **Phone layout fixes: Services Availed, tooth chart, My Availability (2026-10-29).** Checked at 390px in headless Chrome (touch).
  - **Services Availed:** below 640px the pop-up is a flex column (`records_chart_style.php` `@media (max-width: 639px)`: dialog `max-height: calc(100dvh - 24px)`, and `.modal-viewport` / `.modal-track` / `.modal-pane` / `.proc-wrap` / `.proc-main` pass the height down) so the body takes only the room left and the Cancel / Proceed footer is never cut off; the title is 16px. Service rows put the name on its own line, with Qty / Amount / total / × on the next (`max-sm:basis-full`, the Amount box flexes); the amber "Mark the tooth" box puts "Open Tooth Chart →" under its text; the Tooth Chart pill is smaller (`max-sm:px-2.5 max-sm:text-[12px]`).
  - **Tooth chart:** the board starts at the top (no centring gap); `.t-panel .tooth-quad-row .tooth-jaw { flex-basis: auto }` (the panel's 34px jaw width had turned into an empty strip under UPPER / LOWER once the rows stack); legend labels wrap instead of being cut off (`leading-tight sm:truncate`).
  - **My Availability:** cells are centred with short labels below 640px ("6 slots" / "Off" / "Absent" / "Closed", a small amber dot for Custom; the full time range shows from `sm:`), the month nav fills the row and the legend is tighter.
